So he said, "Ya Ubayy, I called you, and you didn't come." So he submitted, "Ya Rasulullah, I was praying." So he said, "Who taught you this prayer?" This prayer, who taught it to you? So he was a bit surprised. He said, "Is prayer greater or my... my command and order greater? My calling is greater?" For this reason, a verse came in the Quran that... what is the verse? That if Allah and His Messenger call you, then you should answer the Messenger. A very strange verse is there. Meaning, it's not that "you should answer Allah and His Messenger," but "Allah and the Messenger call you, so you should answer the Messenger." So there... so this was said.

...`wa ta'azzaz al-maqam al-sani bi-ghayrihi fi lubnani wa-tashahad fi alf surah li-al-anam` (And the second station was magnified by other than it in its core, and he was witnessed in a thousand forms by mankind). And this Imam is such that he can manifest in a thousand forms. `wa fi dhalika wujuh wa asbab wa li-hi al-hujjat al-ujab` (And in that are aspects and reasons, and for him is the wondrous proof). And for his manifestation, there are various aspects and reasons, and his affairs are very strange and wondrous. `wa amma qawluka kayfa kana qabla wujud al-samawat wa al-ardina` (As for your saying, how was it before the existence of the heavens and the earths)... so your question which was that... how... before the heavens and the earth, what was there? So `kanat min al-dhawat al-nadimah` (it was from the penitent essences). So they are the `dhawat al-nadimah` (penitent essences)... `nadin dhawat` (penitent essences), from which this came into existence. `allati usila minha ma hasula wa tarattaba khalq al-aflak allatihiya aqsam al-nadimin` (from which was derived what was obtained, and the creation of the celestial spheres was arranged, which are the categories of the penitent ones). So, the categories of the `nadimin` which were divided into fifteen types, in them, from the first type, the celestial spheres (`aflak`) were made, and this... the heavens and the earth were made. Yes. `wa amma qawluka min ayyi fudulatin kana wujud al-awwal al-jussa' fi safil al-ard` (As for your saying, from what remnants was the existence of the first particle in the low part of the earth)... and your question was that the `awwal jussa'`... from which remnants... the `awwal jussa' ibda'iyyah`'s... its existence in the earth... happened? That the earth is wide and long... `fa-ta'lam hadaka Allah li al-sawab wa bawwa'aka fi jami' al-asbab` (So know, may Allah guide you to what is right and settle you in all the means)... so know, may Allah guide you... towards correctness... and may your station be established in all the `asbab` (means), meaning the `hudud` (ranks). `innama kana wujud al-fudulat al-sharifah illa ba'd dhuhur al-jussa' al-ibda'iyyah` (The existence of the noble remnants was only after the appearance of the originated particle). And the `fudulat sharifah` (noble remnants), their existence happened after the appearance of the `jussa' ibda'iyyah`. Yes. `illa` (only)... `illa ba'd dhuhur al-jussa' al-ibda'iyyah al-dhalifah` (only after the appearance of the weak originated particle). `wa innama yuqal fudlah illa al-baqiyyah min al-jism ba'da sudur al-ruh wa al-nafs` (And it is only called a remnant, that which remains of the body after the emanation of the spirit and the soul). And `fudlah` (remnant) is... what is it? From the body, the remnant that remains... is after the departure of the `ruh` (spirit) and `nafs` (soul). Yes. `bi al-wadih min al-khitab` (with the clarity of the discourse). `wa amma wujud al-awwal al-jussa' al-ibda'iyyah allati khalafat al-nafs al-kulliyyah` (As for the existence of the first originated particle which succeeded the Universal Soul)... and the `jussa' ibda'iyyah` is... which has taken vicegerency of the `Nafs al-Kulliyyah`. The `Nafs al-Kulliyyah` means the `‘Ashir` (Tenth Intellect). Meaning, leaving the Tenth, he... yes. So the `jussa' ibda'iyyah` takes vicegerency of the `‘Ashir`. Meaning, a partial vicegerency of the `‘Ashir`. He takes it. The vicegerency of the `kulli` belongs to the `akhir mawr`, the last person of the `mawr`. Yes. `fa-kana min asfa aqsam al-nadimin` (So it was from the purest of the categories of the penitent ones). So that... the `nadimin` (penitent ones) who... among them, the purest categories, from them its... the `jussa' ibda'iyyah`'s existence happens. `allati kana tartibuha 'ala khamsata 'ashara darajatan fi al-ma'alim` (whose arrangement was upon fifteen degrees in the realms). And these... the `nadimin` have fifteen categories. `wa asfa man habata wa ashrafa man hafaw wa ta'bata` (And the purest of those who descended and the noblest of those who stumbled and were trampled). And they... are more pure... those who fell... and are nobler... those who slipped and were trampled, they are the most noble among them. I mean, the `nadimin` also made a mistake, right? That is, the `‘Ashir` first gave the command that I have made a mistake, so you also accept your mistake and repent. So everyone refused. First of all, everyone refused. That `la nasma'u wa la nuti'` (we do not hear and we do not obey). Then, a second time, the command came to him to guide them again. So he guided them and said that if you do not obey, then I will... remove you from here, from this world. So at that time... at that time what happened? That... the `musirrin` (insistent ones) remained on their stubbornness. So therefore, he removed the `musirrin` from there. The `mutahayyirin` (bewildered ones), and those who were in a state of confusion about what they should do, he removed them as well. And while falling, the `nadimin` (penitent ones) repented. While falling. They were falling, all of them. But in that too, what happened? Fifteen categories were formed. And we believers are in the fifteenth category. Of the fifteenth category, it is also said that we are in the tenth division. Each category has ten divisions. So the believers of the `sitr` are in the tenth division. The last... the last of the last. From this... from this, one can estimate.

Sir, this... what is the reason for the believers' falling? I mean, to fall somewhere means one goes down, so to what extent can one go? We, in the prayer we recite on the 3rd, in that... three... we do its... `darkat` (levels of hell) that we mention... that we seek refuge from them... up to `atiran`... in `du'a yazul qadr`... `damad`... the garment of `damad`... up to there, the garment of `damad` is the last... last thing. The seven... the `darkat` that are in it, the last `darkat`. From which lava comes out. Yes.

Thank you. Sir, the momineen who come later, who enter... No, no... are they not in this category? So the one who comes later... No... The voice is coming... He is not in the category. But here, guidance is received from the Imam or from his `hudud`... guidance is received. So the lower forms ascend. The lower forms... the forms of the disbelievers and polytheists ascend and move forward. Just as... just as `‘uyafa’` are, as Sayyidna Muhammad bin Tahir has mentioned in `Anwar al-Latifah`, that there are `‘uyafa’`, who are such noble people who love the Ahl al-Bayt... like that... and they are non-Muslims, or Muslims... the Muslims who love the Ahl al-Bayt and abide by the shari'ah, the Muslims... and are mindful of their halal and haram... so they... they love the Ahl al-Bayt, so they are `‘uyafa’`, and they gradually ascend. They enter the fold of Iman. Yes. What I want to say is that, whether one is a disbeliever or a non-Muslim, when they come and accept Islam, then those 15 categories, they will not be in them. No, they are not. Sir, Janab Salman al-Farsi was first that... so he was not in that category? I mean, a question arises. No, his reality is different. He was already... he was already... I am just saying... his... his reality is... he was already... he was from the `hudud`. No, that's correct, but outwardly he was of another religion, then he came here. So there is a history, a biography (`sirah`) is formed. Yes. And before that, he was. Yes. And... and... what was I going to say... the disbelievers... the disbelievers do not ascend until they accept Islam. The disbelievers will not enter directly. Because `shirkun zulmun 'azim` (`shirk` [polytheism] is a great wrong). `Shirk` is a great wrong. And Allah Ta'ala forgives all sins, except `shirk`. He does not forgive `shirk`. This is in the Quran as well. That `shirk`... is not worthy of forgiveness. Otherwise, what is there? In the Quran it is said that `Innallaha yaghfiru al-dhunuba jami'an` (Indeed, Allah forgives all sins). Allah Ta'ala forgives all sins. But He does not forgive `shirk`. Because `shirk` is a great wrong. Allah knows when they will turn back. Absolutely. Because for this very reason it is said, right? That in Surah Yasin, that `dhalika taqdir al-'aziz al-'alim` (That is the determination of the Exalted in Might, the Knowing). The one that... the determination... `al-'aziz al-'alim`, by `al-'aziz al-'alim` is meant the `‘Ashir` (Tenth Intellect). Yes. Because the `‘Ashir` knows how many... in this troop, how many `mawrs` (cycles) will it take to be purified. And in every `mawr`, how many `dawrs` (periods) will there be. And in every `dawr`, how many Imams will come. He has the entire map in his mind. So, his... his station is great and vast. And who was he? He was our companion. At one time, he was our companion. Meaning, from what a high station we have fallen, from this one can estimate. That... the `‘Ashir`... the `‘Ashir` was a person within us, right? He was a soul, an intellect. But we... we, by not obeying, due to the `wahm al-musawat` (illusion of equality)... `wahm al-musawat` is such a great curse. `Wahm al-musawat`. Because its first principle is what? That no person... can sit at one station. Two people cannot sit at one station. If there are two Imams also, like Hasan and Husayn. `Imama haqqin qama aw qa'ada` (Two true Imams, whether they stand up or sit down). Both are Imams. But their ranks are different. Similarly, if there are two `da'is` also, like Sayyidna Gulam Husayn Sahib and Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad, at one time there were two `da'is`. Or `ma'dhuns` also, if there are two `ma'dhuns`, their ranks will be different. Yes. So... in one rank, no... no person can sit. Every person's rank is different. So, he did not accept this reality. The `‘Ashir`, who was the third intellect, who later became the `‘Ashir`, he did not accept it, which is why the `wahm al-musawat` was established, that I... and the `Mumba'ith al-Awwal` (First Emanation) are of the same rank. Whereas the gaze of the `Mumba'ith al-Awwal` was on the `surah 'ilmiyyah` (intellectual form) of the First Intellect. And his... his gaze was on the `surah 'amaliyyah` (practical form) of the First Intellect. Yes. So, due to this, a difference was created. `Nazar-i ashraf` (nobler gaze) and `nazar-i adwan` (lower gaze). `Nazar-i ashraf` and `nazar-i adwan`'s difference was created.

O Allah, send blessings and peace upon the Fatiha to the soul of Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Hibatullah, may his soul be sanctified and his grave be illuminated, and may its blessings and support be multiplied upon us. Accept from us, O Allah, the benefit and the benefiting, for the sake of Muhammad and the family of Muhammad, peace and blessings be upon them. Al-Fatiha. Bismillah...

Sir, the believers, what is the reason for their falling? I mean, to fall somewhere means one goes down, so to what extent can one go? We, in the prayer we recite on the 3rd, in that... three... we do its... `darkat` (levels of hell) that we mention... that we seek refuge from them... up to `atiran`... in `du'a yazul qadr`... `damad`... the garment of `damad`... up to there, the garment of `damad` is the last... last thing. The seven... the `darkat` that are in it, the last `darkat`. From which lava comes out. Yes. Thank you. Alhamdulillah.

<!-- lecture 4 -->

And from Imam Ahmad bin Yusuf on the *falak al-mustaqeem* (the straight sphere), the *muheet* (the encompassing one), and `wa anna noor al-shamsi min noor al-'azeem` (and that the light of the sun is from the magnificent light). This is the second question, that the questioner is saying that I benefited (*istifadah*) from my *sayyid* (master), from my *madadgaar* (helper), and from my Imam, meaning the one on whom I rely, Ahmad bin Yusuf. In that era, he must have been one of the *hudood* (ranks of the religious hierarchy). From him... that the *falak-e-mustaqeem* (the straight sphere), meaning *falak-e-muheet* (the encompassing sphere), which is the highest sphere, which is *ghayr-mar'i* (invisible). Yes, *falak-e-mustaqeem*. And it is after this *falak-e-mustaqeem* that the *'alam-e-ruhani* (spiritual world) is to begin. So, `wa anna noor al-shamsi min noor al-'azeem` (and that the light of the sun is from the magnificent light), that the light of the sun is from its light. Meaning, from the light of *falak-e-mustaqeem* or *falak-e-muheet*. And... from the Imam... `wa ma ya'oodu amr al-falak al-mustaqeem` (and what returns of the affair of the straight sphere). And the Imam... and what... what will be the state of *falak-e-mustaqeem*... `'inda irtiqa' al-rubaataat` (at the time of the ascension of the *rubaataat*), that when the *rubaataat* ascend, meaning... this... at the end of the *kawr* (cycle), when all things... become... the skies will fall. So, at that time, what will be the state of *falak-e-mustaqeem*? `Wa ayna yaseeru` (And where will it go?), and where will it go? `Wa ayna su'ooduhu` (And where will its ascent be?), and what will its ascent be like? `Wa ma alladhi yuwasilu ilaihi al-ayimmah minhu` (And what is it that the Imams attain from it?). And what will the *ayimmah* (Imams) attain from it? From *falak-e-mustaqeem*. `Faqad qeela innahu yas'adu` (It has been said that it will ascend...). And it is also said that `innahu yas'adu min mawdi'ihi` (it will ascend from its own place). That it, *falak-e-mustaqeem* or *falak-e-muheet*, will ascend from its own place. From right where it is. Because, what is the principle? Whoever has to ascend, has to progress, he has to come to the earth. And then he has to progress. So... this... the state of *falak-e-mustaqeem* is different, in that it will ascend from right where it is. But this is a question. Reliance is not on a question. The answer that we will give, reliance will be on that. `Wa munni 'alayya` (And bestow upon me...). And... and do this favor upon me... `wa jud 'ala mithli man yurhamu` (and be generous to one like me who is shown mercy). And... bestow... on one like me upon whom mercy is shown. `Warham man laisa lahu rahimun siwak` (And have mercy on one who has no merciful one besides You). And have mercy because there is no one to have mercy on him besides You. `Wa la malan illa iyyaka` (And no refuge but You). And no one will treasure him or benefit him besides You.

`Al-jawab 'ala dhalik al-su'al` (The answer to that question). The answer to his question... That is... now this is the *da'i* (summoner), Sayyidna Hibatullah, is saying that... the answer to the question... The answer is this, to his question...

[Side conversation: What was the question, actually? I didn't understand.]

Yes, it was about *falak-e-mustaqeem*, that... that the concept was that it would depart from its place. And... yes... and this... this was the question. And... but it was also that what would the *ayimmah* attain from it? This... and... the light of the sun is the light from *falak-e-mustaqeem*. This was also a question of his. So, is his concept correct? He is saying.

So, `aqoolu bi 'awnillah al-kabeer al-muta'al` (I say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Most High). I say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Most High, `wa minnati wali al-'asr wa al-zaman` (and by the grace of the Master of the Age and Time), and from the bestowal of... the Master of the Age and Time, `alayhi salawat al-raheem al-rahman` (upon him be the blessings of the Merciful, the Compassionate). Upon him be the blessings of the Merciful and the Compassionate, `wa ta'eed-e mawlaya al-a'lam` (and by the support of my most learned master). And by the support of Mawlaya al-A'lam. By *a'lam* is meant Mawlana Hibatullah. That is his title. `Wa sirrihi al-baahir al-mu'azzam` (and by his manifest and magnificent secret). And by your luminous and lofty secret, `anna al-falak al-mustaqeem` (that the straight sphere...), that *falak-e-mustaqeem* is `wa anna noor al-shams min noorihi al-'azeem` (and that the light of the sun is from its magnificent light). That the light of the sun is from its magnificent light. From the magnificent light of *falak-e-mustaqeem*, the light of the sun is there. `Fa dhalika ka dhalika` (So that is as it is). That the matter you have thought of is just so. `Wa la budda an yuwasiluha shay'un min anwar al-qalb fi jamee' al-masalik` (And it is necessary that something of the lights of the heart reach it in all the paths). And it is necessary that it, meaning *falak-e-mustaqeem*... no, not *falak-e-mustaqeem*, but rather, the light of the sun will receive lights from the lights of the heart. Meaning this... the lights that the light of the sun will receive, they are from the lights of the heart. By heart is meant... the Baytullah (House of Allah) that is opposite the... `taamur` (heavenly counterpart)... the Baytullah that is there, from there lights will be received by this... the sun. By the sun. Yes. That is why the pronoun used here is feminine, `yuwasiluha`, it will receive them. `Fi jamee' al-masalik` (In all the paths), at every moment. `Wa qawluka ma yakoonu haal falak al-mustaqeem 'inda irtiqa' al-rubaataat` (And your saying, what will be the state of the straight sphere at the time of the ascension of the *rubaataat*). At the time of the ascension of the *rubaataat*, meaning the final matter that will happen, at the end of the *kawr*, what will be the condition of *falak-e-mustaqeem*? `Wa yas'adu min mawdi'ihi` (And it will ascend from its place). Your concept is correct that it will ascend from its place.

[Side conversation: It will go into the tenth *barzakh* (intermediate realm), right?]

Yes.

[Side conversation: The tenth *'arsh* (throne) that is made, it will go into that.]

Yes, he hasn't elaborated on it. Yes. And it will ascend from there. `Wa yakoonu 'aqlan qa'iman bil-fi'l` (And it will become an intellect existing in actuality). And it will become an intellect, such that it is `qa'iman bil-fi'l` (existing in actuality). Until now, it was `qa'iman bil-quwwah` (existing in potentiality). Meaning, it was helping others, it was helping the lights of the sun, this... the light of the sun was connected to it, all these things were there. And later we will mention what help the *ayimmah* also received from it. This... meaning, it is happening `bil-quwwah` (in potentiality). The state of `bil-fi'l` (in actuality) will be that it will ascend as an intellect. `Fi jamee' al-haalat wa la budda... wa yakoonu lahu ruhun min noorin... wa maqamuhu ruhun... mashkoorah lahu...` (In all states and it is necessary... and it will have for it a spirit of light... and its station will be a spirit... mentioned for it...). And its station will be of a spirit, mentioned... for which... its affairs, their explanation is long. `Ma yuheetu biha illa al-maqaamaat al-nooraniyyah` (None can encompass it except the luminous stations). And its state cannot be encompassed except by the luminous stations, meaning the words of the *ayimmah*. `Aw man kaana min da'ina... al-mutasawwir bi suwar al-imaniyyah... al-muhawwin lil-mushkilat` (Or one who was from our *da'i*s... who are envisioned in the forms of faith... who make the difficult things easy). Or only our *da'i*, who is envisioned in the forms of faith, who are the ones who make difficult things easy. Meaning religious difficulties. `Wal-'ati bil-khawariq al-'adat` (And the one who comes with extraordinary feats). And who are the possessors of great, extraordinary states. `Wa laqad kashafa lana ma la yakshifuhu` (And indeed he has unveiled for us what he does not unveil). And he has unveiled for us... `wa tafaddala 'alayna ma la yuhaat` (and he has bestowed upon us what cannot be encompassed). And he has bestowed upon us that which cannot be encompassed. `Wa la yukayyaf` (and cannot be described). And its state cannot be described. `Jazahumullahu 'anna ma jaza ahl al-du'at` (May Allah reward them on our behalf with what he has rewarded the people of the *du'at*). The reward that the *du'at* (summoners) receive from Allah, may he also receive such a reward. Meaning, Sayyidna Hibatullah. `Wa 'ammalana min sirrihi wa barakatihi` (And benefit us from his secret and his blessings). And from his secret and his blessings, may we receive benefit. `Innahu waliyu dhalika` (Indeed, He is the master of that). And He is capable of that matter, and He is the master. `Ala ma hunalika` (Over what is there). `Wa amma rooh al-imam al-noorani` (And as for the spirit of the luminous Imam). And the spirit of the luminous Imam's soul is... `wa shabhuhu al-qudsani... fa'lam anna dhalika min asrarillah allati la tusattaru bil-aqlaam` (and his sacred likeness... so know that that is from the secrets of Allah which cannot be written with pens). So know, you all, this matter, that it is from the secrets of the Lord, which... these are such things that cannot be written with pens. `Wa dhalika anna al-maqam 'alayhi al-salat wa al-salam mata haanat nuqlatuhu ila dar al-na'eem al-muqeem, tahallala fil-qalb` (And that is because the station, upon him be prayers and peace, whenever its time of transfer comes to the abode of bliss, the ever-lasting, it dissolves into the heart). That is, while the Imam Alayhis Salam... when the time of his passing comes, to the abode of bliss, the ever-lasting... so when the time of passing comes, so his... it dissolves... in the heart, meaning in the Baytullah. His soul... he... dissolves... from there his... ascent takes place.

[Side question: When they are buried, does it also happen to the *mu'min* (believer)?]

[Response:] Yes. Through the Baitullah, the *mu'min*'s also happens. No, that... that will ascend through the Baitullah, his *fuzala'* (excellent parts) will ascend. His *fuzala'*... the *surat* (form) of the *mu'min*... his *surat* goes to the *hudood-e-'uliyyah* (higher ranks). And the *riyat* (subtle essences) go into... the *aflak* (spheres). They go into the spheres. Yes, in the sun and Jupiter and so on. And his *fuzala'* that there are, they ascend, and while ascending, slowly, slowly they ascend, praying, and then... that... the *mihrab* (prayer niche) where he... where... he used to pray in the mosque, on that station... his... on his station they come, where he had made his station in the mosque for praying. To the extent that while ascending, they come to the *mihrab*. His *fuzala'*. And from there, then they come to the center, which is the center of his *jazirah* (island). So, for us, the center of the two *jazirahs* is said to be the tomb of Sayyidna Sulaiman. From there, his ascent happens. To the extent that there are 28 stations to go to the Baytullah. He travels through 28 stations. And here... to the extent that he enters the Baytullah. And from there too, there are four pillars, from there, while ascending, he goes to the pillars, and then finally he comes to the *rukn-e-aswad* (Black Stone corner), and from there his... ascent happens. And those *fuzala'*, when they ascend, they are called `zu'ubat`. In the state of ascending, they are called `zu'ubat`. And then they become `zakha'ir` (treasures).

[Side question: The four pillars you mentioned...]

Yes, the *asatir*.

[Side question: The four pillars you mentioned...]

Yes, they are the *atqiyah* (pious ones).

[Another side question.]

Yes, they are in their physical form.

And when the time of his passing comes, he finds dissolution in the *qalb* (heart). `Thumma sara mawaddan wa qiwaman li quloob al-ulama min al-hudood` (Then he becomes substance and strength for the hearts of the learned ones from the *hudood*). And after dissolving, what do they do? That they become substance and strength for the hearts of the learned ones who are the *hudood*... the great *hudood* of the *da'wah* (mission), for them they become substance. Meaning substance means that they... the *hudood* of the *da'wah*, the great ones, the strength that they have, the strength of speech, the strength of benefiting others, they receive help in that. Yes. Through them. Yes. Just as for Janab Yusuf there is, `lawla an ra'a burhana rabbihi` (had he not seen the proof of his Lord). If he had not seen the proof of the Lord, he would have erred. With Zuleikha, the matter that happened. So, what is the proof of the Lord? It is this `mawadi` (substance) that is obtained from the Imam. Yes. `Alladheena bi hidayatihim 'urifat ta'at al-ma'bood` (Those through whose guidance the worship of the Worshipped One is known). Such that through his... through their guidance, the worship of the Worshipped One can be performed. `Fa idha haanat nuqlatuhum` (So when their time of transfer comes). And when the passing of these *hudood* occurs, then they... `ila 'alam al-anwar... ittasala dhalika al-maddah bil-qalb al-saafi min al-akdar` (to the world of lights... that substance connects with the pure heart, free of impurities). To the world of lights, when their passing occurs, `yarji'u ila aslihi` (it returns to its origin). So that substance with which they were given strength, strength was given to their hearts, from that substance which dissolved in the *qalb*, of the Imam, it was received from that. So that is returned. It returns to its origin. At the time of passing. These were trusts they had, that in their decisions, in their benefiting, and in the matters of their lives, they should not make mistakes. The *hudood* should not make mistakes, and they should remain safe from evils and sins. So, they become masters of all those states. But they receive it temporarily. Not for always. But at the time of their passing, that connects, it is returned to the... to the *qalb*... to the origin. To the pure one, free of impurities. `Wa yakoonu min ma'ooneen nafsu imam al-sharqi wal-gharbi ma madda lahu min al-qalb` (And from those helpers, the soul of the Imam of the East and the West is that which is supplied to him from the heart). And from that, that... the soul of the Imam is formed. This... these things that... the Imam... the dissolution that happened in the Baytullah, so from that, the next Imam's soul is formed, who is the Imam of the East and the West. `Ma madda lahu min al-qalb` (What is supplied to him from the heart). That which is obtained for him from the heart. `Wa min al-ma' alladhi hawlahu` (And from the water that is around it). And from the water which is around it, from that it is obtained. `Wa shay'in min al-bata'ih allati talyihi` (And a thing from the marshes that are adjacent to it). All these things are obtained for that Imam whose soul's beginning is taking place. `Wa mimma zukhira lahu mimma inhallah min awwal al-fitrah... ba'da awwal al-fatrah, ba'da tamam al-kawr al-mashhoor` (And from what was stored for him of what dissolved from the beginning of creation... after the first interval, after the completion of the well-known *kawr*)... And the thing that has been stored for him, that is, which is dissolving from the first creation, from the first interval, after the first interval, after the completion of the *kawr* which is well-known. That from the first interval, meaning from when... from when the era was created, from there, whatever treasures are coming, from that the Imam's soul has additions. Yes. `Yajdharu minhu fa'idah... 'an idrees al-shurafa'... al-muwasilah min da'ihi... mishkat al-noor` (A benefit stems from it... for the noble ones... which is connected from his *da'i*... the niche of light). So from it arises a benefit... for the noble ones... so that... from it a benefit is connected from his *da'i*, who is the niche of light. `Wa qad awdaha hadha al-ma'na... al-da'i... al-ajall al-'aleem hibatullah bin sayyidna ibrahim fi risalatihi al-mawsumah bi saqilat al-quloob min al-fikr, wa al-munawwirah lil-basa'ir, wa al-musfiyah lil-suwar` (And this meaning has been clarified... by the *da'i*... the most glorious, the most learned, Hibatullah bin Sayyidna Ibrahim in his treatise named `Saqilat al-Quloob min al-Fikr` [The Polisher of Hearts from Thought], and `al-Munawwirah lil-Basa'ir` [The Illuminator of Insights], and `al-Musfiyah lil-Suwar` [The Purifier of Forms]). That... this has been clarified by Sayyidna al-Da'i al-Ajall al-'Aleem Hibatullah bin Sayyidna Ibrahim in his treatise whose name is `Saqilat al-Quloob` and which is... and thought... and which illuminates insights and purifies forms. `Fi jawab su'al al-thani 'ashar` (In the answer to the twelfth question). The one you, meaning Sayyidna Hibatullah, in his that book, in the answer to the twelfth question, has written. `Bi qawlin 'aleem al-ma'ani wa al-gharad` (With a speech of profound meanings and purpose). That which has been authored with great and grand meanings. `Wa ma fihi abyan al-bayan` (And what is in it is the clearest of explanations). In which there is the clearest explanation, `wa awdah al-burhan` (and the most evident proof). And there is a clear proof. `Wa mazkuruhu fi hadhihi al-mas'alah wajhayn fil-haqeeqah` (And its mention in this matter has two aspects in reality). And now I will mention this matter that you have said in two ways. Meaning the state of the Imam's soul, I will explain it to you. This Sayyidna Ali is saying. `Fi haqeeqah... fi wajhayn` (In reality... in two aspects). I will explain it to you in two ways. `Fi muqabalat al-shahadah` (In comparison to the *shahadah*). In comparison to the *shahadah*. The word of the *shahadah*. `Wa huwa anna al-asrar... allati la yattali'u 'alayha illa al-ahad min 'ibadihi` (And that is that the secrets... upon which none are informed except the unique ones from His servants). That it is from the secrets, which no one can know except those who have reached the rank of `ahad` (unique), they can know it. From among the servants. `Wa dhalika anna fil-maqam 'alayhi al-salam... ma yuqabilu qutbayn al-shahadah fil-hukm` (And that is that in the station, upon him be peace... there is what corresponds to the two poles of the *shahadah* in its ruling). If we compare the Imam Alayhis Salam with the word of the *shahadah*, then `li qawli asdaq al-qa'ileen... wa kullu shay'in ahsaynahu fi imamim mubeen` (according to the saying of the most truthful of speakers... "And We have enumerated all things in a clear Imam" [Qur'an 36:12]). As he says... the most truthful of speakers, that `kullu shay'in ahsaynahu fi imamim mubeen`. And we have enumerated every thing in a clear Imam. In Surah Yasin, this verse is there. `Wa kullu shay'in ahsaynahu fi imamim mubeen`. That we have enumerated everything in a clear Imam. In this, there are two things: one is that it has been said `kullu shay'in` (every thing), so by `shay'in` what is meant? That this fan, light, and... carpet is not being pointed to. Because that is also a `shay'` (thing), no? In `shay'` these also come. But there, by `shay'` is meant the form of the *mu'min* (believer). Where... where the word `shay'` has been used, by Allah Ta'ala, it is the *mu'min* and the form of the noble *mu'min*. For example, `kullu shay'in ahsaynahu fi imamim mubeen` has come, or that `innallaha 'ala kulli shay'in qadeer` (Indeed, Allah has power over all things), `wa innallaha bi kulli shay'in muheet` (and indeed, Allah encompasses all things), or `innallaha bi kulli shay'in 'aleem` (indeed, Allah is knowledgeable of all things). These states have come. So that `shay'`, by `shay'` is meant the *mu'min* or the form of the *mu'min*. And here what is it? It was `ahsayna` (We have enumerated). So in `ahsayna`, this happens, that there, `jama'na` (We have gathered) was not said. That we have gathered. In gathering, what happens? Things get jumbled up. In `ahsayna`, it is that each form retains its separate nature. And... its state is separate. Meaning, despite being gathered, they will retain their own individuality. He can also separate it. As the Imams did. That they... with their forms... with one form, they made a manifestation. The Imams. So by what reason did that happen? That if they had been gathered (`jama'`), then they could not have been separated. But it was `ahsa` (enumerated), that they were gathered in such a nature that the individuality of each remains in its own place.

[Side question: In this, the Imam cannot be encompassed, except by the Imam. So does that mean... the Imam can also do it through his own power? The enclosure that is there, that everything is as it is, except by the power of the Imam... what does that mean?]

This is it, no? `Kullu shay'in ahsaynahu fi imamim mubeen`.

[Side comment: This is, of course, the Imam, but... the Imam also does not come within its scope.]

Everything will come into it.

[Side comment: I will... check it and say it.]

`Wa dhalika anna al-maqam fih nasut wa lahut` (And that is because the station has in it humanity and divinity). Now, he is comparing it with the word of the *shahadah*. In the Imam, there are `nasut` (humanity) and `lahut` (divinity). `Nasut` is the collection of `riyat` (subtle essences). When a *mu'min* passes away, then immediately after his passing, the `riyat` that emerge, those are the higher `riyat`. The `riyat` that emerge after burial are the lower `riyat`. From the higher `riyat` that emerge, the `nasut-e-zilli` (shadowy human forms) of the *nawasi* (successors) are made. They are made from the `riyat` which are... which are discharged before... after the passing, before the burial. And from these `riyat`, from which... which emerge after burial for three days... they emerge only for three days. After that, what there is are vapors. For three days, the `riyat` that emerge, from them are made the `nasut-e-tabi'i` (natural human forms). Yes.

So... and...

[Side question: The ones that come out first are more excellent?]

Yes, the ones that come out first are more excellent. But there is also a tradition that the one who passes away should be buried as soon as possible. This is also the guidance of the Messenger, that he should not be kept for too long. But he should be buried as soon as possible. Yes. So, this... these are in comparison to `nasut` and `lahut`, of the word of the *shahadah*, of negation and affirmation. And... this... `nasut` are, and... these `riyat`... from these `riyat`, `nasut` is formed. And `riyat` also do not immediately become `nasut`. But the `riyat` have to go through a process. Those `riyat` go to... the light of the moon draws them, then they go to Jupiter, then from Jupiter again... they go to the sun, then from there... they come to Jupiter again, then... their... their main station is the moon. To the extent that the shining that occurs in the sky on the 13th, 14th, 15th night, it is said that it happens because of these `riyat`. That their congregation becomes very large in those three nights, which is why their shining occurs. And this... this is what happens... for the one who has passed away, for three days the `riyat` that emerge, so if... meaning the relatives of the one who has passed away, so they are giving a notice, in a spiritual sense, that whatever you have to do in these three days, do it. Because I, then, once I leave, I will come back after a thousand years. A thousand... meaning that if you have to give charity, do good deeds, have meals, read *sadaqallah*, or have *fatiha khwani*, or have *beeswa khwani*, or give someone for reading the Quran, or give charity, then you do it within these three days. After that, whatever you do, it will be for the noble form and for the *fuzala'*. I have already departed. Yes. So this is the state of the `riyat`. And the `riyat` are also... the purified `riyat`, those purified `riyat` then descend... like drops of dew they descend. And on some fruit they descend. Which is not cooked. Like an apple, and such things which are not cooked. And then the Imam and the Imam's wife eat those fruits, and from that, conception takes place, and from that, the *Farzand-e-Imam* (Child of the Imam) is born. Yes. And the *Farzand-e-Imam*, as soon as he is born, the nourishment he takes, it is entirely `riyat`. They are actually... they appear to be... drinking milk or later some... eating nourishment, the Imam's child, but in reality, they are `riyat`, which they eat. Just as one incident happened, that... the... Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq's... the `bab al-abwab` (gate of gates) who was there, Mufaddal bin 'Umar... Mufaddal bin 'Umar came to Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq's presence, he was present. Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq was eating food. So he said, "Ya Allah, join us in the meal." So he said, "Mawlana, I have just eaten and come." So he said that what you ate, what we are eating, you have not eaten. What we are eating, you have not eaten. And what you ate, we do not eat. Meaning that what you eat, those are external nourishments that you eat, which Allah has created. And what we eat are `riyat`, in which you should join us. This was his indication. And `riyat`, the *mu'min* also receives them. And when does the *mu'min* receive those `riyat`? During his *wudu* (ablution). As soon as he says Bismillah, and the breath he takes, and the water that touches his body, and that water which enters his body through the pores, those are the `fuzala'` and `riyat` that are received by the *mu'min*. This is one... one time this happens. Meaning, for the *mu'min* to receive it, this is one way. That is why it is said that *isbagh al-wudu* (performing wudu completely) is a great... of great status. Meaning, as it has been said in the *wudu*, that some things become atonements. For major sins, they become atonements. Among them, one thing is *isbagh al-wudu* `fil-burudat` or `sabarat` it has been said in one place. That in extreme cold, he... performs *wudu* with piety. Or `burudat` does not just mean that it is very cold. Not in the atmosphere. But in the temperaments, the coldness that is there... that the temperament is not willing to perform *wudu*. There is laziness in the temperament, in such coldness also, performing *wudu* with completion. This is the meaning. In the external sense also it means, in the time of cold. And in the internal sense, it is that when there is coldness in the temperaments, at that time also, doing it completely.

[Side question: The `riyat` that are received, are they for the common *mu'min* also?]

Yes.

[Side question: For the *mu'min* also?]

Yes.

[Side question: Whose `riyat` are these?]

[Reply:] The `mu'min's` own. The `mu'min's` own `riyat` are received by him. The purified `riyat` that are ascending, they come into the *mu'min's* share. Yes. And those `riyat` are in the moon. Now, the `riyat` have emerged. After emerging, they are in the moon. So from the moon, they are connected to him, those `riyat`, who performs *wudu* and so on. That is why it has been said that after *wudu*, the person who... this is also in a tradition, that after *wudu*, whatever water is left on your hand, you should shake it off. That by shaking it, from every drop that you shake, an angel will be created who will pray for your forgiveness. Yes. And it has also been said that you should recite *salawat* (blessings) and Surah al-Qadr, `inna anzalna`, you should recite. And pray. By doing so, your prayer will not be rejected. This has also been said in the blessings of *wudu*. And this is also there, that... this... in the second state, when does he receive nourishment from the `riyat`? At the time of his *iftar* (breaking fast) and *sahri* (pre-dawn meal). At the time of *sahri*, the things he receives, and at the time of *iftar*, the things he receives. That is why it has been said that... you should have your *sahri* meal at the last moment. Meaning not that you wake up at two o'clock and have *sahri*. In the night. But have *sahri* at the last moment. And in breaking the fast, you should hurry. You should hurry in breaking the fast. Meaning not that... you pray and so on and then break the fast, but hurry in breaking the fast. Because the things you are receiving are of a very high nature. Hurry in obtaining them. Yes.

[Side question: The `riyat` that are received, do they become part of the body?]

[Reply:] Yes, they become part of the body. They ascend. Yes. So, that... the state of that has not been mentioned, or I have not read it, that those `riyat` become part of the body, so do they go into his heart, or do they go into his body? I have not read that. But they enter his body. As water... through the pores or through respiration. Yes. As he says Bismillah, so after Bismillah, the breath he takes, all of that, he gets nourishment from the `riyat`.

[Side question: Are they his own `riyat`?]

[Reply:] No, not his own `riyat`. He has not passed away yet. The `riyat` of the believers. The purified `riyat` of the believers which are gathered in the moon. From there, these things are obtained by him.

Now, once... you... Sayyidna... whom did we visit in Iran? Who was he?

[Side comment: Avicenna.]

Avicenna. So, Ali bin... Sina, who was... he was a great *da'i*, a very great *hujjat* (proof). So, with him was a student who was very dear to him. That student used to say to him that if you were to claim prophethood, people would not deny you. You are so great, and you have mastery over everything. What field of knowledge is there that you do not have mastery over? You have mastery over medicine, you have mastery over... everything. So he would smile, and... not give him any answer. So, once he was traveling with him, and in Iran, that was the time of snowfall. It was a time of snowfall, and he was traveling. So while traveling, at one place these people reached. So they saw that it was the time of *maghrib* (evening prayer). It was the time of *maghrib*. And they saw a person from a distance, that he was... the snow had settled, so he was breaking the snow and making water, so that he could perform *wudu* with it. They saw this. So... he... they were watching this state, both of them. So... Sayyidna Abu Sina... Avicenna said to that person, to that student, that look, you always used to ask me why I don't claim prophethood. That if I were to claim prophethood, people would not deny me. So today I give the answer to that, that 300 years have passed since the passing of the Messenger, may Allah's blessings be upon him. And in his *shari'ah* (law), what effect will there be, in your sacred self, what effect will there be, that today this person, without caring for his own life, is breaking this snow and performing *wudu*. So... and I... well, before that what had happened was that one night before, one night before, these people were in a traveler's inn. They were in a traveler's inn. And that night what happened was that when they went to sleep at night, so... he became thirsty. The master became thirsty. Avicenna became thirsty. So he said to the student, "Please give me some water." So the water pot that was there, it was kept in the veranda, meaning one had to go outside to get it. So he said, "Mawlana, you can drink the water in the morning. Right now, it is very cold. You can drink the water in the morning." So he... the next day he mentioned this, that you were saying that I should make such a claim. My state is such that I could not get my dear student to get up and fetch me water. And this person, 300 years later, following the *shari'ah* of the Messenger, is breaking the snow and performing *wudu*. So this was the state.

So, this `nasut` and `lahut` are in comparison to the negation and affirmation of the word of the *shahadah*. Just as negation and affirmation... `li 'izzat wal-jabarut` (for the Almighty and the All-Powerful). Who is Allah Ta'ala, the possessor of might and power. `Wa lil-imam 'alayhi al-salam thalathat hujub` (And for the Imam, peace be upon him, there are three veils). And the Imam Alayhis Salam has three veils. Meaning, fundamentally there are three veils. Otherwise, they also take work from different veils. `Mathalu thalathat jawahir al-nizam` (The example of the three jewels of the order). Just as in the word of the *shahadah*, there are three jewels: *alif*, *lam*, *ha*. These are the jewels. So in the same way, they have three veils. These are... `tabi'i`... it is pointing towards the three `nasut`. `Nasut-e-tabi'i` (natural human form), `nasut-e-zilli` (shadowy human form), and `nasut-e-khas` (special human form). Yes. `Wa kullu imam min mawlana husayn jama'a rutub al-arba'ah` (And every Imam from our master Husayn has combined the four ranks). And every Imam from Imam Husayn has combined the four ranks. Whereas, what happened with Imam Hasan? Imam Hasan had two ranks. And Imam Husayn had two ranks. Yes. So, in Imam Hasan's time, those two higher ranks were with Imam Hasan. Therefore, he was the Imam. Whoever has the higher ranks is the Imam. Just as happened in the past. In the progeny of Janab Ibrahim, whoever had the two higher ranks, he was the Imam of the time. Meaning Qaydar was there, Mawlana Ismail was there, and the other Imams who were there, they all had the two higher ranks. And the prophets of their time had the two lower ranks, who were for the mission. But... after Imam Husayn, when the four ranks were combined, after the passing of Imam Hasan, so those ranks... the four ranks then did not get divided. Where were the four ranks first gathered? They were gathered with Imam Husayn. But...

[Phone rings and short interruption]

Go ahead and reconnect.

[Interruption continues]

Yes...

[Side conversation: It takes a 40-minute break?]

Yes.

[Interruption ends]

Are you back?

[Response from phone: Yes, I'm back. We were talking about the three veils.]

Yes. Yes. So the three veils are `tabi'i`, `zilli`, and `khas`. These were the three veils. And... this was being discussed, that with Mawlana Husayn, when the four ranks were combined, then after that, with the Imams, with all the Imams there will be, they will have the four ranks combined. Before this, Imam Hasan had two ranks, of Imamate and succession, due to which you were called the Imam. And two lower ranks were with Imam Husayn, due to which you were under his authority, of Imam Hasan. And in the past too, from Mawlana Ismail's progeny that came, Janab Ibrahim's progeny that came, there too, Janab Ismail had the two higher ranks, and his prophet, the prophet of that time, who was Is'haq, with him were the two lower ranks. So this matter continued in the past. But here, after Imam Husayn, when the four ranks were combined, so those four ranks... with the Imams, they always remained. For the future Imams. But this is also a fact that the first Imam with whom the four ranks were combined was Mawlana Ali Zayn al-'Abidin. Because with Imam Husayn also, the two lower ranks dominated over the higher ones. He was also dominated. But once, when the four ranks were combined with Mawlana Ali Zayn al-'Abidin, so his status became higher, within the Imams, it became higher. That is why you are called the `Adam-e-Aal-e-Aba` (the Adam of the family of the Prophet). You are called Adam. Just as Adam was the first, in the same way, this is the Adam of the family of the Prophet. Meaning, he has become the Adam of the Ahl al-Bayt (People of the House). This.

[Side question: The Messenger also had the four ranks, no?]

[Reply:] The Messenger also had the four ranks.

[Side question: They were given to Mawlana Ali.]

[Reply:] Yes. Because there, with Janab... Rasul... what happened was that the two higher ranks, they were also gathered, but with you, those ranks were as a trust (`amanat`). They were a trust because... from the old... after Mawlana Abd al-Muttalib, it happened that with you, the four ranks that were there, with Mawlana Abd al-Muttalib, so you gave the two higher ranks to Mawlana Abu Talib, and the two lower ranks... Janab Abdullah had passed away. A grandfather can bequeath, in inheritance, things can go to a grandson. So you, for your grandson, the two ranks that were there, for Janab Rasul, those were kept as a trust with him. With Janab Abu Talib. And then Janab Abu Talib... now the appointment had to be for Janab Ali. Imam after Imam. So what did you do? Janab Abu Talib gave all four ranks to Janab Rasulullah. The two ranks of mission and prophethood were for Janab Rasul, which had been coming from the old, from Janab Abd al-Muttalib. But the two ranks that were there, from his own side, of Imamate, those were given to Janab Rasul as a trust, that this is a trust for Ali. For Ali. So `man kuntu mawlahu fa hadha 'aliyyun mawlahu` (Of whomsoever I am the master, Ali is his master). Of whomsoever I was the master, meaning of these ranks, Ali is his master. This is the meaning. Yes.

`Wa mathalu arba'at rutub... al-muqatta'at al-arba'ah fil-kalimat al-shahadah: La, ilaha, illa, Allah` (And the example of the four ranks is the four sections in the word of the *shahadah*: La, ilaha, illa, Allah). For the intelligent and powerful one. `Wa istirahat al-maqam fis-sabi' min al-ayimmah` (And the resting of the station is in the seventh of the Imams). And the seventh that comes, so that seventh is called the station of rest. That with him, the things... the seventh Imam... the things of the six Imams are gathered, so it is the station of rest. Now there is a restful station. He doesn't have to do anything now. He has six... seven... the seventh becomes the `jami'` (gatherer). Yes. This is called the station of rest. `Min al-ayimmah al-qa'im... li man sabaqahu min al-ruh` (From the Imams, the قائم (one who rises)... for those who preceded him of the spirit). And that he... for them... for the past Imams, he becomes a station of the spirit. `Mathalu sab'at fusul fil-shahadah` (The example of the seven sections in the *shahadah*). And this is seven sections that are in the word of the *shahadah*. When the word of the *shahadah* is written in the Arabic style, where our pen... lifts off the surface of the paper, those are seven places. So at those seven places, our pen lifts, so those seven... what are they... they are... on the station of rest, for the Imams, or... the seventh who is, the status of the `sabi'` (seventh) is very high, he becomes the `jami'`, it is an indication towards that. `Wal-imam majma' suwar ithna 'ashar jazirah bil-akhir` (And the Imam is the confluence of the forms of the twelve islands in the end). `Mathalu ithna 'ashar harfan fil-shahadah al-kabirah` (The example of the twelve letters in the great *shahadah*). The Imam is the confluence, with him is the confluence of the twelve islands, the twelve *du'at* (summoners). The confluence of the forms of the twelve *du'at* is with the Imam in the end. Just as there are twelve letters in the word of the *shahadah*. On them. `Wa jamee' dhalika... thamaniah wa 'ishruna... suratan haddan... nizam al-'alam al-'ala sarmada` (And the total of that is twenty-eight forms as a limit... the order of the higher world, eternally). And when we add them up, two and three and four and seven and twelve, they become twenty-eight. Which is the `nizam al-'alam al-'ala` (the order of the higher world), eternal. Yes. That is another discussion.

[Side question: There was a mention of *falak-e-muheet* which ascends without coming to the earth. So does it go and meet the `barzakh-e-'ashir`?]

[Reply:] No, that has not been mentioned, or I have not read it.

[Side comment: Because there is a verse of the Quran, `yanfudhuna la tanfudhuna illa bi sultan` (they pass through, they do not pass through except with an authority). So whatever thing is ascending, it waits in the `barzakh-e-'ashir` until the authority comes.]

[Reply:] Yes, yes. So it is deduced from that that it is the same for *falak-e-'ashir*. Yes, yes. It is not mentioned in the text, but it is deduced that it happens this way.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. We make this reading of the Fatiha a gift, a means of connection, lofty, pure, and pleasing, to the soul of Sayyidna Ali bin Hibatullah, may Allah sanctify his soul and may his grace be upon us and may Allah increase us in his blessings. O Allah, accept from us this benefit and benefiting for the sake of what is in it, for the sake of Sayyidna Muhammad and his progeny, upon them be peace. Al-Fatiha. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alameen. Ar-rahman ar-raheem. Maliki yawm al-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayr al-maghdubi 'alayhim wa la al-dallin. For the sake of Sayyidna al-Da'i al-Mutlaq, the chief of the two *da'wahs*, the *zill* (shadow) of the *hadd* of Sindh, the honor of the noble, the pride of the Arabs and non-Arabs, our master Sayyidna Sahib... Sayyidna Muhammad Burhanuddin, may Allah sanctify his soul... his sacred mausoleum, may Allah elevate his station, may Allah protect him and keep him safe and help him and heal him from all diseases and pains and calamities, and increase his life... and give him strength... and may He protect him with the divine protection, the magnificent one... and may Allah prolong his life... may his sacred station and ranks be elevated, and may He grant us and you the visit to the noble presence, and may we and you be with his blessings. For the sake of Sayyidna al-Da'i al-Mutlaq, Mawlana Mufaddal Saifuddin, may Allah prolong his noble life... al-Fatiha. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alameen. Ar-rahman ar-raheem. Maliki yawm al-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayr al-maghdubi 'alayhim wa la al-dallin.

<!-- lecture 5 -->

...that you do not make it, that your hands are tied to your neck. Nor should you open it completely, like you completely open it, then you will be, uh, blamed and regretful. So if you do this, you will be blamed and you will be regretful. Meaning, you will regret thinking, "What have I done?" What does this mean? The hand on the neck. Yes. Do not do it, that your hands are on your neck, tied. And do not open it completely. So, a similar verse is also in Surah al-Furqan. In Surah al-Furqan, what does it say? That... Are you getting the audio, Muslim bhai? Yes, I am. Yes, ji. That... In Surah al-Furqan, it is said that... uh... Yes. I'm not getting the audio properly. Oh, I see. In Surah... In Surah Furqan... Yes... ...which verse? In Surah al-Furqan, where there is mention of `'Ibad al-Rahman` (the servants of the Most Merciful)... Surah al-Furqan, 25th Surah... that, `Walladhina idha anfaqu lam yusrifu wa lam yaqturu wa kana bayna dhalika qawama` (And [they are] those who, when they spend, are neither extravagant nor miserly, and there is ever a just middle way between them). This is a verse of the same nature. Meaning, here in Surah Bani Isra'il, its meaning is that you do not tie your hands to your neck. Meaning, do not become so miserly that your hand does not even go into your pocket. This is the meaning. Your hand doesn't go into your pocket, but rather remains tied towards your neck. Do not become miserly. And do not become so generous that you feel regret because you have not left any single thing for your family or for yourself. In such a state, you will be blamed and you will feel regret. And here, what is in Surah al-Furqan is that, `wa-lladhina idha anfaqu lam yusrifu` (And those who, when they spend, are not extravagant). They are such people, the `'Ibad al-Rahman` (servants of the Most Merciful) whose praise is given, that the servants of the Merciful are such that when they spend, they are not extravagant in it. They do not spend wastefully. `Wa lam yaqturu` (And they are not miserly). And if they spend, they are not miserly in it. `Wa kana bayna dhalika qawama` (And there is ever a just middle way between them). And their conduct is a middle path. `Khayr al-umuri awsatuha` (The best of matters are those in the middle). Doesn't this create a conflict? Because we have been taught that the quality of the Rasul (Prophet) and the esteemed Imams was that they would give everything in charity. To the extent that a famous incident of the Rasul is that he was returning from a journey and gave away all the things on the way, and when he arrived home, there was nothing for the house. Yes. That state, you know, belongs to the `Awliya' Allah` (Friends of God) and the Rasulullah, in short, the `ma'sumin` (infallible ones). For example, what happened once was that a person, he, a person brought two of his sons to Amir al-Mu'minin. He said that I am a man of wealth, I am quite a wealthy person, and I have two sons. The elder son among them, he spends quite a lot, he spends on others, he spends on himself, he lives a very prosperous life. But the second son, he, uh, in him, he doesn't take pleasure in life. Rather, he presents your example, that how Amir al-Mu'minin lived such a brief and difficult life, and his clothing was also so coarse, so thick. And his food is also so little and it is a harsh kind of food. So he presents your example. So Amir al-Mu'minin said that you should not take this example of ours in your life, but rather, you should benefit from the blessings that Allah has given you. Yes. You have said. And you said that what the elder son is doing is correct, and you should also do the same. When Allah has given you blessings, then you should use those blessings. So this is also a narration. Mawlana 'Ali has many incidents where you would do manual labor and on the way, you would give it in charity. When you reached home, you had nothing. And this was the state of Rasulullah as well. The incident they are narrating, at that time, Janab Khadijah had passed away. And the Hudhur, he, a goat... he used to slaughter a lot, a lot. Rasulullah used to slaughter. So he left with a goat from his house, and you slaughtered it. And when he returned, there was only one leg of the goat. He brought one leg home. So Janab 'A'ishah said, `Ma baqiya illa hadha` (Nothing is left but this). No thing is left except for this? You have given everything in charity and as `sadaqat` (alms). So you replied, and it was a very eloquent reply, he replied in the same tone, `Kullun baqiya illa hadha` (Everything remains except this). Yes. And 'A'ishah and Hafsah, how much they must have troubled the Rasul, to the point that a Quranic verse was revealed... where the sound is coming from clearly. Sound... Sir, the sound is coming. Hashim bhai? Yes, I am getting it. Yes, it's coming. Well, yes. So for those who are listening in Sindh, the people of Sindh are getting it? Yes, salam alaikum, ji, it's coming, the sound is coming. Ji. Yes. Ji. What was I saying? That the verse of the Surah is... Yes, that happened. That he said, `kullun baqiya illa hadha` (everything remains except this). Every thing is left except for this. But after that I was saying something. You were saying that 'A'ishah and Hafsah, what they did to the Rasul... Yes. 'A'ishah and Hafsah troubled the Rasul so much that a Quranic verse was revealed that, `laqad saghat qulubuhuma` (your hearts have indeed inclined). That their hearts have become crooked. Brother, I'm not understanding. Brother, I'm not understanding. Is that so? The sound is not coming? It's getting muted. No. The sound is cutting out. The voice is cutting out a lot, it's not coming. This is kept right in front. Uh... No, nothing, nothing. Now, now you listen, for one or two minutes, listen again. My voice is coming clearly. Your voice is coming, but the Hazrat's voice was not coming at all. The Hazrat's voice is coming. One minute, one minute. Ji. Now, now listen, Hazrat... Muslim bhai? Now, now listen. In Surah al-Tahrim, it is said that the hearts of both of them have become crooked. And Allah will not accept their repentance. Although Allah is `Tawwab al-Rahim` (the Acceptor of repentance, the Most Merciful). Allah will not accept their repentance. And if they made a display, meaning, they came in opposition to you, then Allah is sufficient, and Jibril is sufficient, and the `Salih al-Mu'minin` (righteous believers). The `Salih al-Mu'minin` here means 'Ali is sufficient. `La tuzara hidayatuhu`. Yes. Are you getting it, Muslim bhai? Muslim bhai, is the sound coming? Is my sound coming? Hello. Is the sound coming? Is the sound coming? No, no, brother, it's not coming at all. No, no, it's not coming. It's not coming. My voice is coming... Fazil bhai, your voice is coming, keep the Hazrat's mic close to you. Oh, okay. Let's do it. All right. Is it coming now? Yes. Is it coming now? No. Yes, now it's better. Better. Yes, yes, it's better. So, up until here we had read yesterday, that... `Wa fi wajhin akhar` (And in another aspect), the 22nd page. `Fi wajhin akhar` (in another aspect), that in the second reason, `yuniru min ahl al-basa'ir al-suwar` (it illuminates the forms from the people of insight), that the people of insight, it will illuminate their forms, this second reason. `Fa-llahu ta'ala alima` (So Allah the Exalted knows), `ma hadhihi sabila min hadha al-sirr al-ghamiz wa-l-ma'na` (what this path is of this obscure secret and meaning). That the secret in it and its hidden meaning is that `wa ja'alna al-bayta mathabatan li-l-nasi wa amna` (And We made the House a place of resort for the people and a place of security). That we, this is a verse of the Quran, that we made the Baytullah (House of God) what? A place of reward. And or a place of `thawab` (divine reward). For the people, and we made it a place of `amn` (security). So two things were said for the Baytullah. That we made the Baytullah a place of reward, or a place of `thawab`, or and we made it a place of `amn` (security). `Wa fi al-qalb mithlu ma fi al-shahadah al-mu'addiyah li-qa'iliha ila dar al-sa'adah` (And in the heart is the like of what is in the testimony of faith, which leads its speaker to the abode of felicity). And in the heart, there is that thing which is in the `shahadah` (testimony of faith). Such that, it is the one that leads the speaker, the one who says `La ilaha ill-Allah` (There is no god but Allah), towards eternal felicity. `Wa dhalika anna al-qalb al-haqiqi al-mutala'li' bi-l-nur huwa tahta hadha al-bayt al-ma'mur` (And that is because the true heart, glittering with light, is beneath this Frequented House). That the `qalb` (heart), in reality, it, the shining one with light, it is in fact, it, the Bait al-Ma'mur (Frequented House) which we circumambulate, it is beneath it. Which, which, the `qalb al-haqiqi` (true heart), the `qalb al-haqiqi` is beneath this `bayt al-zahiri` (outer house). `Alladhi hadha lahu ka-l-hijab wa huwa hakhadha ka-l-muhtajib` (For which this is like a veil, and it is thus like the one veiled). This outer house which we circumambulate, it is like a `hijab` (veil), and that house, which is beneath it, is like the one veiled. `Fi jami' al-asbab, mithlu al-nafi wa-l-ithbat` (In all the means, like the negation and the affirmation). Just as in the `kalimat al-shahadah` (word of testimony) there is negation and affirmation, in the same way, this, the Bait al-Ma'mur and that which is the `bayt al-haqiqi` (true heart), it is an example of negation and affirmation. And in another place it has come that opposite this Bait al-Ma'mur, the Baytullah, in `ta'mur` there is a `qalb` (heart), a Baytullah, which is opposite to it. This outer, outer house. And that is the one veiled, and this is like the veil. Yes. This explanation has also come. Hazrat, did this explanation not come before? What? Yes, ji. It's below as well. The `shahadah`, `allati man qalaha mukhlisan dakhala al-jannah` (The testimony of faith which, whoever says it with sincerity, enters Paradise). I remember once we were on Hajj, on 'Umrah, so we didn't get space upstairs, so we went down into the basement. Ji. So we prayed there too, so I said there's no issue. Yes. In the Ka'bah, in the Baytullah, in the basement below, we prayed. Ji, ji. Meaning, beneath the Baytullah... Not in the basement of the Baytullah. Meaning, you did not go under the Ka'bah itself. Not in the basement of the Ka'bah. Meaning, say the Baytullah is on this level, so we were praying here. It becomes diagonal. In diagonal it's fine. Yes. No, it wasn't diagonal. The way we are not inside the Baytullah, we are not in the basement of the Baytullah either. We are below the surface of the Baytullah. Yes, below the surface of the Baytullah. Just as we can pray above the Baytullah and it is permissible, in the same way, we can also pray below the Baytullah, that is also permissible. And this is the `kalimah` (word) `La ilaha ill-Allah` (There is no god but Allah), that whoever says it with sincerity, enters Paradise. That Paradise, `allati u'iddat li-lladhina ahsanu al-husna wa ziyadah` (which is prepared for those who do good, for them is the best [reward] and an increase). Such a Paradise that is prepared for those people who did a good deed and increased in the good deed. Now what does this mean? Paradise is prepared for those people who did a good deed. Now what is a good deed? `Al-hasanatu hubbuna wa-l-sayyi'atu bughduna` (The good deed is our love, and the evil deed is hatred of us). Amir al-Mu'minin says. What is a good deed? It is our love. `Al-hasanatu hubbuna wa-l-sayyi'atu`, and evil is hatred of us. Meaning, of the Ahl al-Bayt (People of the House). Now, this is a Quranic verse: `li-lladhina ahsanu al-husna` (For those who do good is the best [reward]). Those people who did a good deed, and increased in the good deed. So what are its realities that have been explained? Those people who did a good deed, meaning, in this `karrah` (cycle), those who took the `'ahd al-sharif` (noble covenant), and after taking the `'ahd al-sharif`, they increased in their faith. So they, in the next `karrah`, in the next `karrah`, whatever rank they were at, they will be given a rank higher than that. They will come to that rank, but they will be granted a rank higher than that. `li-lladhina ahsanu al-husna wa ziyadah` (for those who did good is the best [reward] and an increase). So this is about the next `karrah`. Meaning, those who did a good deed in this `karrah`, then their ranks will increase in the next `karrah`. And in another place, what is it? That a believer, in whatever state he passes away, at whatever rank he passes away in his life, then it is incumbent upon `Bari' zubanuhu qudratuhu` (the power of the Creator) that in the next `karrah`, he brings him to that rank in some way or another. Just as we see that many people progress quickly and move ahead. Like this. So, it appears outwardly that they were very far behind, but they suddenly leap forward and move ahead. So why is this? Because in their previous `karrah`, they were established at that rank. Therefore, the journey that will now take place will be a journey from that rank to further ahead. Yes. For example, with one elder, it happened that someone was given `fursat` (permission/appointment), so someone from among the `hudad al-da'wah` (limits of the mission) said that, "Mawlana, we did not think that you would give him `fursat`." This was said by one of the `hudad`. So, he said that, "Look, we do not give `fursat` based on someone's merit, but rather on this basis, that he may complete his `hasanat` (good deeds) and virtues." Meaning, they were established at that rank in the past, from the previous `karrah`. Therefore, it is incumbent upon us that we bring them to that place. And then, whatever progress they make after that, they will progress through their own knowledge and action. `Wa fi al-qalb thalatha tajwifat, mithlu al-thalathah al-jawahir` (And in the heart are three cavities, like the three jewels). And what happens is that a person himself does not realize at what rank he is established. Just as it is said that with the Da'i, there are many such `hudad` (ranks) who are very high in rank, but they are not in a state where they can be brought from potentiality to actuality. Yes. For example, what is its example? That the `mansub` (appointee) we have, who has the first grave in Baroda, Wali Muhammad Sahab's... the small mausoleum that has been built, no? Mulla Karam 'Ali Sahab. Mulla Karam 'Ali Sahab's. So, Mulla Karam 'Ali Sahab's... she was... at that time, Sayyidna Hibatullah... it was the time of Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahab, to whom these were `mansub`. And at that time, the matter of Da'ud bin Fi had occurred. And therefore, Sayyidna Sahab wrote a treatise in which he clarified, and you clarified that what list of `hudad` I have. So you made a list. It was a very strange thing that happened. Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahab did, because that Da'ud bin Fi said that I am greater in knowledge and in rank than the Da'i. This was his assumption. Later, he repented and then he passed away as a believer. After repenting, he also went to Najran and his repentance was accepted, he was also forgiven by Sayyidna Sahab, but his rank was kept at the level of a believer. Yes. So, Sayyidna Sayyidna Hibatullah bin Ibrahim Sahab made a list, and in that list, he placed Sayyidna Mulla Karamullah Sahab, who was the Da'i of two `jazirah` (islands), meaning `mansub`, he placed him at the 28th number. Meaning, 27 people were superior to you. Even though he was a `mansub` of Hind and Sindh. And in it, at the eighth number was Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah Sahab. In that list. At the eighth number. And the first number was Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hiba's, whose book we are reading. The very first number was Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hiba's. On whom you had performed `nass sharif` (noble designation). But he passed away. So Sayyidna Sahab, leaving those seven, performed `nass sharif` on the eighth one. On Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hiba... Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah Sahab. So this state also exists. So, just as in the time of Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan, `qaddasa Allahu ruhahu` (may Allah sanctify his soul), it happened that Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn al-Mukarrami, who was the `sahib al-waqt` (master of the time), for some reason, he did not come for `majlis` (gathering). It was a Friday. The whole mosque was full. So he sent his servant, whose name was Rayhan, he was an Abyssinian servant. So you sent him to say that I will not be able to come, Husayn bin Hasan will lead the prayer. Husayn bin Hasan will lead the prayer. So there, there were 50 people named Husayn bin Hasan. So then they asked him, and he was told to ask the Da'i, "Who is Husayn bin Hasan?" "Who is Husayn bin Hasan? There are many Husayn bin Hasans." So he went to Sayyidna Sahab and said that people are asking, "Who is Husayn bin Hasan?" So Sayyidna Sahab said in a loud voice, "Husayn bin Hasan bin 'Abdullah bin Ahmad al-Mukarrami." So he came and said from the `mihrab` (prayer niche), in a loud voice, that "The Da'i means Husayn bin Hasan bin 'Abdullah bin..." He recited the whole lineage. He recited the lineage for ten generations. And Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan was sitting at the eighth or tenth number. And before him, there were five `manasib` (high-ranking officials). Five `manasib`. The `mansub` of Yemen, Najran itself, who were Sayyidna Muhammad bin Jamali and Sayyidna Ahmad bin Jamali's father. Yes. So they were there. And all the `manasib` were present. But you chose Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan and said that he should lead you in prayer. And then it also happened that when Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn came, he brought Sayyidna 'Abdullah ibn Hamid here. And when, and he announced to all of us who were being taught by the `hudad`, he announced everywhere that we are going to Hindustan, and whatever matters of yours are there, Husayn bin Hasan will look after them. So people said that this is a `nass sharif` (noble designation). This is a `nass sharif`. And this matter became clear that the one you are taking with you, Sayyidna 'Abdullah bin Hamid, he will not become the Da'i after you. This matter became clear. Because... To leave him... Yes. Because the Da'i, it is a principle of his mission that the Da'i, the Da'i of the three `jazirah`, he cannot leave the `jazirah` of Yemen, which is the `qalb` (heart), unless he leaves his `na'ib al-maqam` (deputy) there. Otherwise, he cannot leave it. So, Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan... Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn left that station of Najran, and after leaving, he also took Sayyidna 'Abdullah bin Hamid with him. So what does that mean? That he won't be. And you appointed Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan there. So this was the situation. At that time, probably Sayyidna 'Abdullah Hamid must have passed away. No, no. At that time... No, no, Sayyidna 'Abdullah Hamid was there. The incident you are narrating... Yes. Yes, yes. Perhaps later, the one you brought... No, no, no. So, the `tahtul baytullah` (basement of the House of God) that came up, in that, there was a mention in another place that beneath the basement of the Baytullah is the `sakhrah` (rock). So the `sakhrah` that is, it is separate. Yes. It is separate. So its state is separate. It is not directly in the basement. So in the `sakhrah` there are ten `abrar` (righteous ones). There are ten `abrar` in the `sakhrah`. And the last person is Nimrud. Yes. After Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il, we were reading, so you asked us that among the ten `abrar`, who is the last person? So we said Yazid, but you said no. Yazid is not even among the ten, you said. Yes. And whose name did you mention? No, I don't remember them, but you said this, and you said that Nimrud is the last person. Because he claimed to be God. Right? `Ana Rabbukum al-A'la` (I am your Lord, the Most High). He claimed this, to be God. To be a god. `Wa fi al-ithna 'ashar al-jazirah, ithna 'ashar qalban sharifan, mithlu al-ithna 'ashar harfan fi al-shahadah` (And in the twelve islands are twelve noble hearts, like the twelve letters in the testimony of faith). And the twelve `jazirah` (islands), so there are twelve `qalban` (hearts) in it, noble. Which is an example of the twelve letters in the `kalimat al-shahadah` (word of testimony). That just as the `kalimah` of `La ilaha ill-Allah` is made up of twelve letters, in the same way, these are twelve `jazirah`. And every `jazirah` has become a `qalb`. Meaning, every `jazirah` is a `qalb`. There are twelve `jazirah` also, and every `jazirah` is a `qalb`. `Min qawlihi al-jamil, jami' dhalika thamaniah wa 'ishruna 'addan` (From his beautiful saying, the total of that is twenty-eight in number). And in this way, there are a total of 28. `Li-l-diyanah wa-l-huda. Fa-sun hadha al-sirr al-masun` (For the religion and the guidance. So preserve this guarded secret). And the number 28 is always maintained. Just as in the `hudad` also, there is a 28th, whose status is very high, like Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman is the 28th in the sequence, their status is very high, and just as the 28th Imam, Sayyidna Ma'add, Mawlana Ma'add, their status is also very high. So in this way, the 28th one, their status is very high. But the 29th one, their status is also very high. Just as you will see that for Rasulullah, what came? That the `kalimat al-shahadah` has come in five tones in the Quran. `La ilaha ill-Allah`, `La ilaha illa hu`, `La ilaha illa ana`, `La ilaha illa alladhi`, and `La ilaha illa anta`. And `La ilaha illa anta` is rare, that it has come only once. The rest, all the `kalimah` `La ilaha ill-Allah`, `ill-Allah`, `La ilaha illa hu`, have been repeated. But `La ilaha illa anta` has come only once in the Quran. And that is in Surah... Yunus, peace be upon him's, it is a supplication that you made as a form of glorification. Yes. So, this `kalimah` `La ilaha ill-Allah`, its completion is what? It is from Muhammad Rasulullah. And Muhammad Rasulullah himself has also come in the Quran. And that has come only once. That has also not been repeated. So this `kalimah` which is `La ilaha ill-Allah`, in five tones, that except for `La ilaha illa anta`, all have been repeated. And Muhammad Rasulullah, that came only once, and that too in Surah al-Fath, in the 29th verse it came. Therefore, which Rasulullah was he in the sequence? He was the 29th. Meaning, Janab 'Ali was the 28th. Meaning, from Janab Ibrahim to, the Imams who have been coming, and in those Imams, who are the appointed Imams, meaning, in the sequence, he is not the 28th in sequence, but such a sequence has been established in which you are the 28th. So the 28th is Janab 'Ali, peace be upon him, whose status is very high, the 28th's. And the 29th is Janab the Prophet. And the 29th one, whether he is among the Imams or among the `du'at` (missionaries), his state is rare, and he disrupts the sequence. He enters into the sequence. Meaning, the sequence that is going on of the Imams or the `du'at`, he enters into that sequence. Just as Rasulullah, `sallallahu alayhi wa sallam`, he entered into the Bayt... an Imam... he was not as an Imam, because after Imam, Janab Abu Talib, it should have been Janab 'Ali. But you entered. And 'Ali became your follower. Because all four ranks were combined in you, of `nubuwwah`, `risalah`, `imamah`, and `wasayat`. Therefore, you became the head, the master, even the master of 'Ali. Yes. So this state became that of Janab Rasul due to his own status, which was established in the `alam al-ruhani` (spiritual world). In the same way, we will see that among the `du'at`, Janab Sayyidna Ja'far is the 28th, and the 29th is Sayyidna Muhammad bin Faraj. So Muhammad bin Faraj, although he was a `da'i ghayr munfarid` (non-absolute missionary), he became a `munfarid` (absolute one). But he was established in the rank of `munfaridin` (absolute ones). Although, according to the principle, Sayyidna Ja'far passed away during his lifetime, you passed away. But you, you, you were established in the rank of `munfaridin`, and its reason was that Sayyidna Ja'far's state became elevated. And you were established in the rank of a `hujjah` (proof). `Hujjah`. `Hujjah` is one who is equal to thirty `du'at`. He is equal to thirty `du'at`. And after this Sayyidna Ja'far, all the `du'at` who have come, they have all been established in the rank of `hujjah`. Until today. There has not been a single Da'i who was not established in the rank of `hujjah`. So this is the status of the `qa'im al-du'at` (established missionaries). And that `hujjah`, the rank of `hujjah` on which they were established, every Da'i is equal to thirty `du'at`. So, when Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman was established in the rank of `hujjah`, then the `da'i al-balagh` (missionary of communication) of the three `jazirah`, the Da'i who was there, his rank became vacant. So on that rank, you performed `nass sharif` (noble designation) here. Therefore, you are kept in the sequence by us. Yes. And this sequence, you will see in the old `sahifah` (books), which are of Sayyidna Ghulam Sahab, Sayyidna Farhat Ali Sahab's, and Sayyidna Ziyayi Sahab Muqaddas's, `Zul Ma'ani`, the `sahifah` of that time, in them there is no mention of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Faraj. And here too, when this new `sahifah` was made, the mention of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Faraj was not made, in accordance with the old `sahifah`. But when Sayyidna 'Ali bin Husayn saw it, for three days you had the `sahifah`, which was not yet printed, its manuscripts were there, Nasrullah Sahab Muqaddas and 'Abdullah Sahab Muqaddas had taken them.
