That they both were in the cave. This is acknowledged by Allah. And then what is there? `*...fa-anzalallahu sakinatahu 'alayh* (...so Allah sent down His tranquility upon him)`. Allah sent down tranquility upon him. This is there. So, a *da'i* we have, whose name is Shahriyar, he has clarified this. That there, now there are two, and tranquility descended upon one. Now no Muslim can dare to say that the tranquility, which is a special form of God's help, descended upon Abu Bakr, and the honorable Prophet was restless and helpless. So, no Muslim would dare to say this. So there, tranquility descended upon the Rasul. Now someone might say that, yes, the Rasul is the Rasul, and Abu Bakr is Abu Bakr. But you see, elsewhere, in Surah al-Fath also, and in Surah al-Tawbah itself also, Allah has said at one place, `*anzalallahu sakinatahu 'alayhi wa 'ala al-mu'minin* (Allah sent down His tranquility upon him and upon the believers)`. That tranquility descended from Allah upon you and upon the believers. So, if he was a believer, then tranquility would have descended upon him too. So, the honorable Shahriyar, the *da'i*, has proven that the honorable Abu Bakr was not even within the circle of faith. If it were so, then that tranquility... for tranquility is descending upon the believers, which is clear in another verse. So he was not even within the circle of faith. So how can he become a caliph? And... and then, when Rasulullah passed away, then... in Saqifah Bani Sa'idah, the gathering was held. And the body of the Rasul, the corpse, was left without burial and shroud. As Shaykh Sa'di writes, that these dogs of the world... this is Shaykh Sa'di's couplet. The dogs of the world, who in their greed for the world, left the Prophet without...

Forty-one minutes have passed.

And there is also this, that as long as Muhammad ibn Abi Bakr's... that was in his loins... as long as it was there... yes, as long as it was there. And you know, both the children... the other one, 'Abd al-Rahman... 'Abd al-Rahman. According to the narrations, both were not there. The narration is about Muhammad ibn Abi Bakr. But another thing is that the matter of Ghadir of the Rasul and the passing away, the incident that happened, there is a gap of two-three months, four months. So during that period also, I have read in history that you had sent Usamah ibn Zayd. And these people were all below him. So they objected that we are being sent under his leadership. And he was seventeen years old, Usamah ibn Zayd. Seventeen years old. No, twenty-eight and a half years old. Yes, son of Harithah. Harithah's son. They did not go. No. Did they go or not? No, no, they didn't go. And Rasulullah, in his illness, kept asking, by name, "Did Abu Bakr go? Did 'Uthman go? Did 'Umar ibn al-Khattab go?" Yes. Is the sound coming? Is the sound coming? We are getting the sound. Yes, yes. We are getting it. Yes, we are getting it. We are not getting your voice. It's coming to Sholapur, they said. Is it coming now? It's coming. Sound check. Yes. Is the sound coming now? Muslim bhai? Yes, the sound is coming. Muslim bhai, is the sound coming? The sound is coming.

Yes. Is the sound coming, Muslim bhai? Yes, it's coming now. It's coming. Okay. Yes.

And the oath of allegiance was taken at a place, the Saqifah Bani Sa'idah, which was a place where thieves and robbers, after looting goods, would come and decide on the division amongst themselves. At that place, they decided on the caliphate. And while the discussion was going on, 'Umar ibn al-Khattab immediately came forward and gave his oath of allegiance. And he said, "Whoever does not give the oath, my sword is on his head." This is how the oath was taken. 'Umar ibn Khattab took the oath, and this is how the honorable Abu Bakr's oath took place. God forbid. Today that place has been turned into a public bath. Yes, it's a place for public baths. Sir, they say that if it's a democracy, then you see that in all three of them, the principle of democracy was destroyed. Yes, yes. There he nominated, and there he made it a council, the thing of six people. Yes, yes. Sir, Ali... democracy... Muslims cannot even speak of democracy. Absolutely not. It was all done in enmity to Ali. These people who were high-ranking, the *da'is*, weren't they aware of all these matters? The ones the Prophet is talking about... yes, and they were aware of all the sciences. Especially Abu Bakr. But the greed of the world and the lust for rank... for rank, they did all these things. Now we also see, right, what happened to Muhsin bin Ali? We know that he had so much knowledge, he was so... so well-read, what can I say? To the extent that at that time, in the time of Sayyidna Husayn ibn Hasan, I am talking about that time, Sayyidna 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad was appointed, and he was not appointed, Muhsin bin... Muhsin bin Ali. And when he would stand in prayer, behind Sayyidna Husayn ibn Hasan, Sayyid... 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad would stand in such a way that half the space would be occupied by him, and he would bring Muhsin close, and half the space would be occupied by him, from behind. Yes. This was the situation. Meaning, they were so close to each other. Such was the friendship. Sayyid 'Abdullah Muhammad had such great friendship with Muhsin bin Ali, that he... and because he used to sit on the left side. He used to sit on the left side. No, when we went on the journey, Sayyid sahib's... Muhsin used to sit behind him. No... no, Sayyid 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad was appointed, no? But when we went, you, along with you... Sayyid sahib would stand, then Muhsin would be there, then Sayyid 'Abdullah... No, no... Sayyid 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad was appointed, so he would stand behind him. But he would stand in such a way that he would bring Muhsin bin Ali close, occupying half the space himself and half the space for him. However, on Friday, Sayyid Muhammad al-Jamali would come, because he would only come on Friday. The rest of the time, he would pray in his own mosques. So he would come. So he was senior, so he would sit... to the left of... behind the Imam. To the left of Sayyid Husayn bin Ismail, he would sit. Yes.

That was the time of Sayyidna Husayn. Yes. When we went again, Muhsin was sitting behind. And after Muhsin was Sayyid 'Abdullah. Yes. Yes, I saw it. To the extent that you don't remember, that we... once... when the summers started, we went and prayed in the courtyard. So Sayyid sahib led the Maghrib prayer. Sayyid Husayn bin Ismail. And he... then... in the period between the two evening prayers, he would sit. He got up and went into the prayer niche. You remember? We all went behind him. So then he called Sayyid 'Abdullah Muhammad, Sayyid sahib did. And said something to him. And Sayyid sahib said that... you pray your prayer, I will pray my prayer. So at that time, Muhsin led us. I don't remember that. I don't remember it, it's not in my mind. The affairs really deteriorated then. Now, this... this was the situation, meaning between Sayyid 'Abdullah Muhammad and Muhsin, there was such friendship. Now it happened that when the clarification came, and Sayyid sahib forgave him... Sayyid 'Abdullah Muhammad, for the reason he was angry, and for many years, he tested Sayyid 'Abdullah Muhammad and Sayyid Ahmad al-Jamali. After that, he also performed the *nas* (appointment) and forgave him. So, when he became a *da'i*, Sayyid 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad, Muhsin came to your place on the third day. And he came and shook hands. He did not do the *musafahah* (full handshake). He shook hands. And said that you and I were at the same station. This was the sentence. You and I were at the same station. So, Sayyid 'Abdullah ibn Muhammad said that, "Yes, this is correct, that you and I were at the same station. But from my station, I have built a building. And you have... on your station... you have remained on that same station." Meaning, you remained on that same station. But from my station, I have built a great building... of faith. Yes.

Abu Bakr was also among the confidants of the era of 'Isa. No. No, he was not among the confidants. They are other individuals. Yes. They are... outside the list of the righteous. Yes. No, no... the six names have... yes... their names are there. In the six names, there are also common names. The teachers of the Rasul, among them and these confidants of the era of 'Isa. Yes. Now, these confidants of the era of 'Isa, their joining that happened... the six confidants, between the Rasul and 'Isa, there were six such individuals who... the precious treasures of the teachings of 'Isa, they gathered them. So, where did their joining happen? So then it was said that their joining happened with the Rasul. Although they were the confidants of the era of 'Isa. The confidants of the era of 'Isa. Who are also among the chosen ones of the `*dawr*`. Yes, they have a great station.

`*Fa-hwa yushiru 'ala anna kulla 'aqlin min al-'uqul al-sab' al-'izam yanzuru ila man qabalahu min al-rutab al-nutuqa' al-sab'a fi al-sarb wa al-nizam* (So he indicates that every intellect from the seven great intellects looks towards the one who is opposite it from the seven ranks of the Nutuqa' in the series and the order)`. And it indicates... this matter indicates that every intellect, which is from the seven great intellects... looks towards the one who is opposite it, from the ranks of the *nutuqa'*. The seven... in the series and the order. And the indication is there. The seven intellects are which ones? Leaving aside the first intellect and this... the tenth, leaving it aside, how many are in between? Eight. Eight. So in that, the first emanated one is also left out. The one who became unique. So after the first emanated one, until... before the tenth, there are seven intellects. So those seven intellects, every intellect, it... opposite it, the one who is... is there, meaning, the first intellect, it is... no, the first intellect means the third intellect. The third intellect, it is... giving materials and support, to whom? In the *nutuqa'*, the one who is the first. Who is that? Adam, peace be upon him. Similarly, the fourth intellect, which is the eternal one, it is giving support to the honorable Nuh. In this way it happened. That... `*Wa amma qawl al-natiq li-Kalilah, thumma istaqbili al-'aql al-khamis, dhu al-fadl wa al-fadilah, sahib al-nur al-a'zam al-anwar, mufid al-khayrat wa mu'ti al-fada'il, ila an qala 'alayhissalam wa al-salam minhu zaharat al-qudar al-Fatimiyyah* (And as for the saying of the Natiq to Kalilah, 'Then come forward to the fifth intellect, the owner of grace and virtue, the owner of the greatest, most luminous light, the bestower of good things and the giver of virtues,' until he said, peace be upon him, and peace is from him, 'from him the Fatimi powers appeared')`. What does this mean? I did not understand its meaning. That your, the Natiq's saying to Kalilah, Kalilah who was a jackal, that... you... come forward. So that, which is related to the fifth intellect, which is very virtuous and... etc. Which is the owner of the great light. What does this mean? I did not understand it. `*Fa-l-'aql al-khamis 'arrafna fi hadha al-wajh wa al-ma'na huwa al-Husayn ibn 'Ali Amir al-Mu'minin, al-khamis min al-Nabi* (So the fifth intellect, we have understood in this aspect and meaning, is al-Husayn ibn 'Ali Amir al-Mu'minin, the fifth from the Prophet)`. And this, the fifth intellect, is... in one aspect and in one meaning, it is Husayn ibn Ali, which is a reference to him, who is the fifth from the Prophet. Meaning, in the *Panjatan-i Pak* (the Holy Five), the honorable Amir al-Mu'minin... the honorable Prophet and the honorable Ali and the honorable Fatimah al-Zahra, Hasan and Husayn, they were the fifth. It is a reference to this. `*Wa al-qudar al-Fatimiyyah, hum al-a'immah min awladihi* (And the Fatimi powers, they are the Imams from his progeny)`. And the Fatimi powers are the *a'immah* who are from his progeny, meaning from the progeny of the honorable Imam Husayn, the *a'immah* who are, they are called the Fatimi powers. `*...wa awlad Maulana Fatim al-huda* (...and the children of our master, Fatim the guide)`. Who are the children of Maulana Fatim, who guide. `*Wa minhum ashraqat badayi' al-suwar al-musawwarat* (And from them, the splendors of the pictured forms shone forth)`. And from them, meaning from the *a'immah*, new forms appeared and shone forth. New, new forms... their advent shone forth. `*Wa bihim taharrakat al-mutaharrikat* (And by them, the movers moved)`. And by them, the ones who move, moved. These are all spiritual matters that happened, and the joinings that happened, there is a reference to that. And this... in the *Kanz al-'Ulum*, which was mentioned, that book, and which is of Sayyidna Ibrahim. Ibrahim ibn Muhammad al-Fad. *Kanz al-'Ulum*. One is *Kanz al-Walad*. *Kanz al-Walad* is of Sayyidna... Sayyidna... the second *da'i* who was, first was the *da'i* Sayyidna Dhu'ayb, the second was Sayyidna Ibrahim ibn Husayn al-Hamidi. Sayyidna Ibrahim ibn Husayn al-Hamidi wrote a book, *Kanz al-Walad*. And he wrote it for his son, Sayyidna Hatim. That a son who reaches the station of a *da'i*, what things should he be aware of? So, you wrote the *Kanz al-Walad*. Sayyidna Ibrahim did. But here, *Kanz al-'Ulum*, you... Sayyidna Ibrahim wrote it, who... Ibrahim ibn Muhammad al-Fad, and he mentioned in it that the *a'immah* who appeared, who kept coming forward, in the progeny of the honorable Husayn ibn Ali, in one sense they are also called *A'immah Fatimiyyin*. And the reason you stated for this is that they are in awe and reverence of the station of the honorable Bibi Fatimah al-Zahra. Yes. And it is also said that the honorable Rasulullah's state was that when you would come, when you would come, he would often stand up, and he would seat you in his place. So the reason for that was also this, that the gathering that had assembled with you, a gathering of such station, that when the Master of the Universe saw that gathering, out of joy and to honor that gathering, you would stand up. Yes. And what gathering was that? That gathering was... the gathering you assembled was of the *muhtajibin* of the *awsiya'*. The gathering of the *muhtajibin* of the *awsiya'*, you gathered them. Which is a very magnificent gathering. Yes. Meaning, the 'ayn... the 'ayn... yes, the 'ayn. Meaning, all the *awsiya'* that there are, their *muhtajibin*, the honorable Fatimah's *muhtajib* gathered them. Because of which... because of which, the station of the *hijab* increased. Your... the states that were there, of Fatimah bint Muhammad, that was the state of the *hijab*. But when the station of the *muhtajib* increases, then the station of the *hijab* also increases in this way. Yes. Wherever the footsteps of the Rasul fall, there the footsteps of Fatimah will fall. Yes, the footsteps of Fatimah will fall. The `khilafah`... the `khilafah` was established. Yes. And then, the `du'at` are in this regard in awe and reverence that in the station, who should be appointed? Bibi Fatimah al-Zahra or Ibrahim Khalilullah? Among them, who is the successor in the `khilafah` of the `ayn`? In the `khilafah` of the `ayn`, after the Rasul, in the `khilafah` of the `ayn`, will Fatimah al-Zahra come or will Ibrahim Khalilullah come? They are in awe and reverence about this. They have not clarified it. They are the envoys of the Rasul, no? Yes. The envoys of the Rasul will come. Yes. One of the `da'in`, Sayyidna 'Abdullah al-`Ali` I think, he has clarified that you have written a book. What is the name of that book? So, in that book, the brother Mukarram Saifullah sahib had transcribed it. So, that is a magnificent book. In it, you have written that the successorships of all the Imams until the Day of Judgment have been explained. Who will become the successor of the `ayn-i 'azimah` after whom. A very magnificent book. Every single... every single point, you have clarified.

`Wa bihim tajalla al-'ayn al-'azimah, 'ilmuhu, 'ulumuhu, wa biyutuhu, wa maqamatuhu al-fahimah` (And through them the Great 'Ayn manifested, His knowledge, His sciences, His houses, and His exalted stations). And by all of them, meaning, by the Imams of purity, the Great `'Ayn` manifested. In this state, that they are His sciences, and they are His houses, and they are His exalted stations. Meaning, the honorable Ali became the *muhtajib* for all the Imams. And they, the Imams, are His *hijab*. Yes. This is the state. Meaning, this is the station... this is the station of the Master of the Universe. And this matter... the station... and the station of the Imams, in Surah al-Fatihah, it has been clarified in this way: Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik says that Allah has started His word, after Bismillah, with `Al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin` (All praise is due to Allah, the Lord of the worlds). So, `al-hamd`, its `ma'rifah` (definite form)... this is `ma'rifah`. Its `nakirah` (indefinite form) is `hamd`. From `hamd`, `al-hamd` is made. So `hamd` is `ha`, `mim`, `dal`. And its numerical value is how much? `ha` is eight, `mim` is forty, and `dal` is four. So in this way, its numerical value becomes fifty-two. Fifty-two hundred... fifty-two. So from fifty-two, what is meant? That... the three (`thalathah`)... Muhammad, Ali, and Fatimah. And forty-nine Imams. Who are up to Abu al-A'immah. After Abu al-A'immah, then the era of manifestation (`dawr-i kashf`) begins, meaning, the Mahdi of the end of time will come, and the manifestation will occur. So, the honorable Muhammad and `salawatullah 'alayh`, and the honorable Ali, the honorable Fatimah al-Zahra, these three became. And after that, these forty-nine Imams. So forty-nine and three become fifty-two. So it is as if we, in `Al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin`, are recognizing them. We are recognizing them. `Wa-'bud rabbaka hatta ya'tiyak al-yaqin` (And worship your Lord until there comes to you the certainty [death]). That you should worship your Lord until certainty comes to you. Meaning, that where the word `yaqin` (certainty) is used, there it also refers to Amir al-Mu'minin. It also refers to Amir al-Mu'minin. And secondly, in the literal sense, `yaqin` means death. You should worship Allah until your death. Yes. And... until certainty comes to you. Meaning, you meet Ali. Because after passing away, the meeting will be with Ali. Yes. And he will ask you questions. Yes. As it is in the Quran, that when... what is the word used? That when they will be taken towards the heavens, the group will be taken... towards the heavens, which is said in Surah al-Zumar, which is the 39th Surah, that when they will be taken there, they will be stopped and questioned. They will be questioned. So, the commentators, the Sunni and Shia commentators, say that what was that question? What will be asked? That is the question of the love and `walayah` of Ali that will be asked. That is proven by دلیل (proof). Yes. That in your life, did you have the correct `walayah` for Ali or not? That question will be asked. Yes. As the Master says, that if a person, between the Rukn al-Asad and Maqam Ibrahim, which is the gap, which is a very blessed gap, so at that place, for his entire life, while worshipping, his joints become loose, and in his heart, there is no love for Ali, then Allah will throw him into hellfire headlong. Yes. Because the essence... the essence of the matter is what? Once, Rasulullah asked, "What is the essence of faith?" The companions were there. The Sahabah were there. So someone said, "It is prayer." So you said, "No, prayer is not the essence of faith." Then someone said fasting, someone said Hajj, someone said jihad. So, they had said all these things. Then the Rasul said, "It is not this, not this." You said, "It is `walayah` and love." Meaning, the `walayah` for Ali is the essence of faith. Yes.

Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. O Allah, convey the blessings of these holy offerings, a gift and a means, pure, pleasing, to the souls of the Holy Five, our masters Muhammad, Ali, Fatimah, Hasan, and Husayn. And accept from us this benefit and utilization. And may Allah sanctify the soul of our master Ali ibn Hibatullah. And accept, O Allah, this benefit and utilization in the best way possible, by the right of our master Muhammad and the family of Muhammad, and bless and grant peace. Al-Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin. Ar-rahman ar-rahim. Maliki yawm id-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayril maghdubi 'alayhim wa la ad-dallin. On behalf of Sayyidna, our master, the absolute Da'i, the treasurer of the house of truth, Sharafuddin, and Zeeshanuddin, Ghulam Husayn, son of Sayyidna Mansur, the sanctified one, and for Walidna Nasrullah ibn 'Abdullah Hibatullah ibn Mansur, the sanctified one, may Allah exalt his station. May Allah the Exalted protect him and guard him, surround him and conceal him, suffice him and heal him from the evil of jinn and men, and the evil of diseases, aches, pains, and calamities. From the evil of the treacherous of time and the days, from the evil of the movements of hours and moments, and the glances of the eyes, and the stumblings of the feet, and the machinations of the schemers. And may He make good his end in goodness and well-being. And may He protect him in the rest of his life. And may He prolong his pure life, and protect him in the fortress of his lofty station. And may He make his days pleasant in a good life and in the attainment of ranks and stations. O you who have been guided, may Allah grant us and you to be in the fortress of his `da'wah`. And may He grant us and you the blessings of his stations. By the right of Sayyidna and our master, the Imam, the manifest one, with magnificent authority, upon whom and upon his pure forefathers be blessings and peace. Al-Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb il-'alamin. Ar-rahman ar-rahim. Maliki yawm id-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat alladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayril maghdubi 'alayhim wa la ad-dallin. Shukran. Thank you. Thank you. Thank you. Thank you. Thank you. Thank you. Thank you.

<!-- lecture 8 -->

You may begin. In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful. And in Him we seek help, and may Allah's blessings be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and the family of Muhammad, and may He grant them blessings and peace.

The sixth question.

*Sound of a Bluetooth device connecting: "Krisonic, the Sound Master. Bluetooth mode."*

The sixth question.

I thought to myself, that is, when I fell short of drinking from the spring's source.

I reflected within my soul that I fell short of drinking from the spring's source, meaning, from the presence of the `Da'i`, that I was not in the presence of the `Da'i`, and I did not acquire knowledge directly from the `Da'i`. So I knew that this was a sin and a failing on my part. And I know that this matter—that I could not learn from the `Da'i`—is my sin and it was my failing that I did not get this opportunity. So, will I return, will I be, in the second form... Now I am asking you, will I, in the `fudla al-thāniyah`, that is, after my passing, when my `raj'at` (return) happens, the second time, in the second `karrah` (cycle), will I be in the same state as these present ones, just as I am today? Will I be the same? So be gracious, and inspire me with that which will remove this concept from me. So, please be gracious and inspire me so that it may open up this concept of mine. And make it clear. And remove from me regarding that every concealed veil. And lift for me every concealed veil.

So I hasten to seek forgiveness. And I am hastening to repent for my sins. That you be gracious unto me with the indication. That you be gracious unto me with knowledge. And that you cause me to be allotted a Hajj. And that you give me the opportunity to perform a Hajj of one of the `Hudud` (ranks of the Da'wah). Yes. To have my sins forgiven. Or any deed that will free me from those limits. Or I should perform some action that will release me from the `hudud` (limits/bonds) in which I am bound. Or I intend [to go to] the House of God. Or I should intend [to go to] the `Baytullah` (House of God). And I reside therein. And go and stay there. For years, in which I suffice. And I will abide by the command. And we seek Your forgiveness. And I seek forgiveness from Allah, that I should ask You about that of which I have no knowledge. And I ask you about a matter of which I have no knowledge. And unless You forgive me. And that you forgive me. And have mercy upon me, I shall be among the ignorant. And have mercy upon me, that I may be among the ignorant. And I seek refuge in Your pleasure from Your wrath. And in your forgiveness from your anger. And I seek refuge in Your pleasure, that I may be saved from Your anger. And from Your anger, that I may be in Your forgiveness.

The answer to that question. The answer to your question is: we say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Most High... We say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Most High, and with the grace of the `Wali al-'Asr wa al-Zaman` (Guardian of the Age and Time), and through the grace, meaning the blessing, of the `Wali al-'Asr wa al-Zaman`, may the blessings of the Merciful, the Compassionate, be upon him. May the blessings of the Merciful, the Compassionate, be upon you. And the support of our master, the most knowledgeable, the most luminous. And through the support of our master, the most knowledgeable, the most luminous, who is most radiant, and the secret that flows to the subtleties of the forms. And through your secret that is flowing towards the subtleties of the forms.

Regarding your statement... your question was that the `fudla al-thāniyah`... the coming `fudla`... about that... will you return like the first one? Will you return just as you are today, like the first? In the past cycles. As you were in the past cycles, will you be the same in the future? And "yes". So you replied, "Yes, it is so." Just as you... that is, in this `karrah` (cycle) as you will die, in that same state you will be born in the future. So you will return just as it was first. And you will return just as it was in the beginning. If he was a `Bab`... If he was a `Bab` (Gate), meaning `Bab al-Abwab`, in the previous `karrah`, then he will be a `Bab`. Then in this `karrah` too, he will be born as a `Bab`. For the people of the `milla` (community). For the people of the `milla`. And if he was a `Hujjah`, then a `Hujjah`. If he was a `Hujjah`, at the rank of a `Hujjah`, then here too, in this `karrah`, he will be at the rank of a `Hujjah`. And if he was a `Da'i`, then a `Da'i`. And if he was a `Da'i`, then he will be born as a `Da'i`.

He allots the `Hujjahs` and he allots the guided ones. And... this matter clarifies this thing. And if he was a `Ma'dhun`, then a `Ma'dhun`. If he was a `Ma'dhun`, then in this state he will come as a `Ma'dhun`. After being born a `Ma'dhun`, progressing in rank is a different matter. But it is the work of `Qudrat` (Divine Power) to place you at that rank... at the rank of `Ma'dhuniyyat`. And if he was a `Mukasir`, then a `Mukasir`. And if he was a `Mukasir`, then in this `karrah` too, you will be a `Mukasir`. Upon this foundation. And... upon this foundation. And if he was a `Mu'min`, then a `Mu'min`. If he has passed away as a `Mu'min`, then it is incumbent upon `Qudrat` that it brings you... in the second `karrah` which will... be born, you will be born as a `Mu'min`. Yes... will bring you to the level of a `Mu'min`.
Those who have `raj'at`, their death happens quickly, in childhood...
Yes, that is a different situation. Then for them, what is it that... it has been said that... they did not get the opportunity to take the `‘ahd-e sharif` (sacred covenant)... or their lifespan did not allow it. So then, when they come in the future `karrahs`, they will be born in the state of `iman`. Yes.

This rank is not transgressed. And in this way, it will not be transgressed that, well, he was born at one rank and then he will come to another rank, it does not happen like that. With increase or decrease. Neither will there be an increase nor a decrease. Of this text. So on this very pattern, the vehicle will keep moving. And he will be from the locality or the town from which he originated. And in whichever locality, whichever city, or any place, in whichever land he was born, and where he grew up, according to this course, according to the word of Allah, the Exalted... then in this `karrah` too, your birth will be in the same place. You will be born. As Allah Ta'ala says that, "From it We created you, and into it We will return you, and from it We will bring you forth another time." When we... in the grave... when we put soil on the deceased, we recite this verse from the Holy Qur'an, that "From it We created you... that We created you from this very soil. And into it We will return you. And you have to go into this very soil, into this grave. And from it We will bring you forth another time. And We will bring you out from this very place." Once, someone asked Amir al-Mu'minin, peace and blessings be upon him, "O Mawla, in the state of prayer, the two prostrations (`sajdahs`) that we perform, what is the reason for them? What is the reason for performing two prostrations?" So he said that, "Have you read the verse of the Holy Qur'an?" He repeated this verse. "From it We created you, and into it We will return you, and from it We will bring you forth another time." So he said that when Allah Ta'ala said, "From it We created you," "I created you from this," this is an indication towards the first prostration. That you... I created you from this soil. "And into it We will return you." "And in this soil, I will merge you." This is towards the second prostration.

And He will bring us into existence with the `Imam` of his time. And, you will be born in the era of whichever `Imam al-Zaman`'s time it was in the previous `karrah`. And the `Da'i` of his era. And in the time of that `Da'i`, in whose time you were born. So they will not respond except to the one who called them. And they will not have `iman`, and they will not respond, except... their... it will be with the very one who had called them in the past. And they will not obey except the one who guided them. And they will not obey anyone except the one who had guided them.

As for increase or decrease...
The same `Imam al-Zaman` and the same `Da'i` in whose time one is born, the `nashist` (era/sitting) which is...
That `nashist` will not sit right according to the ages, no? That `nashist` will not sit right.
No, the point is that now the Imam has progressed in rank, but his `niyabat` (representation) remains, right? His `niyabat` is established. That whoever becomes his `khalifah` (successor) and his `na'ib` (deputy), he will be the Imam. That is the meaning.
No, no, I understood that part. But the era and the `nashist` that will sit, according to age, that `nashist` will not sit right. The era and the age that is, that is different. For everyone, individually, it is different.
Some died at a young age.
Young age, old age, for example, the Imam's age, the `Da'i`'s age, the `Mu'min`'s age, or say, the other `Hudud-e Kiram`'s age, the `nashist` you are talking about sitting... that the Imam, `Da'i`, and `Mu'min`, the `nashist` that is, that `nashist`...
...will not sit right according to the time.
For example, if the `raj'at` happened after a thousand years, then at that time, the Imam would be different. You are saying that in his `niyabat`...
Yes, no, no, in his `niyabat`. Yes, it's possible that... he hasn't clarified it further... that one who... after a thousand years, with whichever Imam he is appearing, then he would be the `khalifah` of that Imam, or in his... the `khilafat` which is established among the Imams, so it could be that.
A statement came earlier that the 1000-year period of returning in the night is a general principle. As the ranks increase, the period of their `raj'at` increases. The one coming in 1000 years, or 2000 years, or 5000 years...
No, that is fixed. For the `Mu'min` as well as for the non-`Mu'min`. That 1000-year period is fixed. That after 1000 years, he has to come again, a second time. That is there. It is fixed. There is no change in it.
The `Ahl al-Nass` and `Ahl al-Nass` will be separate, right? The `du'at`, their... their `raj'at` will be delayed... The `du'at`...
No, the `raj'at` will be delayed, so then what about the `Mu'minin`? The `Mu'minin` will be born in the era of the Imam or `Da'i` with whom they had their `‘ahd` (covenant) or `wilayat` (allegiance). In that same... they will be born in that era. In the previous era, with whom they had `iman`, on his hand...
For the `du'at`, their ranks increase very quickly, and their time is also short. So, their... is their `raj'at` also after 1000 years?
Yes... for the `du'at`... albatta, their `khalasi` (liberation/release) happens very quickly. As it has been said that the Imam, upon him be peace, will not stay for more than three days. Right? His `raj'at` will be in three days, he will not stay longer. Similarly, the `du'at`, 30 years. Their period is stated as 30 years that the `du'at` stay in the grave. And after that... And `Mu'minin` for 300 years. And for `Mu'minin`, it is 300 years. So... this is the situation. But that is about their progress. But the `raj'at` which is... because all things... all things of the `du'at` do not progress. Some things are left behind. Then `Qudrat` brings them again for purification. Similarly for the `Mu'min`.
...and the disgrace...
...the white...
It's going. It's going.
It's coming.
Yes.
And poverty and honor. And in poverty or in the state of honor, and disgrace. And or he is disgraced. So these conditions that happen, and being lowly. And... he does not remain very high, he remains lowly. And intelligence and cleverness. And with extreme intelligence and cleverness one lives life. And eloquence. And he becomes eloquent and articulate. And the lightness... or it is that his ability to speak and his eloquence is not there. The lightness of the intellect, meaning his intellect is weak. And soundness. And the sharpness of the intellect. And other than that, from what this is its meaning. So it is according to the measure of what was from him in the first form. It happens according to the measure of whatever happened with him in the `fudla al-ūlā` (first form). Meaning, his actions also have a reflection here. The reflection of his actions also falls here. And what his two hands sent forth. And what his hands did. And according to the measure of the righteous deeds. And the righteous deeds which he has done, according to their measure also all these things happen. In obedience to the `Hudud`. The righteous deeds he has done in obedience to the `Hudud`. The reward is from the One Worshipped. That his reward is from the One Worshipped, meaning from Allah Ta'ala. And according to the measure of the good he does. And according to the measure of the good deeds he does. To his believing brother. The good deeds he does with his `Mu'min` brother. Of cheerfulness and welcoming. And with cheerfulness and with welcoming, that is, he meets him with happiness. And gentleness of the side. And with extreme humility. And according to his teaching him. And that, whatever he has, he gives it to him, he teaches him. And his guiding him. And he guides him. And his causing him to ascend to the highest of ranks. And he tries to elevate him.

And according to the standing and the advice with sincere intentions. And the way this person stands. And with sincere intentions, the life he leads. And the service in the `Da'wahs` (missions). And in the `Da'wahs`, his... meaning in the `Da'wat-e Hadiyah`, the service he renders. And according to the measure of what the man does to the people of the ranks. And the people of ranks, with them this person does good deeds. The people of high ranks who are worthy, the `Hudud` of the `Da'wah`, with them he does some good deeds. According to the time and the moment. And the pressing need. And as the situations arise. And likewise the land-tax and the ransoms. And in this way, he gives his `fiqāqāt` (ransoms). Through which is salvation. Through these `fiqāqāt`, his salvation happens. And success and arrival at the permanent abode of felicities. And he is successful, and he reaches the abode of permanent felicities, the destination.

And the `fiqāq` is also of great importance. Because what happens with `fiqāq` is that our `nafs nāmiyah` (vegetative soul), `nafs hissiyah` (sensory soul), and `nafs nātiqah` (rational soul), the defects and impurities within them, the flaws, are removed. So, the `fiqāq` is also of great importance. Just as with `zakat` our wealth is purified, and our souls are also purified, so with `fiqāq` too, our these souls—`nāmiyah`, `hissiyah`, and `nātiqah`—are purified.
And the `ahl-o-'ayal` (family) also... they stay together...
Yes...
Their `raj'at`, like everyone's `raj'at` is happening together... The `ahl-o-'ayal` and children also come together...
No, that is not mentioned here. But...

The matter will be the same. Yes, I am telling you. The same who had come with Imam or Dai, they will also come. Yes, but he has not used the word... Yes. They have... he is a *momin* (believer) and is on the *haqq* (truth), their... for all those who are on the *haqq*, the matters that happen with the people of *haqq*... his matter, understand it as... his track becomes completely different. Then with him, how will his accounts be settled? When will he come, and when will he come again? For this, what has been said is, look... for example, for example, there is a person, uh, there is a person who is a blind person, who is a *kafir* (non-believer). Now, this *momin* brother sees that a *kafir* is, uh, crossing the road. So, uh, he... he... considering it a work of *neki* (good deed), has him cross the road. And he doesn't ask him, "Brother, what is your name? What is your religion?" Rather, he did a good deed. From a human perspective. In this way, the various works that keep happening in our world, and such *nekiyan* (good deeds) keep accumulating, so in that, what happens is that, uh... uh, uh, that is, how will his *hisab kitab* (account) be fully settled? Of such good deeds, because uh... uh, the... uh, good work that... people did, its *jaza* (reward) is to be given to the *momin*. So how is the work completed? So it is said that, uh... uh, their, those who are *ghair-e-iman* (without faith), so their, uh, uh, in the second realm, in the other... in the other chamber, uh, their good deeds... sometimes it also happens that they did good deeds for them. So, uh, from the... uh... good deeds of such *ghair-e-momineen* (non-believers), uh, water is made. And after making water, uh, it is brought into his use. It is brought into the use of *wudu* (ablution) and other works, and in this way, the accounts between them are settled. This has also been said. So the matters that happen at the hands of the Imams... how many of the Imam's matters... matters of this kind that happen... the accounts are also fully settled. But this is positive, that okay, he was helped. There are also negative ones. *Qisas* (retribution) also happens. Yes, negative ones also happen. *Wa lakum fil qisasi hayatun ya ulil albab* (And for you in legal retribution there is life, O you of understanding). That your... uh, your... in *qisas* is your life. O intelligent people. Meaning, you will see how great a verse of the Holy Quran it is, *wa lakum fil qisasi hayatun* (and for you in legal retribution there is life). Meaning, for you, in *qisas* is your life. Meaning, the point is that *qisas* is a very large circle, and our life is running on a very small track. A small line is running. So the circle of *qisas* is very large. Meaning, just as Imam Ali Zayn al-Abidin is famous for this, right, that he, uh, you said that, uh, I am afraid of Allah that I may not get caught in small sins. So what example did you give? That, uh, I used to see my mother, that to light a fire, she would use small, small pieces of wood, from which the fire would light. So in that same way, I am afraid that I might be caught by small, small sins. Yes. Although the Imam is *ma'sum* (infallible), they have no mistakes or sins. But that then becomes a *seerah* (way of life/example). The Imams' *seerah* is formed. Allah... [unclear] work... Okay. Uh, another... another... we had a wife of an Imam, whose name was Aziza Bu. So, uh, her grave is also present in the mausoleum of Maulana Shahid Saheb Muqaddas, inside the *rawdah* (tomb). Uh, who is Aziza Bu? Look at this. Aziza Bu bint Qasim Khan. And she, uh, uh, is the *zawjah* (wife) of, uh, Muhammad Ali bin Swaleh Badri. Who is the first *mansoob* (a high-ranking official), who is buried in Ghash. So she is his *zawjah*. And she was so wealthy that she had trade in seven cities. Her trade was in seven... seven cities. And this is from the time of Sayyidna Hibatullah. And she used to send her *fitrah* (religious dues) every year. Every year she would send *fitrah*. And whatever the *fitrah* was prescribed at that time, whatever the *Dai* had prescribed, she would send a thousand times that amount. A thousand times more she would send. And in this way, she, uh, sent it for several years, she sent *fitrah* to Sayyidna Saheb. Yes. And the *mansoob* of that time was told that he should take counsel from her. Yes. Take counsel from her and, uh, keep her with him in consultation. Uh, it was said to Shaykh Yasin. Shaykh Yasin, who was the *mansoob* in that era, he was told that you should not carry out any affairs of the *da'wat* (mission) without the counsel of, uh, Sayyidati Aziza Bu. Yes. Meaning that, on his wife's saying, he went astray. And finally was removed from the *da'wat*. Yes, yes. Removed. Such a great station she had, his wife. Yes. For those who go astray... Amir-ul-Momineen has a saying, that don't look at how those who went astray went astray, but see how those who were saved were saved. This... this is a saying of yours, and in a poem you have said, that see how those who were saved were saved. Pay attention to that. So there are many such incidents. Now look at Dawood bin Fee. Dawood bin Fee was a very... a very great scholar. But, just this... this was that, okay, I am greater in knowledge than Sayyidna Hibatullah Saheb. Yes. The *nass* (designation) for Sayyidna Hibatullah Saheb was performed by whom? Uh, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. Muhammad bin Isma'il, Sayyidna Hibatullah Saheb was his uncle. Right? Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il bin, uh, Muhammad bin Ibrahim bin Muhammad bin Fahd. Sayyidna Ibrahim's son was Isma'il. He had performed *nass* on him, but he passed away in your lifetime, Sayyidna Isma'il did. So you performed *nass* on your grandson, Muhammad bin Isma'il. Who has a very great station. He is the only *Dai* who today... today... among the 54, 55 *Du'at* (pl. of Dai) we have, so he, among those 55 *Du'at*, no one has led prayers outside the Arabian peninsula (*kharij-e-jazirah*). Except Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. You led prayers in Habasha (Abyssinia), by the Imam's command. So that... so Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, uh, had designated his uncle, Sayyidna Hibatullah bin Ibrahim. He had performed *nass*. So, they were... so at that time, this Dawood bin Fee was there in, uh, Najran. So his claim was that I, uh, am greater in knowledge. And then Sayyidna Hibatullah Saheb had, uh, uh, written a letter. He wrote a letter. That here, it should be read out to the *ikhwan* (brethren). But he did not make that letter public, he did not read it out. Then Sayyidna Saheb... from Sayyidna Saheb's side, some *ikhwan* went from there, Arabs came here. Because there was a lot of coming and going. Because in the time of Sayyidna Hibatullah Saheb, there were many migrants. So at that time it was found out that he had not read out the letter at all. So then *itab* (censure) was sent down. And then he later repented. Dawood bin Fee repented. And Sayyidna Saheb also forgave him. But he kept him in the order of the faithful (*iman walon*), he kept him among the *momineen*. And then he was given leave, that you may go back. And while going back, he passed away at a place called Wasit. He passed away on the way. Abdul Majid and others, in the time of the Imams, went astray, how did they go astray? They were very, very... people of great station, these, Abdul Majid, Dawood Qutub. How he went astray, look at that. And the one who... who progressed and was saved was Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. Look, Sayyidna... Sayyidna... Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb, the *nass-e-sharif* (noble designation) he performed, uh, on Sayyidna Ja'far, so, uh, you did not write the name of the *mustawda'* (trustee). You left the name. He kept it blank. That I will clarify it later. And then when he performed the second *nass-e-sharif*, then at that time you wrote the name of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. Yes. And, uh, and Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd's habit was that, uh, he took charge of the *da'wat* and he remained steadfast, your feet did not waver at all. For this reason, you are called *Ghadab fil Da'wat* (Anger/Zeal in the Mission). And for you, look, the *jaza* (reward) that was given is that the way you took care of Sayyidna Ja'far and took care of the *da'wat*... because Sayyidna Ja'far was 11 years old. At the time of the death of Sayyidna Sulayman Saheb. And he taught him, educated him, made him capable. And so, uh, for that the *jaza* given was that until *Qiyamah* (Day of Resurrection), there will be *Du'at* in your progeny. This *jaza* was given. Just like Sayyidna, uh... uh... Sayyidna Ali bin Muhammad bin Walid. Who are our *Dai*, the fifth *Dai* I believe. Uh, Sayyidna... Fifth. Fifth. Okay. Fifth *Dai*. Yes. Uh... [unclear] Yes, [unclear]. They were very great elders. Yes. So, uh, the *jaza* that was given to him was that, uh, Sayyidna, uh... uh, Sayyidna... Sayyidna, who was it? Hatim. Ali bin Hatim. Sayyidna... Sayyidna Hatim... at the time of Hatim, they were there, and they were great scholars, Sayyidna Ali bin Muhammad Walid. And, uh, they were great elders of the *da'wat*. So... and Sayyidna Ali bin Hatim used to study with him. Sayyidna Ali bin Hatim used to study with him. So that era, uh, had many wars. With the Zaydis, there were many wars. So in that, uh, in the wars... so, okay, what happened was that Sayyidna Hatim, *qaddasallahu rawahahu*, performed *nass-e-sharif* on Sayyidna Ali bin Muhammad Walid. So you said that, "Maulana, please reconsider, that why don't you perform *nass* on your own son?" So you said, Sayyidna Hatim said, that "Brother, you know that we didn't get a chance to prepare our son." "For this position. Because all... all our time, our... was spent in wars and its concerns, in preparation, the time passed." So you said that, "You... you should at least test your son." So the *Dai*... what a difficult test he must have taken. They asked very high-level questions to Sayyidna Ali bin Hatim. And they saw that he possesses great substance (*johar*). He gave all the answers. So he was astonished... Sayyidna Hatim bin Ibrahim was left speechless. And he asked Sayyidna Ali bin Muhammad Walid, "How did he get this knowledge?" So you said that, "Maulana, uh, the wars that used to happen, in those wars, we were busy in the wars during the day, but there wasn't a single night... there wasn't a single night that he slept. And I would teach him and he would study." So look, in the war... a *Dai* was prepared on the battlefield. So this state that was there, so because of that state, Sayyidna Hatim then performed *nass*. And then Sayyidna Ali bin Hatim performed *nass* on you, on his teacher. Yes. And the goodwill you showed, with sincerity of intention, so its... its *jaza* given was that your... you and then in your progeny, this... the *Du'at* of Bani Anf continued. So 17 *Du'at* came, just imagine. 17 *Du'at*. And today's *Dai* is approximately the 19th or 20th *Dai*, who is from the progeny of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. Yes. Sayyidna Ahmed is also from your progeny, from Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd's progeny. Sayyidna Ali bin Hasim is also from his progeny. The 20th, 21st *Dai* is he. Sayyidna Taher Saifuddin used to recite this poem a lot for them, "*Antum al-anf wa ghayrukum al-dhanab*" (You are the nose, and others are the tail). Yes, yes. Often he would recite it for them. And why did this name *Anf* (nose) come? *Anf* means nose, right? So Sayyidna, uh... Sayyidna... who... uh, who was it? Maulana, our 18th Imam... Maulana Ma'add al-Mustansir. In the time of Ma'add al-Mustansir, a person used to come from Yemen. He would come from Yemen. And, uh, he had great closeness to the Imam. And he would stay with the Imam for a few days and then return to Yemen. So the... the ministers (*wuzara*) there, so... because you had a kingdom, right? They were ministers. They used to be astonished that we are so close to the Imam, but who is this person who becomes even closer than us to the Imam? And the Imam honors him so much. So one day, they asked the Imam, "Who is this person whom you honor so much... you... you honor him and also show him regard?" So he said that, "*Hadha anf min min Yemen*" (This is a nose from Yemen). "This is the nose of Yemen." So his name was Ali bin Ibrahim. But the name, uh, became Anf on the Imam's saying. Yes. So this... and his progeny, they... they were called Bani Anf. Now, that was in the time of the Imam, the 18th Imam. This happened many... many centuries later, that these became Bani Anf. Yes. And their story is also this, that the Rasulullah was in a *jihad* (battle). And, uh, he was on a high place, on a mountain, you were... with your companions. So you saw that in the oasis, below, in the valley, and there the army of the *kuffar* (unbelievers) is gathering. Slowly, slowly. So you told your companions that, "Look, these *kuffar* are coming to confront us. And, uh, among them, the one who... who is visible on a red camel..." You said, "The one on the red camel who is visible, that person... he is... his name was also mentioned by you, that... [unclear]"... perhaps his name was Walid. "The person you are seeing, he is a very, uh, base and vile person, who has come to confront the Rasul of the time." You said, the Rasul did. "But in his progeny, very righteous people will be born." Look at the vision of the Rasulullah! "But in his progeny, very righteous and pious people will come." So this was recorded. It was recorded in history that the Prophet had said this. Now, an investigation began, that whose progeny is this? So it was found that Ali bin Ibrahim is from his progeny. And then the *Du'at* of Bani Anf are from his progeny. How God gives *jaza*. Yes. But now there is no one from their progeny. Yes, there are two houses. In Najran there are two houses, which belong to Bani Anf. The entire... the entire Bani Anf became Zaydi. Now you can imagine, a family from which 17 *Du'at* have come, the state of their descendants was such that all of them went astray and became Zaydi. So, *Allahu yahdi man yasha' wa yudillu* (Allah guides whom He wills and leads astray [whom He wills]). So this is the absolute truth. And it happened so. And there are two houses in Najran which belong to Bani Anf. The rest, all became Zaydi. During the time of Sayyidna Yusuf, he had made a claim... to be a *Dai*, the last one from Bani Anf... the 17th *Dai* made a claim... after the *Dai*... [unclear]... During the time of Sayyidna Yusuf, when he was appointed as *Dai*, so one from Bani Anf wanted to become the *Dai*, something of that sort happened. No, I don't remember it that way. In your time, there was Sayyidna... In your time there was Sayyidna Muhammad bin Husayn. Yes. Sayyidna Muhammad bin Husayn, in Yusuf's time. So, who is the last *Dai* of Bani Anf. Yes, the last one. The 24th *Dai* of ours is he. The last *Dai* of Bani Anf. Maulana, and another... I can't remember the *Dai*'s name, but they are also from Yemen and they are bankers. And I have seen his name in the *Du'at*, his surname is on it. Okay. I can't remember the name, but it's a very nice name. He is very well-known, that banker. NCB, National Commercial Bank, and this who is, he is among its owners. Okay.

Yes. That is also in *Mahfuz*, it is in *Mahfuz*.

Yes.

Is he there? Has he come? He's come, he's come. It is in *Mahfuz*. It is in *Mahfuz*. It is there. It is in *Mahfuz*. No, no, no, I had mentioned it before that... they are also in *Mahfuz*, aren't they? What's his name, the whole... His name is Ali bin Hanzala al-Mahfuz. Yes, Ali bin Hanzala al-Mahfuz. Ali bin Hanzala is a very high-ranking *da'i*, and the *risalah* (treatise) that he wrote, what is its name? *Simt al-Haqa'iq*. *Simt al-Haqa'iq* and there is another *risalah*, in which there is... There is only one such *risalah* in which the *kuffar* (unbelievers) and *mushrikin* (polytheists) and the enemies of the *Awliya Allah* (friends of God), the punishments that will be given to them are mentioned. Yes. There is only one such *risalah*, I am telling you its name. So Ali bin Hanzala... and Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan, when he would perform the *ratiqah*, he would take the names of all the *du'at*. From Sayyidna Dhu'ayb bin Musa until Sayyidna Jamali Ali bin Husayn al-Makhrami, you would take their names. So you... and you would take their names with their *alqab* (honorific titles). So your *ratiqah*s would be very long. For half an hour you would perform the *ratiqah*. So you... when Sayyidna Ali bin Hanzala's name would come, you would say it like this... Sayyidna Ali bin Hanzala, *Sahib Rutbat al-Fadl* (The Possessor of the Rank of Grace). I remembered that you would use this *laqab*.

So in the book of *Fiqah*... Look, Sayyidna Ibrahim (`alayh al-salam`) Muqaddas, when he went there... to Najran... no, it wasn't Najran then, it was Badr at that time. So, after he reached the *da'i*, after a few days the *da'i* said that you have to pay your *fiqah* (religious offering). You were very, very poor. So you said, "Mawlana, I haven't paid the *fiqah* yet." So he said, "You have to pay the *fiqah*." So you had two... you had only two sets of clothes. One set of clothes you kept for ordinary days, and the other set of clothes you kept for Jumu'ah (Friday) and for special occasions. So you presented that good set of clothes in *fiqah*. To the *da'i*. So you can imagine, this is also a historical thing, that fine clothing was presented as *fiqah* to the *da'i*. And there was only one set of clothes, which you would wash and wear. In such difficulty you lived and moved forward. The way he passed away does not come into... yes... how he passed away... he fell ill. At that time there were also epidemics and such. Yes, it's possible. Similarly, it has also come for Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn Muqaddas that when you would be present with Sayyidna Ali bin Muhsin Shawam, your eyes would be watering, meaning, tears would keep flowing from your eyes and they would be red. So Sayyidna would ask, "Is your health okay?" So you would say, "Yes, it's okay." That you have a cold. In this way, it went on for several days. Later, the *da'i sahib* came to know that where he had his *iqamah* (residence), where he lives, below it live those *Udayni* people, migrants, who cook their food on wood. Because of that, smoke rises, and the smoke rises and is troubling him. But he did not tell Sayyidna sahib that because of the *Udaynis'* cooking, I am having trouble and my eyes are burning. You can imagine this thing. So, for this reason, in the *Nass al-Sharif* (sacred text of designation), you see, the... that... in the *Nass al-Sharif*, your... your *laqab* has been used, that `al-walad... al-walad al-adib... al-walad al-adib dhi al-khulq...` You have said, `al-adab al-allaf al-arib dhi al-khulq al-ajib` (the most well-mannered, intelligent one of wondrous character). My brother of great, great... great qualities, who has very wondrous qualities, is the owner of them. Is the owner of morals. You have said `dhi al-khulq al-ajib`.

`wa al-fawz wa al-falah fi da'im al-sa'adah. Wa amma al-hajj ila Bayt Allah al-Haram, fa kafa bi qawlihi ta'ala 'alayh` (And triumph and success in everlasting bliss. As for the Hajj to the Sacred House of Allah, the saying of the Exalted suffices concerning it). And this, that performing Hajj towards the Sacred House of Allah, for this the saying of Allah is sufficient, that `man arada khayr al-dunya wa al-akhirah` (whoever desires the good of this world and the hereafter), as the Messenger says. The saying of the Messenger is sufficient, `alayh al-salam`, that `man arada khayr al-dunya wa al-akhirah` (whoever desires the good of this world and the hereafter), whoever intends for the good, for the well-being of the world and the hereafter, `fa l-ya'umma hadha al-bayt` (let him intend [to visit] this house). So he should intend to visit this House of Allah. `aman ba'da am` (year after year). One year after the other. `wa al-istighfar bi khalis al-niyyat` (and seeking forgiveness with pure intentions). And he should seek forgiveness with pure intentions. Yes. `wa yamhu al-dhunub` (and it erases the sins), which, with pure intentions, that seeking forgiveness erases the sins completely. It wipes them out. `wa al-sayyi'at` (and the evil deeds). And the evil deeds. `Waffaqana Allah wa iyyaka ayyuha al-akh li mardatih` (May Allah grant us and you, O brother, success in what pleases Him). O brother... Allah, You... and us, grant us success, and you, O brother, for His pleasure. `wa nazamana fi silk majami' al-anwar` (and may He organize us in the thread of the assemblies of lights). And may our organization take place in the assemblies of lights, which are the assemblies of the Imams, the Imams of the religion, in their assemblies of lights, may our organization take place. `wa du'atih` (and His callers). And of their callers. `wa la yu'akhidhna bi mubighat al-dhunub` (and may He not take us to account for the gravest of sins). And may He not hold us accountable for our major sins, may Allah the Exalted. `wa satara 'anna jami' al-'uyub` (and may He conceal from us all faults). And may He conceal all our faults. `wa waffaqana wa saddadna fi jami' al-af'al wa al-aqwal` (and may He grant us success and make us upright in all deeds and words). And grant success, and grant uprightness to us in our all our deeds and words. `bi haqqi Muhammad wa alih khayr al-al` (for the sake of Muhammad and his family, the best of kin). Yes. `khayr al-al`, those who are the best kin.

Mawlana, a question is this, that the *salawat* we read, yes, in that *salawat*, `Allahumma salli 'ala Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala ali Sayyidina Muhammadin kama sallayta wa sallamta wa barakta wa rahimta wa tarahhamta 'ala Ibrahima wa 'ala ali Ibrahim...` (O Allah, send prayers upon our master Muhammad and upon the family of our master Muhammad, just as You sent prayers and peace and blessings and mercy and compassion upon Ibrahim and upon the family of Ibrahim...). So upon Janab Ibrahim (`alayh al-salam`), what kind of *salawat* was sent down? About which we say `kama sallayta wa sallamta 'ala Ibrahim` (just as You sent prayers and peace upon Ibrahim). So is that... is that defined anywhere? No, I... look, for any Imam, the formula of *salawat* has not come. Meaning, for any Imam, this quality was not there. But it happened for Janab Ibrahim. Yes. And... and this is also there, look, that we say that `Muhammadun wa 'Aliyun khayr al-bashar` (Muhammad and Ali are the best of creation). And `wa 'itratuhuma khayr al-'itar` (and their progeny are the best of progenies). And we repeat it, we say it two times. That `Muhammadun wa 'Aliyun khayr al-bashar`. Meaning, Muhammad and Ali who are the best of creation. So for that, what does Sayyidna Ali bin Sulayman say? That... Muhammad and Ali are the best of creation. That Muhammad and Ali, who are the best of creation, so to what does it refer? The first sentence refers to those who... the Imams who have passed in ancient times, up to Sayyidna Hunayd. So from them, these Imam... these... Muhammad and Ali of this time are the best. This time's Muhammad and Ali are the best of creation from those Imams who have passed until *kashf* (unveiling), until Janab Hunayd. And then it is said that `Muhammadun wa 'Aliyun khayr al-bashar wa 'itratuhuma khayr al-'itar`. And that these... this Muhammad and Ali who have appeared, and their progeny which has appeared, are better than those who... the Imams who started from Janab Hunayd, until Janab Hashim, they are better than them. You and your... the Imam of the time. So in this way, we say this thing twice. As far as I don't forget, that on the seventh Imam Muhammad, this also... The seventh Imam who is Muhammad bin Isma'il... On him also comes the other... What thing? This `Muhammadun wa 'Aliyun khayr al-bashar`. `wa 'itratuhuma khayr al-'itar`. Muhammad who is... the one on the 7th Imam... No, that which is *mawsul* (connected) on Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, *salawat Allah 'alayh*, that... we... when we give *shahadah* (testimony of faith). Meaning, `ashhadu anna Muhammadan rasul Allah` (I bear witness that Muhammad is the Messenger of Allah). That is one. And the other testimony we give is for Mawlana Muhammad bin Isma'il. Yes. Who is the *Sahib al-din al-tahir* (Master of the Pure Religion). And look, the one who is the *Sabir* (the seventh), who is the *Sahib al-din al-tahir*, he abrogated the *shari'ah* of Nabi 'Isa. So you also had the authority to abrogate the *shari'ah*. But in the blink of an eye (`chashm-e-zadan`), you abrogated the *shari'ah* and then you also enforced that same *shari'ah*. Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. And you have a great station, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, that due to being the seventh, and you... in this way, there are four verses in Surah *Qul Huwa Allahu Ahad*. Surah *Qul Huwa Allahu Ahad* has four verses, and in one narration there are five verses. `Lam yalid wa lam yulad` is considered two verses. But there are four verses. So in the four verses, those... in the *dawr al-qiyamah*, and in the *dawr al-nabawi* and *dawr al-tayyibi* and *dawr al-qiyamah*, there are four such Imams who are the essence. And they are attributed to the verses of *Qul Huwa Allahu Ahad*. So among them, who is the first? That `Qul huwa Allahu ahad` (Say, He is Allah, the One) indicates Sayyidna... Mawlana Muhammad bin Isma'il. The second, `Allahu al-samad` (Allah, the Eternal Refuge), is Sayyidna al-Tayyib, Mawlana 'Ali bin al-Tayyib. `Lam yalid wa lam yulad` (He neither begets nor is born) is our 35th Imam, who is Sayyidna Ahmad, who is the *waqih* Imam of Sayyidna Sulayman sahib. And the fourth, `wa lam yakun lahu kufuwan ahad` (Nor is there to Him any equivalent). This. This indicates our 49th Imam. Who is the Imam of today. Who is the Imam of today. Whose name has not been revealed. The 49th, whom we call Abu al-Qa'im, the Qa'im of the Imams. So it refers to these four Imams. These four Imams are from the four verses of *Qul Huwa Allahu Ahad*... are attributed. The *Maghrib ki tatawwa`* is also from you, isn't it? Yes, the *Maghrib ki tatawwa`*. For the *Maghrib ki tatawwa`*, therefore Amir al-Mu'minin's saying is, no? That `man lam yatatawwa' fa laysa minna` (Whoever does not perform the *tatawwa'* is not from us). That whoever leaves the *maghrib tatawwa'* is not from us. Meaning, to establish its greatness. And these four *rak'ahs* of the *maghrib tatawwa'* are attributed to you. These four *rak'ahs* are attributed to you. Sayyidna... Mawlana Muhammad bin Isma'il. Yes. And look, in the progeny of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, there were... three who were *muhtajibin* (veiled) and were also *wasi*s. What a station! Meaning, your son Yad'a became, and... Ali became, and Abu Talib became. Not Abu Talib, Ali became. So they all became *muhtajibin*. And then their... *hujjat*s were made, `Abd Allah, Ahmad, and Husayn. These *hujjat*s were made.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Sharf al-Fatihah* to the souls... Sayyidna Mawlana 'Alim bin Hibatullah, *qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara zarihahu wa ja'alana min barakatihi wa a'azatihi wa ta'idatihi wa taqabbal minna al-ifadah wa al-istifadah bi ahsani ma yakun bi jah al-mursalin wa Sayyid al-awwalin wa al-akhirin wa al-Qur'an al-'Azim wa Ahl al-Bayt al-Tayyibin al-Tahirin salawat Allah 'alayhim ajma'in*. *Al-Fatihah*. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alamin. Arrahmanirrahim. Maliki yawmid-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdinas-siratal-mustaqim. Siratal-ladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayril-maghdubi 'alayhim wa lad-dallin.*

[Du'a in Dawat ni Zaban/Gujarati begins, very fast and unclear. Only fragments are discernible.] `...Sayyidna Mawla 'Ali...`

*Al-Fatihah*. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi rabb al-'alamin. Arrahmanirrahim. Maliki yawmid-din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdinas-siratal-mustaqim. Siratal-ladhina an'amta 'alayhim ghayril-maghdubi 'alayhim wa lad-dallin.*

Shukran. Please sit. Shukran. Shukran. Shukran, Mawlana. Yes, just pray. God willing. Shukran sahib, Nazar sahib shukran. Yes, shukran. Yes, *Allah hafiz*.

Yes. Whose phone is it? It's my phone. Yes, I'll take it.

As-salamu alaykum, Bidayat Sahab.

A'udhu billahi min al-shaytan al-rajim. Bi-fadhli Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, wa bihi nasta'in. Wa salla Allahu 'ala rasulihi, Sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan. By the grace of, in the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful, and in Him we seek help. And may Allah's blessings and peace be upon His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and upon the family of Muhammad).

Today is the 21st, and all the du'as of Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq...

Today is the 21st of Rabi' al-Awwal, and when the 21st of any month occurs, then it is among the dates, the seven dates that are such that inauspiciousness (*nahusat*) is gathered in them, it is one of those dates. But you see, this du'a in it is a very rare (*nadir*) du'a. And Imam Ja'far al-Sadiq must have composed these du'as with such great subtleties, keeping this in mind, that you see, look at its tone, that I ask You...

...that you fulfill my needs, etcetera, etcetera, and forgive my sins. And I will not rise from this spot until you do this for me. So what a strange and wondrous tone it is, just as a child is stubborn and says to his father, "Get me this thing," and he makes his father agree in some way or another. So this tone is just like that, that You do this for me, do that, and until you do it, I will not rise from this spot. So this is a very strange and wondrous tone of this du'a which you recited on the 21st date.

It is also the day of the passing of the *awsiya'*. Yes.

Generally, the mistake people make in Ayat al-Kursi, in it I feel that in the beginning of Ayat al-Kursi there is *bi-'urwatillahi al-wuthqa* (by the firmest handle of Allah). Right? In that, there is a lot of mistake. And then the second place is, *li wa lakum wa li-walidayya* (for me and for you and for my parents). And in that, they also say *li wa-likum*. It's not *wa-likum*, it's *wa-lakum*. There is a *zabar* on it. Yes. And *wa man ya'mal mithqala* (and whoever does an atom's weight)... what is it? *shahida Allahu annahu la ilaha illa Huwa wa al-mala'ikatu wa ulu al-'ilm... la ilaha illa Huwa al-'aziz al-hakim. Inna al-dina 'inda Allahi al-islam... wa man ya'mal... su'an* (Allah witnesses that there is no god but He, and the angels and those of knowledge... there is no god but He, the Almighty, the Wise. Indeed, the religion in the sight of Allah is Islam... and whoever does... evil). Yes, it is *wa-man ya'mal*. People read it as *wa-min ya'mal*. But the tone of the Quran Majid is *wa-man ya'mal*. Two places have it written differently. Yes, two places. In the short form it's different, in the long form it's different. Okay. In the Quran Karim it is *wa-man ya'mal*. But in the beginning, it is *wa-man ya'mal*. Yes, *wa-man ya'mal*. It appears in two places. Yes, in two places. But one that comes later is *fa-man ya'mal*. But the one that comes at the beginning is *wa-man ya'mal*. That is not from the Quran Majid. The one in the *wazifa* is part of the du'a. The one that comes at the beginning. But the one that comes later is part of the Quran Majid.

And *wa yastaghfirullah*. No. *Yastaghfirillah*. Look, in both, down below, there is *yastaghfirun*. *Yastaghfarun*.

Another one is that a very big mistake is made. They say *'Aliyyun al-'Azim*, whereas it is *'Aliyya al-'Azim*. That is the biggest mistake. Yes, yes. And it comes in three ways: *'Aliyyun al-'Azim*, *'Aliyya al-'Azim*, and *'Aliyyi al-'Azim*. Yes, in the Quran. But all three have different contexts, in different places. There's *zabar*, *zer*, *pesh*, all three have come.

But here, in Ayat al-Kursi, at the end, when we do *istighfar* seven times, it is *al-'Aliyya al-'Azim*. With a *zabar*. In the *ad'iyah*, they recite *'Aliyyan*. Yes, yes. At the beginning.

And here when we recite seven times, at the end we say *astaghfirullah al-'aliyya al-'azim* (I seek forgiveness from Allah, the Most High, the Most Great), right? So, well, there are several reasons for this. Among them is that in our seven *dawr al-sitr* (cycles of concealment), this existence of ours is the seventh existence. It is the seventh *zuhur* (manifestation). So we are doing *istighfar* for the six *zuhurs* that have passed, and then for the seventh, we do *istighfar* as *al-'aliyya al-'azim*. Yes, these are the seven *istighfars* that we are doing.

Another thing, in a previous *majlis* I heard, someone said that 'Ali, the name of Mawla 'Ali, is in the Kalima Sharif. Well, that is in many places, *'Aliyyan waliyyu Allah* (Ali is the friend of Allah). He said *'Aliyyun* is in Surah Maryam. Yes, yes. *'ala siraatin mustaqeema...* (upon a straight path). It is *'Aliyyan mustaqeema*. So the *zer*, *zabar* were changed, but the intention was directed towards Janab 'Ali.

What is the verse? *Inna hadha siraatu 'aliyyin mustaqeem* (Verily, this is the path of 'Ali, a straight one). This is it. *Sadiqun 'aliyyu... sadiqun 'aliyyu*. No, *siraatun*. So, on that, he said that it is Mawla 'Ali's own testimony that you were *sadiq* (truthful). *Sadiqun 'aliyyu*. He proved it with that. No, the word *sadiq* is not used there. No, *sadiq 'aliyyun* or something like that. I will look at the verse in the Quran Majid. *Wa'j'al li lisana sidqin 'aliyya* (And grant me a tongue of truthfulness, an exalted one). Yes, that is different. But this is *inna hadha siraatu 'aliyyin mustaqeem*. Yes, *sidqin 'aliyya*. On *sidqin 'aliyya*, he did that.

*Al-Su'al al-Sabi'* (The Seventh Question). *Qala ba'du al-mufidina, jazahum Allahu 'anna afdhala al-jaza'* (Some of the benefactors said, may Allah reward them with the best of rewards on our behalf). Seventh question: some benefactors said, may Allah reward them on our behalf with the best reward. *Wa kadhalika fi kitab Kalilah wa Dimnah* (And likewise in the book Kalilah and Dimnah). This is the book, Kalilah wa Dimnah, which became very famous. And it is an Arabic translation of the book Panchatantra. This book was written based on its material. But in this book, Ibn al-Muqaffa' wrote it. Ibn al-Muqaffa' was a great scholar. He wrote several books, *Al-Adab al-Kabir* and *Al-Adab al-Saghir* are his books. And he also wrote this Kalilah wa Dimnah. But in it, since he was a person from after the time of the Prophet, he also wrote some things about the Hudud. So they say that *yawma sa'ala al-natiq* (on the day the Natiq asked), the mention of which will come, that the north (*al-shimal*) is the path (*masir*) and destination (*ma'ad*) for the opponents (*azdad*) of the A'immah and their followers in the west (*fi al-maghrib*). The north... what north? The peripheries. There is the northern pole and the southern pole. Those parts of the earth that are extremely snowy or where there is snow. So in the north, the extreme north, and the extreme south. So in the north, the path (*masir*), the place of return, for whom? For the opponents of the A'immah (*azdad al-a'immah*) and the followers of the opponents of the A'immah. That is, their followers. So their destination will be where? In that north. In the North Pole, their destination will be. Those who are opponents of the A'immah and their followers. *wa fi al-maghrib wa azdad al-anbiya' fi al-junub* (and in the west, and the opponents of the prophets in the south). And their followers, their destination is in the west. And the opponents of the A'immah... uh... the opponents of the Anbiya' (Prophets) are in the south (*al-junub*). *Wa al-tabi'ina bihim fi al-mashriq* (and their followers in the east). And the followers of the opponents of the Anbiya', those people are in the east (*al-mashriq*). This is what has been said. But there is also another explanation that the opponents... the opponents who are opponents of the A'immah and the Hujjat and the Hijabs of the A'immah, they are gathered in *ra's wa dhanab* (head and tail). The *dhanab* (tail) is a more evil place than the *ra's* (head). These are all illusions (*wahmi*), which are beyond the celestial sphere (*nitaq al-qamar*). So... *ra's wa dhanab*, which are also called Rahu and Ketu. In its *dhanab*, in the tail, whose destination is it? The opponents of the A'immah and their Hijabs. And the *ra's* (head), in it are the opponents of the Anbiya' and the *Du'at*. That is their destination. This is what is said.

That is why in the North Pole etc., there is no namaz. Yes.

What is meant by *mashriq* and *maghrib*? Those are places, but they are very cold. The north and south are very cold, but *mashriq* and *maghrib*... The extreme east and extreme west also have this state. There is snow there too. They come within the world's map, don't they? Yes, within the world. That is, within the world, the north, the south, the east, and the west... the extreme points on the boundaries, they are all bad. It's a very widespread misconception. Meaning it's not in our area. Because we also come in the east and west, if you look at it. No, that west and east... End corner, like Tibet etc. Tibet is there, and then Alaska is there. The extreme... Tibet and Alaska, look, east and west. Alaska is up there. No, I mean the extreme, faraway places. Where our east ends or our west ends, or north or south ends. Those places are being discussed. The places that are always covered in snow, where there are six months of day and six months of night. Those kinds of places. That would be up there, north and south, where this situation exists. Six months... Where it snows, but it's not like... Yes, it's there. There are many islands. There are many islands in the Pacific. Around the dateline, on both sides. Yes, on the Japan side and the America side. The islands are very... The islands... Those are extreme. Those are extreme east and west. The volcanic islands of Hawaii and all are there. Yes, they are all in the middle, in the Pacific Ocean.

*Fa-kayfa yunsakhu ma yunsakhu ila hadhihi al-mawadhi' al-nufus wa'l-ajsad?* (So how is that which is transmigrated transmigrated to these places - the souls and the bodies?). So how do the transmigrating things reach there? To these places? And do the souls (*nufus*) of the opponents of the A'immah or Anbiya' reach there, or do their bodies (*ajsad*) reach there? The questioner asks this. And since Sayyidna Abdullah Sahab always used to say that one should rely on the answer, not the question. *A'udhu bika min masiri hadhihi al-hussad* (I seek refuge in You from the destiny of these envious ones). We seek refuge in Allah from the destination, the place, of these envious ones. *Wa a'tasimu bika min fi'li ahl al-fasad* (And I take refuge in You from the actions of the people of corruption). And we seek *'ismah* (protection) that we may remain free from the actions of these people of corruption. *Wa 'ala kam mudadin yulhaquna bi-tilka al-diyar?* (And for how long a period are they attached to those lands?). And for how long do these people go and reach there, the opponents of the A'immah and the opponents of the Anbiya'? *Wa hal al-riyah yuwassiluhum bi-shay'in wa al-riyah shimal wa junub wa dabur wa saba?* (And do the winds connect them with something, and the winds are north, south, west, and east?). And do winds blow that meet them? And are the winds, which are north, south, and the winds named *dabur* and *saba*, available to them? *Fa-tawallaw 'alayya* (So they turned away from me). Just have mercy on me. *Wa a'udhu bika wa bi-Sayyidina Muhammad ibn Isma'il, wa bi-waladika Sayyidina 'Ali ibn Hibatullah min amrin taqulu 'anni atasawwaru ma laysa yu'dhanu li* (And I seek refuge in You and in our master Muhammad ibn Isma'il, and in your son our master 'Ali ibn Hibatullah from a matter about which you would say of me that I imagine what is not permitted for me). And I seek refuge in You and in Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Isma'il that and your son Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Hibatullah, that I may not have such a conception for which I am not given permission. That is, for which I am not permitted. *Wa as'aluka bi-man ya'lu 'alayka ila babika al-'azim wa khalifatika al-karim, an tasturani wa tamhunani ridaka wa sitraka wa 'awnaka wa safhaka* (And I ask You by the one who is exalted before You to Your great door and Your noble successor, that you conceal me and grant me Your pleasure, Your covering, Your help, and Your forgiveness). And I ask you by the one who is exalted before you, up to your great door (*babika al-'azim*) and your noble successor (*khalifatika al-karim*), that you keep my faults concealed, and that I may attain the honor of Your pleasure, and that you keep my affairs concealed, and that Your help may be with me, and that you forgive me. *Wa anta al-mukhyyar fi al-'ata'i aw al-man'* (And you are the one with choice in giving or withholding). And you have the choice to grant or to refuse, in the answer to my question. *Al-jawab 'ala dhalika al-su'al, naqulu bi-'awnillahi al-kabir al-muta'al wa minnati waliyyi al-'asri wa'l-zaman* (The answer to that question, we say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Exalted, and the grace of the Master of the era and time). The answer to your question, we say with the help of Allah, the Great, the Exalted, and with the grace of the *Wali al-'Asr wa'l-Zaman* (Master of the Era and Time), by his blessing. *'Alayhi salawat al-rahim al-rahman, wa ta'idi mawlaya al-'alam al-anwar wa sirrihi al-sari ila lata'if al-suwar* (Upon him be the blessings of the Merciful, the Compassionate, and the support of my master, the luminous banner, and his secret which flows to the subtleties of the forms). And upon him be the blessings of the *Rahim* and *Rahman*. And with the support of my master, the banner, i.e., Hibatullah Sahab, with his support, who is very luminous, and his secret flows towards the subtleties of the forms.
