And these six days of Hajj, it becomes the ruh (spirit) of those days, 'Arafat. That if a person, on the occasion of Hajj, went, and did not have enough time to perform the 'Umrah of *Mutamatti'*, or if... so he reached 'Arafat directly. On the ninth day of the month. So his Hajj is considered done. His Hajj is considered done. That is, if he didn't even go to Mina, still, by reaching 'Arafat, his Hajj is considered done. Such is the 'azmah (greatness) of 'Arafat, because the three maqamat (stations) that there are, namely Mina, and 'Arafat, and Muzdalifah, these three maqamat are upon three 'Uzama' (great ones). Three 'Uzama' are upon them. Meaning, 'Arafat is upon Mawla-i Ka'inat, Mawla Rasulullah Salawatullah 'Alayh. And Mina, or the maqam of Mina, is upon Janab-i 'Ali 'alayhi al-salam. And upon Janab-i Bibi Fatimah, is this Muzdalifah. And in the Dawr al-Satr (Period of Concealment), these three are such personalities that there are no other personalities to compare with them. Yes. These people are the *lubab* (essence). The *lubb al-lubab* (very essence) of the Dawr al-Satr. Mawlana, in 'Arafat, you have said that before sunset... before *ghurub al-shams* (sunset), one must arrive. Yes. One must set foot. Absolutely. Before sunset. Because then the other day starts. The tenth date starts. On the ninth date... one must reach on the ninth date, somehow. Before sunrise... before *ghurub al-aftab* (sunset), his feet must touch the ground. Jabal 'Arafat has its own *sharafah* (nobility). When one reaches there. Yes. Because there the *khuddam* appeared. On Jabal... on Jabal 'Arafat, the *khuddam* appeared, and it is the most noble place. 'Arafat is the most noble place.

*Wa-li-hadha innaha*... So, beneath it is a *sirrun latifun* (a subtle secret). And Yawm 'Arafah, Yawm 'Arafah is the day when *tashruqu*... and this Yawm 'Arafah is when *wahi* (revelation) was continuously descending upon Rasulullah. When you were reaching the plain of 'Arafat, what must have been the state of Rasulullah at that time? So much revelation was descending, so many times, that the she-camel would sit down from its weight. It is said that from the weight of the revelation, the she-camel would repeatedly sit down, and people would try to lift her, but Rasulullah would say that, "Don't lift this, it sits down from the weight of the revelation." So just imagine how pure and clean Rasulullah's blessed heart must have been, that an animal like a she-camel could not bear it. The Quran and its *anwar* (lights) descended upon your heart.

So, on Yawm 'Arafah, *ishraq* (shining/illumination) occurs in that place, of *suwarat* (forms/images), such *suwarat* that "*ma la yakhturu 'ala qalbi basharin*" (what has never occurred to the heart of any human). Such *suwarat-i nayyirah* (luminous forms) manifest and shine upon it. And the *ishraq* that happened, the most intense *ishraq* that happened was due to Ghadeer-i Khumm. Meaning, due to the arrival of Ghadeer. They were going to Ghadeer, right? After that. Yes, after that he went to Ghadeer. That was when... Rasulullah went to Makkah and from Makkah... Why did the *suwarat* descend so intensely on 'Arafat? What was the reason? No, that was... one, the station is very high, and Rasulullah is associated with it. Rasulullah's 'Arafat is associated with him. Mawlana, the noble Kalam was almost complete. Yes, yes. Only the last *surah* was... yes. The one related to the succession. Yes, yes. No, the *wahi* was... No, it was descending. Whatever *wahi* might have been descending, whatever *anwar* or whatever *ahkam* (commands) there might be, whatever was coming from Allah's side, due to that, Rasulullah, 'alayhi al-salam, his she-camel was in that state.

And then Rasulullah, immediately after Hajj, headed for Medina. And what happened was that on the 13th of the month, you arrived... you arrived in Makkah. And there, you did what? You told Janab-i 'A'ishah that Allah has commanded me to make 'Ali my *wasi* (successor) and *khalifah* (caliph), and you should keep this matter a secret. But she went and told her father, Abu Bakr. So Abu Bakr, and then Abu Bakr told 'Umar, and their planning began. To the extent that when Rasulullah reached Ghadeer-i Khumm, and... before reaching Ghadeer-i Khumm, they did what? They were passing through a valley that was an extremely dark valley, passing through it at night. So, some persons, the *munafiqin* (hypocrites), with the intention of attacking Rasulullah, drew their daggers. But what happened was that, by God's power, lightning flashed so brightly, the lightning flashed so forcefully and for so long, the lightning kept flashing, that Rasulullah saw all their faces. Yes. And among those who saw was Hudhayfah al-Yamani. So, Rasulullah said, "Hudhayfah, did you see what I saw?" He said, "Yes, O Rasulullah, I also saw." So he said, "Then I am telling you to keep this a secret and not tell anyone." Therefore, for as long as you lived, for Rasulullah's entire life, you did not tell anyone. Then what happened was that 'Umar ibn al-Khattab would always ask Hudhayfah al-Yamani, "Was I among those who attacked the Prophet?" So Janab-i Hudhayfah al-Yamani would say, "Rasulullah made me swear that I should not say this to anyone." So he would say, "By Allah, I was among them." Yes. So these are great spectacles. Did Rasulullah go to Makkah and then to Medina and then to 'Arafat? Or... no, he came to Makkah, and from Makkah, he went to... Ghadeer-i Khumm. To Ghadeer-i Khumm. He did not go to Medina. From Makkah itself, to Ghadeer-i Khumm...

*Wa-li-hadha tunfara fihi al-dhunub* (And for this reason, sins are dispersed therein). So, due to the shining of these *suwarat*, what happens there? Sins are forgiven, and faults are forgiven, and the *mawayiz* and *balaya* (calamities and tribulations) are uncovered, meaning, they are finished. *Wa-fi al-mawaqifi quma'irun tahirah wa-asrarun zahirah* (And in the stopping places are pure hidden treasures and manifest secrets). And in the *mawaqif*, there are pure *quma'ir*... *mawaqif*, meaning... Mina, Muzdalifah, 'Arafat, these are the *mawaqif*. So, at the stations of the *mawaqif*, there are such *khama'ir* that are very pure, and there are secrets that are manifest. Meaning, *tayy al-ard* (folding of the earth). There are things like *tayy al-ard*. *Wa-li-hadha*... Therefore, *hurrima 'ala al-anam qatl al-ashjar* (it was forbidden for mankind to kill the trees). Therefore, for this reason, it has been forbidden to cut the shrubs there. *Fi tilka al-jiha al-lati nahat fiha al-asrar* (in those regions which contained the secrets). In those stations, where the secrets are hidden. *Fa-hiya mujawiratun li-tilka al-khama'ir al-kaminah* (for they are neighbours to those hidden treasures). These, the... *ashjar* (trees), they have taken refuge with those hidden *khama'ir*. They are neighbours, and they are mixed with the *dhubat al-batinah* (hidden essences). *Wa-hadhihi qatratun min bihar 'ilm Mawla-i al-'Alam* (And this is a drop from the oceans of the knowledge of the Master of the World). And this is a drop. A drop from the oceans of the knowledge of Mawla-i al-'Alam. *Wa-sadaqatun min jumlati sadaqatihi al-mursalah 'ala 'abdihi al-mawjud al-mujid lahu min al-'adam* (And a charity from his many charities, sent upon his servant, the existent, whom He brought into existence from nothingness). That is... it is a current that flows towards your servant, such that you created him from nothingness. Meaning, I was nothing at all. I was nothing, and from there He made me, and made me capable of giving answers and clarifying realities. This is about his *walid* (father). Yes. *Jazahu Allahu 'an imami wa-sayyidi afdala ma jaz al-du'at* (May Allah reward him on behalf of my Imam and my master with the best of what the supplicants are rewarded). May Allah reward you. On behalf of my Imam and my Imam's master, with the best of what the supplicants are rewarded. *Wa-razaqna minhu maqbul al-shafa'at* (And grant us sustenance from him of accepted intercession). And grant us sustenance from his accepted intercessions. *Wa-sunna 'an al-bayni al-ladhi ulqiya 'alayka bi-haqqi mawlaya* (And protect us from the veil which was cast upon you by the right of my Mawla). And may we be protected from that veil which has been cast upon you, by the right of my Mawla, which is established upon you. *Wa-man adha'a shay'an min hadhihi al-asrar fa-qad tabawwa'a maq'adahu min al-nar* (And whoever broadcasts anything of these secrets, has certainly prepared his seat in the Fire). And whoever has spread any of these secrets, his abode has been made in the Fire. *Wa-bari'un min al-'Aziz al-Ghaffar* (And he is disavowed by the Mighty, the Forgiving). And he will be disavowed by the 'Aziz al-Ghaffar. Yes. Disavowed means he will be disassociated, Allah will be free of him. Allah... Allah... as in Surah al-Bara'ah, what is it in... *bara'atun min Allahi*... *bezari* (displeasure). Displeasure is taken, that Allah and His Messenger's displeasure is with the polytheists. This is what it means. To become independent/free.

The eleventh question, from Sayyidna Amin al-Mu'taman Sulayman ibn Hasan. The title of Amin al-Mu'taman was given to him by Sayyidna Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah. Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah had Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan write two *nussus* (texts of appointment). So, the first *nass* is very long, and it was written by the hand of Da'ud ibn Qutb. It is a very long *nass*. And in it, at the end, he has written that, "*ana turabu na'layhi*" (I am the dust of his sandals). I, Da'ud ibn Qutb, am the dust of the feet of Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan. And the second *nass*, you had dictated to Sayyidi Raj, three days before your passing. In 970 Hijri. So this *nass* that is from Sayyidna Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah's first *nass*, in it he has used various titles. You have used 28 titles. So Sayyidna Ja'far... Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fa'd says that these 28 titles are indicating the *darajat* (ranks) of Sayyidna Sulayman, that the 28 high titles... you were the جامع (possessor) of them. And that the 28 *huruf* (letters) of the *Kalimah al-Shahadah*... that when the 28 *huruf*... now, after this *nass*, if any person recites the *kalimah* "La ilaha illa Allah, Muhammadun Rasulullah, 'Aliyyun Waliyullah," then it is necessary for him to bear another *shahadah* (testimony). And that *shahadah* is for Sulayman ibn Hasan. Otherwise, he does not remain within the circle of Imamah. Yes. He has established this, Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fa'd al-'Alawi. When Sayyidna Da'ud passed away, his two sons were with him, right? Yes. Da'ud ibn Qutb did not... The two sons... yes, yes. *Ahl-i akhbar*. Yes, there are many proofs that Sayyidna Da'ud ibn 'Ajabshah's two sons, Ibrahim and Ahmad, are with him. And his *ahliyah* (wife) was also there. Yes, his wife was also with him.

Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Fa'd has offered magnificent praise for Sayyidna Sulayman Sahib. In one place, he said that Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan, for the Imam of his time, was at the *maqam-i nasut* (station of humanity). He was at the *maqam-i nasut*. The Imam was at the *maqam-i nasut*. And for his *abna'-i jins* (fellow believers), the circle that is his, Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan's, the circle of believers, for the *abna'-i jins*, you were at the *maqam-i lahut* (station of divinity). You have said this. This is a very profound statement.

*Wa-iqamatuhu li-waladihi fi ardhi... bi'rayn* (And his establishing of his son in the land of the two wells). And you, Sayyidna Sulayman ibn Hasan, established your son Ja'far, at... the two wells, when you were going somewhere, at those two wells you established him. What is this incident? I don't know it. Sometimes incidents have been abbreviated. So how did that matter happen? How was the matter of Mawla Sulayman ibn Hasan accomplished? Please clarify that. But I don't have this incident in my mind. And Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Isma'il, *qaddasa Allahu ruhahu*. Who is this Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Isma'il? The one who made Sayyidna Hibatullah the Da'i. Yes. Meaning, he was the *taq* (arch/support) of Sayyidna Hibatullah. Yes, yes. Sayyidna... Sayyidna Isma'il ibn... Muhammad ibn Isma'il made Sayyidna Hibatullah the Da'i, he is the one. *Fa-qad sami'tu annahu salla jama'atan fi tarfi bilad al-habashah* (For I have heard that he led a group in prayer in the region of the lands of Abyssinia). I have heard that they led a congregation in prayer, a group, who were from the lands of Habashah. In the lands of Habashah, he led the prayer. *Fa-la-'amri la-qad faqa man mada* (So by my life, he certainly surpassed those who have passed). That, by Allah's oath, he surpassed whatever had passed before. Meaning, the *du'at* had never done such a thing, to lead prayer outside their own three islands. So, *fa-haqqiq li-'abdika al-faqir* (so investigate for your humble servant). Who led it? Sayyidna Muhammad ibn Isma'il. Yes. So, investigate for your humble servant... *...fi hadhihi al-mas'alah, bi-haqqi fulan, bi-haqqi fulan* (this matter, by the right of so-and-so, by the right of so-and-so). So, I have asked you many questions, by the right of so-and-so, by the right of so-and-so. *Wa-lakin akhafu an tatla'a 'ala da'fi 'aqli* (But I fear that you will become aware of the weakness of my intellect). And I fear that you will discover the weakness of my intellect. *Wa-qad 'udtu bi-dhambi wa-dhilli wa-dhalili fa-ya harrun* (And I have returned with my sin, my abasement, and my lowliness, so O protector). I am laden with my sins and my slips. Mawlana, the three islands you are mentioning, so in the first place, Iran and other places all had prayer... No, Iran... In Iran, the Da'i of Iran used to lead the prayer. But the three... meaning, the Da'i of Yemen was for three islands, and outside those three islands, he never led the prayer. Habashah and others all had their own... their own Da'is would lead. Mawlana, you said that he doesn't pray in Madinah Munawwarah... No, no. That is the island of Sham. The island of Sham. That is the island of Sham. Therefore, no Da'i prays there. When we went to the holy places, Yahya was there, the one from there... He said you cannot lead the prayer. Yes, yes. The Da'i of Yemen is superior to the *du'at* of all the islands. Yes. So he should have permission... Yes, despite that, he restricts himself to the three islands, that he only leads prayer there, in Hind, Sindh, and Yemen. Yes. Now, Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Husayn al-Makrami went to London, but you did not lead the prayer there. It is outside the island. He cannot lead prayer outside the island. Neither can he lead prayer outside the island, nor in other islands. I heard, these are the words of the Da'i of Khurasan, who had come to see Da'ud. In your presence... you did not lead the prayer. No. The Imam led. Yes, Sayyidna Da'ud said, "I have been commanded by the Imam's side that I should pray behind you." Yes. Sayyidna Sulayman Sahib said, "You should lead us in prayer." And Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Sulayman says that when your meeting took place, Sayyidna Sulayman would tell them stories of Yemen, the conditions there, and he would tell stories of Khurasan. Yes. The *mansub* (appointee) of Yemen is a *mansub* that is superior to all the islands' *manasib* (appointments). It is superior. The *mansub* who is in Najran, he is superior to all the *manasib*. Even if he is appointed later? Even if he is appointed later, he is superior. His nobility and station are very high. Because he is, *bi-l-quwwah* (by potential), the successor of the Da'i. Even if the Da'i has not performed *nass* (appointment), he has the eligibility to become the khalifah of the Da'i. Yes. If there is one *mansub* or two, whatever it may be. The one who is the primary *mansub*, when there are many *manasib*, the primary *mansub*, he... his is the talk. That he is superior to the *manasib* of the other islands.

*Wa-qad ahattat bi dhambi wa-yuhallu bi qarbi* (And my sin has encompassed me, and it is resolved by my closeness). Pardon my sins, and my debt that is there, resolve that as well. *Wa-lakin aqulu ya Allahu ya man azhara al-jamila wa-satara al-qabiha* (But I say, O Allah, O He who makes the beautiful manifest and conceals the ugly). But I say that, O Allah, you who have manifested my beauty and concealed my ugliness... This is actually the du'a of Husayn ibn 'Ali. *Fa-mnun 'alayya bi-ihsanika wa-fadlika* (So favor me with Your favor and Your grace). So bestow Your favor upon me with Your favor and Your grace. *Fa-kayfa akhafu wa-ihsanuka awsa'u wa-fadluka 'indi ajma'u* (So how can I fear, when Your favor is most vast, and Your grace is most complete with me). And how can I fear? Your favor is very vast, and Your grace is very vast. And the blessings you have bestowed, are gathered with me. Meaning, blessings have been continuously bestowed upon you. So, how can I fear any matter? *Wa-la ziltu fi hifzi al-da'i wa-husni ri'ayatihi* (And I have never ceased to be in the protection of the Da'i and his good care). And I have always remained in the protection of Allah and in the good care of Allah, meaning in Allah's protection. By the right of Sayyidna Muhammad and his pure and noble family. The answer to that question, with the help of Allah, the Great and Exalted, and the bounty of the Wali of the Age, may the blessings of the Merciful be upon him. And with the support of my Mawla, the Most Luminous Secret and the Great Ocean, and with the support of Mawla Hibatullah and his shining proof... When there is *ifadah* and *istifadah* (imparting and receiving benefit), when the beneficial and the beneficiary sit together, then the *suwar* (souls) of the believers who have passed away, and who had a longing for knowledge, then that longing for knowledge... they cannot act. Meaning, the souls of those who have passed away, their prayers are left, so they themselves cannot pray, because they do not have a body. They do not have a body. So they are helpless. So as much work as there is to be done, like performing Hajj, performing Jihad, keeping fasts, whatever there is, it is a matter of life. It is related to the body. But one thing is that the quest for knowledge, that remains. Meaning, even after passing away, the state of seeking knowledge remains. And when such *majalis* (gatherings) are held, and such sessions are held, in which there are the words of the Awliya' Allah, like the *majalis* are happening, the words of the Awliya' Allah are being discussed, or there is *ifadah* and *istifadah*, there they gather. And the questioner who asks a question, in reality, it is not the questioner who is asking, but through his tongue, they ask the question. Yes. It's as if the *hadd-i 'ali* (high-ranking hudud) is making him ask the question. Yes. So this is the state. Now, once it so happened that Dhul-Ma'ali, in the old Ziyarah Muqaddasah, due to his health, because of a pain in his leg, you started giving *asbaq* (lessons) in your house, in the mosque, after the Fajr prayer. So... then what happened was that... in fact, what happened was that... the incident that he brought up, that the mother of Hadrat Ziyarah Sahib Muqaddas, a woman possessed her. Meaning, a shadow fell upon her. And she was possessed. So then you, Hadrat Ziyarah Sahib Muqaddas, with your knowledge... you knew all these things... so through your knowledge, when you questioned her, it was discovered that she was a Bohra woman who had possessed her. And you, you scolded her, but she said, "No, I have not come with any bad intention, but I want to enter your noble *ahl* (community)." So you administered the *'ahd al-sharif* (noble covenant) to her. So after taking the *'ahd al-sharif*, she left. But after some time, she came back again. So you really scolded her, saying, "You promised that I would leave and not come back." So she said, "I have not come by myself, but I have been sent." "I have been sent." And the one who sent me is... "Your Mawla and mine, Fardali Sahib, has sent me." And Fardali Sahib Muqaddas had already passed away. So, "My Mawla and yours, Fardali Sahib Muqaddas, has sent me with some instructions." So, what is it? So that woman said, "The first thing is that he has told you that the *asbaq* you conduct in the Ziyah Manzil, you should conduct those in the mosque, because the *suwarat* that attend, they have a longing for... they have a longing to hear it." And you should conduct the *asbaq* in the mosque. And second, what did he say? He said that Ja'far 'Ali Sahib, who is... who is the forefather of Maqsood bhai and Safdar bhai who are in Hyderabad now... Ja'far 'Ali Sahib. So, he is building a house. He is building his own house. And to supervise the workers, he sits under a wall, for shade. So tell him that that wall is about to fall. And he should not sit there. Yes. So, convey this. And the third thing he said... The third thing... some other thing was said by him... that Hadrat Fardali Sahib has said that... these two things were there and... I don't remember the third thing. Does anyone remember it, Muslim? You don't remember? Okay. I don't remember the third part of our story. Yes. A third thing was also said. One was about the Ziyah Manzil, and the other was about Ja'far bhai's house. And... I don't remember the third thing. So you see how these things happen, and how spirituality enters into matters. Just as in a *majlis*, Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan was delivering a sermon. And the *ikhwan* were present, just as we have *majalis*. So you, in the middle of the *majlis*, Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman would stand up. And he would say, "Mawla, you have graced my *majlis* with your presence, and it is a great honor for me that you have come to my *majlis*. And pray for me." So people would be astonished. And then he would sit down. This happened several times. So people said, "What is happening?" He said, "Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan has come." Yes. So such strange and wonderful things have happened. Now, when the Da'udis filed a case... in the time of Sayyidna 'Ali. In the time of Sayyidna 'Ali, they filed a case. So Sayyidna 'Ali and Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik had to appear. They had to appear. So... so the one... the day before the appearance in court, Sayyidna Ja'far would appear in Sayyidna 'Ali Sahib's dream. And Sayyidna Ja'far would say, "Look, tomorrow, this judge will ask you this question. And you should give this answer." So it was like this. So the next day they would go, and the judge would ask that very question, and then you would give that answer. So these are also strange and wonderful things that have happened. And one day they went, and they were very worried. The people... about government matters. So the Quran... a person appeared. A person appeared, and he recited a verse of the Quran to both of them, to Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman and to Sayyidna Hasan Khan bin 'Abd al-Malik. And he said, "*La takhafa, inna... in... anā ma'akumā*" (Do not fear, for... for I am with you both). There is a verse like this in the Quran. That you... you should not fear, we... we are with you both. Or, "I am with you both." So, with this... it's a verse of the Quran. So with this, they were consoled. So such states also are established. Sayyidna Sahib was performing tawaf of the Ka'bah, and Mawlana Fardali and... yes, yes, yes. "Wa shauqah ya shauqah" (O my longing, O my longing). Yes, yes. That is also an incident. Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad. Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad, he was in Ta'if. Because in Ta'if he had been put under... like a house arrest. And he would come to Baitullah from Ta'if. And when he stayed in Makkah, he would perform seven *tawaf* every day, without fail. Yes. And when he was performing *tawaf*, or was doing *tawaf* at some point, he raised both hands and said, "Wa shauqah, wa shauqah." So the companions who were with you, the *ikhwan* and *hudud* who were there, they later asked, "O Mawla, what was this state of yours? We have never seen such a state in you." So he said, "I was seeing three *suwar* (souls). One was Sayyidi Fardali Sahib Muqaddas. And Sayyidi Ziyali Sahib Muqaddas, and Sayyidna Ghulam Husayn Sahib Muqaddas's souls, I was seeing them doing *tawaf*. And they... they were doing *tawaf* with great speed. But I, with my body, was doing *tawaf*, so I could not do *tawaf* with their speed. So my longing (*shauq*) increased at seeing this, that I could not perform *tawaf* like them, because of my body." So this was a state that happened. And one time, a person told me in Najran that he was sitting and reciting, and Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il was also reciting. They were in front of me. He used to recite a lot, Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il. And he would recite the Quran very fast. So, he said, "I was reciting, and I saw that a person came and presented his hand to Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il." Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il was sitting. He presented his hand before you. So as soon as you saw him, Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il kissed his hand. And immediately stood up. And that person vanished. He said this. So it's possible that it was Imam 'Ali 'alayhi al-salam himself. You, Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il, kissed his hand, and he immediately... that person vanished.

So, with your support, Mawla-i 'Alam, and with his... his secret that is magnificent, from the affair of Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, Sulayman-i Humam, Sulayman al-Imam. The story of Sulayman-i Humam is that he, in the *jazirah* (island), is Sulayman, and one of his forms is established in Hadramawt, whose name is Humam. This is something that has not happened with anyone else. Because the Imam of your time is the 35th Imam. And he is the seventh (*sabi'*) of his cycle (*haftah*). And not only this, but he is included among those four Imams, Sayyidna Ahmad bin Muhammad, whose greatness and station are very high. And you have combined two cycles (*haftahs*). You are the possessor of 14 Imams. So, due to these circumstances, your station is very high. And when the Imam's station is high, the Da'i's station also becomes high. This... *Wa-iqamatuhu li-waladihi Sayyidna Ja'far* (And his establishing of his son, Sayyidna Ja'far)... And you established your son, in Bi'rayn, so... that is among the people. *Wa-dhalika kama fa'ala asluhu al-'azim fi al-dawr al-qadim* (And that is as his great forefather did in the ancient period). So this matter happened just as in the ancient period, when Sulayman bin Hasan and Ja'far bin Sulayman appeared, you did it then. So now too, you have done the same. What that incident is, I don't know. *Allahu yahdi man yasha'u ila siratin mustaqim* (Allah guides whom He wills to a straight path). Allah guides whomever He wishes to the straight path. *Wa-aydhan lamma ittasalat maddatu imamatihi ilayhi* (Also, when the substance of his Imamah connected to him)... The substance of Imamah... when the Imam's confirmations connected with you, *dhakara qurbanahu wa-mathubatahu* (he mentioned his sacrifice and his offering). That you should present your sacrifice. So... *Wa-amma sallatu Sayyidna Badruddin* (And as for the prayer of Sayyidna Badruddin)... *'ala qudsuhu fi lina kama dhakarta fi ghayri hadhihi al-jazirah al-yamaniyyah* (may his soul be sanctified, in a land as you mentioned, in other than this Yemeni island). And the prayer led by Sayyidna Badruddin, that is, Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il, his leading of the prayer, as you have mentioned, in other than the island of Yemen, that island was not Yemen's. That is, the prayer he led in Habashah, so that was... *fa-dhalika li-'izzati rutbatihi al-bari'ah* (so that was due to his sublime and great rank). So that matter happened because your rank is very sublime and great among the people. *Wa-li-ihtijabi imamihi 'anhu bi-suratihi al-mu'azzamah* (And because his Imam became veiled from him with his magnificent form). The Imam became veiled from you. With his magnificent form in the island, he became veiled from you. *Wal-idhnu lahu mu'adhdhinan lahu bi-l-salah fi ghayri jazirah al-mukkaramah* (And the permission for him to lead the prayer in other than the honored island). And the Imam gave you permission to lead the prayer. In a non-... yes, outside the honored island. Meaning, outside the island of Yemen. *Wa-lahu sha'nun 'azimun* (And his is a great station). And your station is very great. *Wa-khatbun jasimun* (And a weighty matter). And your condition is very great... weighty. *Wa-ma hajara min ardhihi wa-baladihi illa li-'adhami sha'nihi* (And he did not migrate from his land and his country except for the greatness of his station). And you did not migrate from your land and your country, but for the greatness of your station. You were where? There. In Yemen. In Yemen, you were in Sa'fan. But you migrated to Najran. And you went to Habuna. So that... you did not do that, but... your great and magnificent state, because of that, you migrated. *Wa-quwwatihi al-ruhaniyyah wa-li-ihya'i marasim al-da'wah* (And for the strength of his spiritual power and to revive the rituals of the Da'wah). And due to your spiritual powers, and the rituals of the Da'wah, the secrets of the Da'wah, to revive them. Did he have a personal meeting with the Imam? Like that? No, the Imam's luminous form became veiled from him. I don't know how that happens. There is a treatise by Kamaluddin, Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad himself, "Al-Hijab wal-Muhtajib" (The Veil and the Veiled). It is a small treatise, but it is very difficult. It is not understood what he wants to say. So... these states are beyond comprehension. That how a veiled one... is veiled by a veil, because the veil is actually...

He is going for a test. I am stopping the lesson here. It's alright, it's alright, Father. I am finishing now. It's alright. He has arrived. Let's recite the Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. O Allah...

Maulana, may I?

Attendees, I am stopping the lesson here. A doctor has arrived, for whose sake I have to get up. I will recite the Fatihah, and we will continue the lesson tomorrow, Insha'Allah. Yes. We will continue tomorrow. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. We present the Fatihah, as a gift and a connection, pure and pleasing, to the soul of Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Hibatullah, may Allah sanctify his soul and illuminate his resting place, and may his blessings, lights, and supports be multiplied for us, and may He accept from us this imparting and receiving of benefit in the best possible way. By the right of Sayyidna Muhammad and the family of Muhammad, peace and blessings be upon them. Al-Fatihah. Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi Rabb al-'alamin, al-Rahman al-Rahim, Maliki Yawm al-Din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat al-ladhina an'amta 'alayhim, ghayr al-maghdubi 'alayhim wa-la al-dallin. Amin. *Wa-bi-niyyati Sayyidi Mawlaya al-Mansub al-Mutlaq fi al-Jazirah al-Hindiyyah al-Sharifah ila al-Faqih al-Nabil, al-'Alim al-Jalil al-Hajj al-Sharafi Usamauddin Ghulam Husayn ibn Sayyid al-Mansub al-Muqaddas Nasrullah ibn Hibatullah ibn al-Mansub al-Muqaddas Fathullah. Tawwala wa-abqa'ahu Allahu Ta'ala, yahfazuhu wa-yahmihi wa-yubqihi bi-tayyibi 'ayshihi min sharri jami' al-amrad wal-asqam, min sharri al-awja' wal-a'lam, min sharri al-ghilal wal-hijam, min sharri al-nufus al-khabisat wal-fusufat, min al-taqdidat wal-tathrisat, wa-bi'ati al-munazarat, wa-yudda wa-yazid fi nasrihi wa-yahfazuhu fi ma'uni 'amrihi min 'aybi al-nawafis wal-nuqsan, wa-yahmihi fi al-hifazat al-rabbaniyyah al-shahaniyyah, wa-tawala Allahu fi tuli hayatihi, wa-af'ali wa-kalamihi wa-darajatihi, wa-yashkurana iyyakum fi sa'ati da'awatihi, wa-yadhkuruna iyyakum fi anfa'i hadaratihi, bi-jahi Sayyid al-Mursalin, Sayyidina Mawlana al-Mubayyin wa-Ahl Baytihi al-Tayyibin al-Tahirin, salawatullahi wa-salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in. Al-Fatihah.* Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. Al-hamdu lillahi Rabb al-'alamin, al-Rahman al-Rahim, Maliki Yawm al-Din. Iyyaka na'budu wa iyyaka nasta'in. Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim. Sirat al-ladhina an'amta 'alayhim, ghayr al-maghdubi 'alayhim wa-la al-dallin. Amin. Thank you. Fi amanillah.

<!-- lecture 13 -->

...that the matter of praying in the ghayr jazirah was being discussed. In Habashah. So its mention was happening. This is page 62 in my book. Which book do you have? 28. Twenty-eight. Okay. Is there a da'wah in Habashah? Yes, there is a da'wah in Habashah. There is a da'wah. But they are... they do not give allegiance to our Da'i. No. It was up to the time of Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il... up to Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan. Ali bin Ali related that Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan had sent me to Habashah to establish a *qiyam* there. Yes. So after returning from there, I... perhaps in Shahwallah, he had established the *qiyam*. A strange thing I learned about my [unclear]... I had not mentioned it to you. That... one Sajjad, right? There was a Sajjad, who had said that... that... Syedi sahib... had said that... your father is... In the last phase of his life, he will be the *Da'i* of Habashah. The Prophet had not given permission. In the ghayr jazirah. Aah... and he was given permission for the prayer in the ghayr jazirah of Makkah al-Mukarramah. `Wa lahu sha'nun 'adhim wa maqamun jasim` (And he has a great station and a lofty rank). Your station is very great, and your rank is very high. `Wa ma hajar min ardihi wa baldatihi illa li-'adhm shanihi` (And he did not migrate from his land and his city except for the greatness of his station). And you did not perform *hijrah* from your land, that is, where you... ah... were born, from that land, you did not perform *hijrah*, and from your city, except that it was for your *'adhm* (greatness) and *shan* (station). The people of Satan, when... you presented a *nadhraanah*. So that... that *hijrah* which was done, was because of your great station. Yes. And after that, after many years, Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan went... to those places where... who had performed *hijrah*, Sayyidna Mahmud bin Isma'il. And he then stayed there for several months. He also built a *masjid* there, Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan. And he also built a *Dar al-Hijrah* there. And then later, in our time, Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan built a magnificent *Dar al-Hijrah* there, and it was a very splendid *masjid*. Yes. So then we went there, so... I said that... we were sitting in the *masjid*. I said that, "Malik, if you permit, I will... recite about Sayyidna Sulayman sahib." He said, "Yes." I recited the *qasidah*. So... it was a very long... *majlis*... they kept listening, Sayyidna sahib, with concentration. `Wa li-ihya'i marasim al-da'wah` (And for the revival of the ceremonies of the Da'wah). And you... the *marasim al-da'wah* (ceremonies/documents of the Da'wah), meaning those written documents... and the *nusus* (texts) that were there, you... reissued them, or brought them to notice. The things you did in the Da'wah, so you did all these things. `Wa fi'iluhu bi-al-wa'il...` (and his action by the [unclear]...) Until they were... they were lying in the abyss. But you brought them into action, meaning, you made people aware that, look, these have been written in the past, these documents have been made, that... these writings, the *du'at* have ordained. Apart from the books. So, you manifested that. `Wa li-istikhraj al-sawa' al-kamil min bilad al-Yaman` (And for extracting the complete secrets from the lands of Yemen). And the things that were hidden in the lands of Yemen... `bilad al-Yaman`. You brought it to light. `min al-sadis...` from the sixth... `shurur al-sharifah` (the noble evils/secrets). And you brought to light those `shurur al-sharifah`. `Wa law tulina al-kalam...` (And if we were to prolong the speech...). And if we... speak on this, it will become very long. The ranks of the *du'at* are... `rutab al-'aliyah` (high ranks). And the... ranks of the *du'at* are, and besides that, the ranks that are, the high ranks that are, they are different. `fi 'uluww al-darajat` (in the loftiness of degrees). They... they are different. Meaning, it is not one type of rank. Because of their lofty degrees. `Li-anna kull al-martabah fi dhatiha sab' rutab` (Because every rank in itself has seven ranks). `Yu-khafi min al-asrar al-thaniyah` (hidden from the second secrets). And every rank... this is divided into seven ranks. Meaning... the... high... secrets there are, according to that. Meaning, if there is a *da'i*, then one *da'i* is divided into seven ranks. And among them is one who is in the highest rank among the seven ranks. Sometimes it happens that a *da'i* is in the highest rank among the seven ranks. `Inda dhawi al-adhan al-saqiyah` (With those of piercing intellect). in the `dhawi al-adhan al-saqiyah`. `Wa minhum man yakunu fi awsatihi` (And among them is one who is in its middle). Some *du'at* are in the middle of these ranks. `Tilka al-rutab al-madhkurah` (Those mentioned ranks). Yes. ...whose mention has been made. And among them is one who is in the lowest rank. And some *du'at* are in its lowest rank. Yes. And from them... --And who decides which rank you are in? --That is the Imam of the time. The Imam of the time decides which *da'i* is in which rank. And then its declaration happens later. The succeeding *du'at* say that he... --After their passing away. --Yes? After their passing away? --After their passing away. Then the succeeding *du'at* say that he was in that rank. And his rank was this. Its... its mention is in our books. Some *du'at* have also expressed their ranks. That this one was in such-and-such rank, or that one was in such-and-such rank. Then it is said that among them, there are also such *du'at* who... because of their past *sawabit*... *hasanat* (good deeds), the good deeds that were theirs, because of that... `ila adna rutab al-hujjiyah` (to the lowest of the ranks of the Hujjiyah). They come to the lowest rank of the *rutbah* of *hujjiyah*. Meaning, they leave the rank of *da'i* and... they come to the lowest rank of *hujjat*. `Ma fawqaha min rutab al-'aliyah` (What is above it from the high ranks). *'Aliyah*. *'Aliyah*. *'Aliyah*. Yes. Or... they go to a rank even higher than that. Meaning, the *hujjat*... the rank of *hujjat* also has several ranks. So they go into that as well. Yes. A *hujjat* is of two types. One is the rank above the *du'at*, that is the rank of *hujjat*. And one *hujjat* is also what the son of the Imam is called. The son of the Imam is also called a *hujjat*. --Today, are our *da'i*s... this... --Yes, yes. All the *du'at* who came after Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, until the *Qiyamah* (Resurrection), they were all established in the rank of *hujjat*. Meaning, their ranks were elevated. And they were established in the rank of *hujjat*. This is their *shan* (station). Meaning, the *qa'imiyyat*... the *du'at* of the era of the *qa'im*... Because there are three eras, right? The *dawr al-nabawi* (prophetic era), the *dawr al-tayyibi* (Tayyibi era), and the *dawr al-qa'imi* (Qa'imi era). The *dawr al-nabawi* lasted until when? Until Mawlana Ali al-Tayyib. And then after Mawlana Ali al-Tayyib, the *dawr al-tayyibi* happened. Until Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, then the *dawr al-qa'imi* began. When 1000 years were completed, the *dawr al-qa'imi* began. `Wa minhum man yakunu mustawda'an` (And among them is one who is a trustee/temporary). `Wa minhum man yakunu mustawda'an` (And among them is one who is a trustee/temporary). Now look, this is a special point. And among those *du'at*, there is also this, that some are of the rank of *mustawda'* (temporary). And... and... `wa fi al-mustawda'... wa minhum man yakunu mustaqarran fi al-istida'` (And in the temporary... and among them is one who is permanent in the trusteeship). And some are *mustaqarr* (permanent) in the *istida'*. *Istida'* is the rank of *du'at*, it is a *mustawda'* rank. Yes. And the rank of the *a'immah* is *mustaqarr*. So, in *istida'*, some are *mustawda'in* (temporary). And some *du'at*... become *mustaqarrin* (permanent). Yes. --Can you pinpoint? --Like... like it was said that Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, despite being so learned, and so much greatness he had, that an example was set, in your presences, the *shan* that Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan had, despite that, you were in the rank of *mustawda'in*. And Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman, he was *mustaqarr*. And other *du'at* came after him. Yes. Well, not all *du'at* have been mentioned, but some *du'at* became *mustaqarr*. But among them, from them, the first was Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman, who was in the rank of *mustaqarr*. Meaning, in the *istida'iyyat*, he became *mustaqarr*. Yes. They are all *mustawda'in*, but in the *istida'iyyat*, they became *mustaqarr*. So Sayyidna Ja'far's rank became even higher. He reached the station of *hujjat*, *hujjiyat*. Yes. --Like the Nabi's was. --Yes. The Nabi's station is something else. But... --Mawlana, the definition of *mustaqarr* is... --That... that is... --The succession of son after father, that's what we... --No, not like that. It's not like that. --Not here. --No, it's not like that. That... that is only in the case of the Imams. The lineage of son from father is among the Imams. But this is that... he... *mustawda'* and *mustaqarr* means... it... its explanation has not come, but their... their rank... from *istida'* goes towards *istiqrar* (permanence). And it's possible that they become masters of special *majami'* (collections/sciences). And... special *majami'*... they become masters of... Allah knows best. --From *mustawda'* to *mustaqarr* is higher, that is what we understand from this passage. --Yes, yes. It could be that they become masters of *majami'*, special ones. That special support is obtained by them from the Imam. Just like Sayyidna Ja'far also received. When... Sayyidna Ja'far... went to Janad, right? In... in a place in Yemen, there is Janad. So when you went to the *masjid* there, you... you were on a journey. With you... with you was Sayyidna Ibrahim. Your... who were... brother. Meaning... the son of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawz. And... with you was Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad. Your sister's son. And there was a Sindhi brother with you. On the journey. So, right at the time of *Maghrib* (sunset prayer), when the time for *Maghrib* was very near, Sayyidna Ja'far said, "Look, at this moment, the *tajalli* (manifestation) of the Imam is upon me." "And at this moment, whatever *du'a* (supplication) you make, it will be accepted." So Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad bin Fawz made this *du'a*, that my... my son should be a *Da'i*. And the lineage of *du'at* should continue in his progeny until the *Qiyamah* (Resurrection). He made this *du'a*. And Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad said that I should obtain such knowledge that neither the first ones have received nor the last ones have received. So then he wrote such a magnificent book... [unclear]... *Badi'at al-Bayan*, that book is... the *Hashiyah* on the *Anwar*, that book is... and other books there are, in which... the *du'at* are astonished and perplexed. The *du'at mustaqarrin* (permanent *du'at*) are astonished and perplexed. And when they mention his name, they call him Sayyidna Husam. While he was not *mustaqarr*. You... you were... you remained *ghayr munfarid* (non-absolute). But they mention your name with great respect and call you Sayyidna Husam. And then that... the Sindhi brother who was there, his handwriting was very poor. So he said that my handwriting should become very good. So, just as the capacity was, so were the supplications. So, his... his handwriting also became very good. This... this narration we were reading. In *hijrat* (migration). In the time of Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan, between *Maghrib* and *'Isha*, the book of *Sirah* of the *du'at* used to be read. So, incidents of the *du'at*'s *sirah* (biography) would come up. So one time, Sayyidna sahib... this... this incident passed. So... Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan looked at Ghulam and said, "Ghulam, you people are always worried about your handwriting." At that time, Syedi Abdullah had arrived from Hindustan, Hadrat, Hadrat 'Abdullah sahib had arrived. So... so Sayyidna sahib said that, "Mawlana, he was not a Hindi, he was a Sindhi." Sayyidna sahib passed away. --Syedna Husayn bin Ahmad Dhul Jaddayn you are talking about? --Yes. Dhul Jaddayn. --Yes, yes. --No, no. The one who is in Baroda, Sayyidna, he is not Dhul-Jaddayn. He... Ali bin Husayn... Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Husayn bin Dhul-Jaddayn... Husayn bin Ahmad bin Dhul-Jaddayn. Meaning, Sayyidna Husayn bin Ahmad who... whose story is being told with Sayyidna Ja'far, he is his grandson. Here... he was sent in the time of Sayyidna Hibatullah, Ali bin Husayn. So... this is actually a misunderstanding that the descendant of Dhul-Jaddayn is here. But the Dhul-Jaddayn who is... it's not his son, it's his grandson who is here. He passed away at 45 years of age. And... --in Baroda. --Yes. And he was a great scholar, that person who is buried here. He was a very great scholar. Yes. --That is why Sayyidna Sulayman sahib always... --Yes. --referred to Sayyidna Ja'far as "my mawla". --Yes. `Al-Mawla`. "Mawla Ja'far, Mawla Ja'far". What a great station it must be that the father... the *abyat* (couplets) that Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan wrote, in which he has described the *shan* (station) of Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman, there... there the word he has used is "Mawla Ja'far, Mawla Ja'far". You can imagine. Then Sayyidna Ja'far, `qaddas Allahu ruhahu` (may Allah sanctify his soul), after your passing, transcribed it. Your handwriting was very strange and unusual, completely unique. So you transcribed it, and you removed the word "Mawla". Yes. When Sayyidna Sulayman sahib passed away, Sayyidna Ja'far was 11 years old. Despite that, at such a young age, if he had been much older, and you called him "Mawla", then the matter would be understandable. But at such a young age, you... it was said that, "my Mawla Ja'far". But I... Sayyidna Husayn... Ali bin Husayn... the one you just mentioned, he is the one whose *rawdah* (tomb) is... --Yes, Hadrat Shakir sahib is buried there. --Yes, yes, he is the one. --...is called the *rawdah* of Dhul-Jaddayn. --Yes. And in it... Hadrat Shakir sahib Muqaddas is also there, and 'Azizah Bu is also there. On one side is Sayyidna... Hadrat Shakir sahib Muqaddas, and on the other side is 'Azizah Bu. Yes. --Yesterday was his day, wasn't it? --Yes? --Yesterday was his day. --Yes, yesterday was his day. Yes. And... when Sayyidna Ali bin Husayn al-Makrami came to Hindustan, Hadrat Shakir sahib Muqaddas was alive. So you requested, in Baroda, that "Mawlana, my age has advanced, and `kullu nafsin dha'iqat al-mawt` (every soul will taste death), so I want to... select the place for my burial." "My... where I will be buried." So you... Syedi sahib... he looked at all four sides, and then said, "No, your place should be here." Meaning, where you... you are present, right? On one side... there are four corners, right? So in one corner, you are buried, in one corner is 'Azizah Bu. 'Azizah Bu was already there. You said that... "this is your place." Yes. --Hadrat Shakir sahib had chosen another spot? --Yes, you had thought of being near Dhul-Jaddayn. Yes. You said, "This is the place." Mawlana, about Sayyidna Sulayman sahib, what you mentioned about Sayyidna Ja'far, so at the birth, what you... was that not an indication? That your... that your *Da'i* has been born. Meaning, greater than you... because at that time you had not become the *Da'i*, Sayyidna Sulayman sahib. Yes, yes. So was that not an indication that... Yes. At that time, it was the era of Sayyidna Da'ud bin 'Ajab Shah, right? Who passed away in 999 Hijri. Yes. So, before that is the matter, that when Sayyidna... when you became pregnant and... Khadijah bint Nuh. And you... you were in the womb, Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman. So... you... you said to Janab Khadijah bint Nuh, "Your son is very blessed." So she said, "Mawla, who knows whether it will be a boy or a girl?" So you said, "It will be a boy, and he will be the *Da'i* of his time." And you also said that "you will serve his *da'wah* greatly." The believer sees with the light of Allah. That... this happened and... the second thing that happened was that... you... you... what did you say? For Khadijah bint Nuh... what did you say? That... `hiya... hiya...` ...you used the term `rijal al-da'wah` (men of the Da'wah). That she... is wearing an Isai [Christian/worldly] dress, but in reality, she is from the `rijal al-da'wah`. She is one of the `rijal al-da'wah`. And Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fawz, `'ala al-usul`... he mentioned to Sayyidna Ja'far... when the *intiqal* (passing) had happened. So he mentioned that, "I think that... for the prosperity and well-being of the *da'wah*, I should... marry the wife of Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan." Sayyidna Ja'far related this to his mother. So... she said, "I will ponder this matter." So then after that, after some time, you agreed. And... then after that, she was such a blessed wife that... you can imagine, that from Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, Sayyidna... Sayyidna... Yusuf bin Sulayman was born. From Sayyidna Ja'far bin... Sayyidna Yusuf bin Sulayman was born. Then Sayyidna 'Abd al-Tayyib was born, and Sayyidna Ali was born. And all four became *du'at*. Sayyidna Yusuf and Sayyidna 'Abd al-Tayyib were *du'at ghayr munfarid* (non-absolute *du'at*). And these two, Sayyidna Ja'far and Sayyidna Ali sahib, were *munfarid du'at*. And Sayyidna 'Abd al-Tayyib, he... he wrote many books. Your... your handwriting was very... very fast and very clear. He wrote several books. Yes. And then, when he married her, Mawlana Khadijah bint Nuh, so Sayyidna Ibrahim sahib was born. And... other children were also born. Yes. And Sayyidna Ibrahim sahib... when... in Yemen, he was... the Zaidi king had imprisoned him. So for 14 years, you remained in prison. And for 14 years, Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan used to say that... "Ghulam, the love of you Hindustanis for the *du'at* is astonishing and strange." He said. "That our grandfather, when he was in prison, a Hindustani brother was there who... would perform *Hajj* for him." In those days, performing *Hajj* was a very difficult task. With great difficulties, *Hajj* was performed. So, "our... for our grandfather, a Hindustani brother would perform *Hajj* every year." So what a great matter of greatness it is. --The lineage of *du'at* continued through Sayyidna Ibrahim. --Yes. --None of his brothers... --No, none of his brothers. Sayyidna Ibrahim sahib had appointed his son... Isma'il. But he passed away in his lifetime. Then after that, you appointed Sayyidna Muhammad bin Isma'il. Your grandson. On your grandson, you appointed. Yes. `kama kana sayakun` (as it was, so it will be). Just as it has happened, it will happen. `wa ma 'ulima sayu'lam` (and what was known will be known). And just as it has been known, it will certainly be known. `wa ma taht al-shams shay'un bi-jadid` (and there is nothing new under the sun). And under the sun, there is no new thing. I am taking the covenant of Allah, which is very firm. `wa mithaquhu al-shadid` (and His covenant is strong). And His covenant is very strong. `alayka ayyuha al-akh al-sa'id` (upon you, O fortunate brother). Upon you, O my fortunate brother, in protecting the secrets of the `al-'Aliy al-Majid` (the High, the Glorious). And this noble covenant is that you will protect the secrets of the `al-'Aliy al-Majid`. Yes. `wa man nadhara ila hadha al-kitab bi-ghayr idhn al-da'i al-majid` (and whoever looks at this book without the permission of the glorious Da'i). And whoever looks at this book without the permission of the Da'i al-Majid. `aw mimman lahu 'adw al-'ilm` (or from one who has the reins of knowledge). Or without his permission, in whose hand is the rein of knowledge. Meaning... making decisions, or the decisions in the *da'wah*, without his permission. `wa al-nadhr al-sadid` (and with sound insight). And... who has a special insight, meaning he is high in rank. Without... without his permission, if you look, then `fa-qad bari'a min al-Mubdi' al-Mu'id` (then he is free from the Originator, the Restorer). So... so he will be free from `Mubdi' al-Mu'id`. `wa qawluhu...` And his saying... `man adha'a sirrana adhaqahu Allah bard al-hadid` (whoever spreads our secret, Allah will make him taste the coldness of iron). "Whoever... broadcasts our secret, Allah will make him taste the punishment of `bard al-hadid` (cold iron)." Meaning, the cold iron's... he will be made to taste the punishment of cold iron. Cold iron's punishment is what? That until... his punishment... he... will be in Qutb Shumali or Qutb Janubi. Meaning... the... which... the three punishments, right? One is `adhab al-adna` (the lowest punishment), `adhab al-awsat` (the middle punishment), and `adhab al-akbar` (the greatest punishment). So... he... he will go into the `adhab al-awsat`. Yes. --Meaning, without permission, if he reads and... --Yes, without permission, if he reads and... And now we... are concluding this book... Alhamdulillah... we are concluding this book. `al-'Aliy al-Wahhab` (the High, the Bestower)... that... on Allah the `al-'Aliy al-Wahhab`... with His praise, and `al-salat wa al-salam 'ala sayyidina Muhammad...` (blessings and peace be upon our master Muhammad...) `al-muntaqil min al-aslab al-tahirah ila al-arham al-mutahharah` (the one who transferred from the pure loins to the purified wombs). And... may Allah send blessings and send peace upon our master Muhammad, who... kept transferring from the pure loins of the pure forefathers... to... the pure and holy wombs. Meaning, you... none of your ancestors committed shirk. Meaning, they were all pure and clean... because they were the *a'immah* of their time. So how could they commit shirk, or how could they... all of them were righteous and extremely... the essence of the universe, those people were. The *a'immah* of the time. `wa 'ala akhihi wa wasiyyihi` (and upon his brother and his legatee). And then, the two sons of Janab Ibrahim who were, one was Isma'il and the other was Ishaq. So you are from the progeny of Janab Isma'il. You are not from the progeny of Janab Ishaq. And from the progeny of Janab Isma'il, came the *a'immah*. *Mustaqarr*. Yes. And from the progeny of Janab Ishaq came the *anbiya'* (prophets). You see, Janab Isma'il's station is very high, that with Janab Ibrahim, in the building of the Ka'bah, in placing the Hajar al-Aswad, you were also with him. And in raising the walls, you were also with Janab Ibrahim. And... until... the genesis of Zamzam also happened through your feet. Everything is there, but your rank is less than your son's. Your son, Qaydar, right? Qaydar. His rank is less than Qaydar's. Because Qaydar is the seventh Imam. Yes. `wa sallallahu 'alayhi wa 'ala akhihi wa wasiyyihi` (and may Allah send blessings upon him and upon his brother and his legatee). `al-mu'in al-a'immah al-ghurr` (the helper of the brilliant Imams)... `bi-wasitat al-pak` (through the medium of the Pure one)... by the medium of the Pure one... `fi al-sirr al-mustasirr` (in the hidden secret). `wa 'ala al-a'immah al-akhyar, majami' al-anwar` (and upon the righteous Imams, the collections of lights). And the `a'immah al-akhyar`, who are the `majami' al-anwar`. `wa 'ala Imam al-zaman, al-natiq, al-mubdi'...` (and upon the Imam of the time, the speaker, the originator...) `...fi hal al-wiladah bi-'alaniyah` (in the state of birth, openly). This... the *Mubdi'*... the *Mubdi'*... in the state of *wiladah* (birth), spoke with you openly. This they are saying. `wa al-radi' fi hadd al-tufuliyyah ila awan al-zuhur bi-nuraniyyah` (and the infant at the stage of childhood until the time of manifestation with luminosity). And... you progressed from the stage of childhood... towards... until your manifestation happened with *nuraniyyah* (luminosity). `wa sallam 'alayhim` (and peace be upon them). --Whom is he talking about? --He is talking about the Imam of the time. That the Imam of the time, who... whose rank is such that `Mubdi' Ta'ala` (the Exalted Originator) spoke with him in the state of *wiladah*. Openly. And he progressed... from the state of childhood... towards... until... your manifestation happened. Meaning, you grew up. With *nuraniyyah*. `wa sallam 'alayhim` (and peace be upon them). And may blessings be upon them, all of them. And upon their forefathers, and upon their pure progeny, and upon their *du'at*, who are `al-mayamin` (the blessed), noble, and honorable. `al-na'ibina manabahum ila qiyam yawm al-din` (their representatives until the establishment of the Day of Judgment). --The mention of *Mubdi' Ta'ala*, would that not be something high? Meaning, 'Aql al-Awwal or 'Aql al-Thani, if mentioned, it would make sense, but *Mubdi' Ta'ala* is... --Yes. Now, by *Mubdi' Ta'ala*, it's possible that the term *Mubdi'* has been used, but it refers to the ranks below it. Because *Mubdi' Ta'ala*... it so happened that... --Nowhere else has the mention of *Mubdi' Ta'ala* come. --Yes. I mean, by *Mubdi' Ta'ala*, that is not the intent, the one who brought existence from non-existence. That is not the intent. Because his... he... what he did, he brought existence from non-existence and it finished. After that, he has no other action. He has no other action. Except that he... I mean, until it was asked that... will he do this action again? Bringing existence from non-existence? So it was said that he... will never do such a thing. He... will not do it again... bringing existence from non-existence. So he said, "What? Does he not have the power?" He said, "No, despite having the power, he will not do this." Yes. And... about him... I mean, since when he is, what he is, what he is doing, and why he did it, so the *du'at* have no answer for that. Except this, that he did it, so it was his *tafaddul* (grace) and *mihrabani* (kindness) that he did such a thing. That's all the answer there is. Now this is that 'Aql al-Awwal (the First Intellect), the *ma'rifah* (gnosis) that he had of `Mubdi' Ta'ala`, from that, 'Aql al-Awwal became a *hijab* (veil), a *bab* (gate), and a continuous and flowing knowledge was established upon it, which can never stop. For this reason, it is said that `la ta'khudhuhu sinatun wa la nawm` (neither slumber nor sleep overtakes him). That he neither feels sleepiness nor is he overcome by slumber, 'Aql al-Awwal. Meaning, in sleepiness, what happens? A person becomes partially heedless. And in sleep, he becomes completely heedless. Neither is he partially heedless, 'Aql al-Awwal, nor is he completely heedless. Yes. And `al-Hayy al-Qayyum` (the Living, the Self-Sustaining) he has become. Meaning, alive and life-giving. This. Until it was said that `al-Hayy al-Qayyum`... which is... one of Allah's... names, it is among the high ones. And in this `al-Hayy al-Qayyum`, the *Ism al-A'dham* (the Greatest Name) is present. Some such... words are there, that it is said that, like... `Dhu al-Jalal wa al-Ikram` (Possessor of Majesty and Honor). `Ya Dha al-Jalal wa al-Ikram` (O Possessor of Majesty and Honor). In this, the *Ism al-A'dham* is present. Or `al-Hayy al-Qayyum`, which is the *tasbih* for Sunday. So... `al-Hayy al-Qayyum`... this points towards 'Aql al-Awwal, that he is alive and life-giving. He. And then what did he do? That the substance and support that he received, 'Aql al-Awwal, from `Mubdi' Ta'ala`, so he gave to 'Aql al-Thani (the Second Intellect), meaning al-Munba'ith al-Awwal (the First Emanation), he gave him only two and a half percent of it. He gave two and a half percent of it. Like it is in *zakat*. So whatever things were gathered with him, he gave a two and a half percent portion of it. Because if he had given the whole thing, his existence would have burned up. So... and in the same way, al-Munba'ith al-Awwal gave to the ranks below him, two and a half percent of his. So, as it went on and on, this one... and that... which is the Qur'an al-Majid, it is said that the Tawrat, Injil, Zabur, and `Suhuf Ibrahim` (Scrolls of Abraham), their descent was from 'Ashir (the Tenth Intellect). It was from the side of 'Ashir. But Kalamullah (the Word of Allah)... Kalamullah's descent was from 'Aql al-Awwal. It was from 'Aql al-Awwal. And it was from 'Aql al-Awwal, and... but... it came into the form of words at that time when... that *nur* (light) descended upon the heart of the Rasul, and the Rasul said that this is the Kalam of Allah. Until then, it was in the spiritual realm. It was the Kalam of Allah, but it was not in this state that it could come into the form of words. When did it come into the form of words? When it was sent down upon the heart of the Rasul. So... it could come into the form of words. And then you see this miracle as well, that upon the heart of the Rasulullah, the *anwar* (lights) of the Qur'an al-Majid descended, and Rasulullah is manifesting it as the Kalam of Allah. And from the same tongue, from the same blessed mouth, you are speaking your own words and also speaking the Kalam of Allah. And no one has the audacity to say that this... I mean, what great care Rasulullah must have taken that he kept his own tone for his speech completely separate. So that it did not seem to people that... that... it is the Qur'an al-Majid. Or the Qur'an al-Majid's tone was not such that it seemed like the speech of the Rasul. Completely separate... was his speech. Until it is... which is in... inside it, in Yasin, it is, that `wa ma 'allamnahu al-shi'ra wa ma yanbaghi lah` (And We did not teach him poetry, nor is it befitting for him). That "We did not teach him poetry." Allah Ta'ala says. "And it was not appropriate for him either that he should become a poet." So Rasulullah, one time... one... one line of a couplet he said. And the second line, he broke its meter. Meaning, it did not become a poem. I don't remember it, that... in what way he said something... and the second line, if he had said it in the same way, then you would have become a poet. But you made the second line such that its meter was broken. --The Nabi's Kalam was of one type, Allah's Kalam used to come. Second, the Nabi's own Kalam. --Yes. --The *Qudsi* was separate from that. --Yes. The *Hadith Qudsi* which was... --Yes. --That is separate. --*Hadith Qudsi* means that Kalam which Allah has said, but which has not become a part of the Qur'an al-Majid. Yes. --For what reason? Some... --Yes. Some scriptures there were, like... the *Sahifat al-Qaf* is there. And other... scriptures, which are mentioned in the Sahihain. So they... are not part of the Qur'an al-Majid. But they came to the Rasul from the side of Allah. Yes. --The Qur'an al-Majid that exists today, is it only for this era, or will it continue in the *dawr al-kashf* (era of unveiling) and beyond? --No, the Qur'an al-Majid will always remain. Yes. Meaning... its *anwar* (lights) will always remain. Yes. Meaning... from the beginning to the end, the *anwar* of the Qur'an al-Sharif are there, but the form in which the Qur'an al-Sharif is with us today... that same Qur'an al-Sharif... Yes, it has been said that the Qur'an al-Majid will also remain in the *dawr al-kashf*. It will remain in the *dawr al-kashf*. And we... from the Qur'an al-Majid, the... the meanings we derive from its words, they... the people of the *dawr al-kashf* will derive much better meanings from it. That's right. The Qur'an al-Sharif came after the Rasul. So... from the time of Adam, the Qur'an al-Sharif must have existed. Even before that, the same Qur'an al-Sharif would have existed. Meaning, there wouldn't be another Qur'an al-Sharif, this same Qur'an would exist. --No, it was not revealed in their time. This... in the time of the Nabi, the Qur'an al-Majid came. --A great era had passed. --Yes. --The Qur'an al-Sharif was present. --Yes. It was hidden. It was hidden. I mean, before that... for 6000 years, the Qur'an al-Majid was hidden. And other words of Allah were being revealed. Yes. Adam was also taught various principles of the *shari'ah*. On... on Janab Nuh also some things were revealed. On Janab Ibrahim, the `Suhuf Ibrahim` (Scrolls of Abraham) and Musa have been mentioned. Yes. In the future *kashf*, then... in the future *kashf*, and then the `fatrah` (intervening period) will come. So at that time, then... then the same cycle will happen. Then a Rasul will come, then after that... --So at that time, like the Qur'an al-Sharif will not remain... --No, the Qur'an al-Majid will remain. The ascendancy of the Qur'an al-Majid will not end. Until it is said that the *Da'imayn* (the two Eternals)... *Da'im al-Awwal* (the First Eternal) and *Da'im al-Thani* (the Second Eternal), they will also remain. But... *Da'im al-Awwal*... will not have importance. Because they are the *ibadat* (acts of worship). And *Da'im al-Thani* will remain in its place. And this is also such a strange thing, that after the revelation of the Qur'an al-Majid, what the believers are reading, what the *du'at* are reading, what the *hujjat* are reading, what... the `awliya' Allah` (friends of Allah) are reading, everyone is reading. So from it, the meanings they are deriving, the... the people, according to their own capacity, according to their scholarly stature, the meanings they are deriving from the Qur'an al-Majid, so that circle that is, of their meanings, of deriving, of understanding, the entire Ummah's circle that is, the Ummah of Islam's circle, Rasul 'alayhissalam is aware of that circle. The Rasul knows that these states will come into the minds of people. So no one can say this, that "the meaning of the Qur'an al-Majid that I have derived today is so novel that no one has thought of it before." Before that, it is present in the mind of the Rasul. Yes. And that... he does not have this distinction, that he alone has thought of it, and his... way of thinking is a unique way. No one can say this. Rather, it is present in the mental sphere of the Rasul. Mawlana, the Qur'an al-Sharif, which is... it is... for our... after falling, the mistakes we made, to correct that, we have been given this Kalam-i Sharif. So at that time, you say that... 'Aql al-Awwal also had this *nur* (light). So at that time, nothing had happened. No mistakes were made, nothing had happened. So this... this charter was made for us, that now we have come into the world, we have made mistakes, so we have to correct our own selves. So at that time, this matter was not there. No, no. The *anwar* (lights) of the Qur'an are separate, and this Qur'an is separate. It is a part of the *anwar* of the Qur'an. Yes. The *anwar* of the Qur'an were obtained by everyone. Just as the capacities were, of the `uqul` (intellects) of the spiritual world, and those `uqul` who... who were... who were given permanence, before 'Ashir, the people who are, they... have become *mustaqarr*, they have been given permanence. They have come into the world of `uqul`. Okay. And those who after that, are in `tafawwutayn` (the two differentiations), in `labu nida` (the game of the call), so they are also noble people. But that state has not been achieved which... the `uqul` before 'Ashir had. But everyone is receiving its *anwar*. They are receiving the *anwar* of the Qur'an. According to their capacity. Now it is not necessary that this incident itself should happen, these... the incidents that are described in the Qur'an al-Majid. They have no concern with that. It doesn't match with them. But the *anwar* of the Qur'an, they are being obtained. --These *anwar* are different, those *anwar* are different. --No, the *anwar*... the Quranic *anwar*... have very... statuses. The Quranic *anwar* have layers. Layers. Layers. Just as it is said that the Ka'bah... yes, sit down. --...two batteries... --Yes... let it be. --How many are left? Fifteen, fifteen. Fifteen have passed, fifty. --Should I cancel? What should I do? --Cancel. --Another fifteen are left. --It will be done. Yes. So, like there is the Ka'bah. So when we kiss the Hajar al-Aswad, so Hajar al-Aswad gives witness. To our kissing. So the one who kisses is a hypocrite, a believer, a *da'i*, and an Imam. As their capacities are, Hajar al-Aswad changes its layers. Yes. So... just as Imam Ali Zayn al-'Abidin came to kiss. So at that time, Farazdaq was present. So Farazdaq... what did he say? That... O Hisham bin 'Abd al-Malik... the king was also there. And the king didn't get a chance to get close. There was such a crowd. And when Sayyidna Ali bin Husayn... Imam Zayn al-'Abidin arrived, the crowd completely parted, the path... and you, with great ease, came near the Hajar al-Aswad and you gave it a kiss. So he, sarcastically, said this, he knew... Hisham bin 'Abd al-Malik knew that this is Ali Zayn al-'Abidin alayhissalam. But sarcastically and tauntingly, he said this thing... that... "who is this, for whom there is so much reverence and people are giving?" So you said that... "You do not know? And you think that he is kissing the Hajar al-Aswad? Rather, the Hajar al-Aswad rose and kissed his hand." He said this thing. So that... as the person is, so are its layers. And what is Hajar al-Aswad? That it... from the *fudulat* (emanations) of the *hujub* (veils) of 'Ayn al-'Adhimah (the Great Eye/Source)... are its *fudulat*. From the *hujub* of 'Ayn al-'Adhimah, which happened, its *fudulat*... came in this form, in the form of Hajar al-Aswad. So it has... layers. It has spiritual layers. A *da'i* comes, so he receives his share. A believer comes, he receives his share. Yes. Now... now see, this is also what a thing it is, that one time, 'Umar ibn al-Khattab was performing *tawaf* (circumambulation). And Amir al-Mu'minin was present there. So 'Umar ibn al-Khattab said... said to his companion that... "This stone can neither benefit nor harm." "This is a stone, it... it can benefit, nor can it harm." A small gathering was there, at that time it was not a crowd. So Amir al-Mu'minin was present there. So... and he said this, that "I have seen the master... the master of the universe... kissing it. Therefore, I am giving it a kiss. But I know it is a stone." "I am kissing in imitation of the Rasul. But my belief is that it is a stone, it can neither give benefit nor harm." So at that time, Amir al-Mu'minin said, "`yanfa'u wa yadurr, hadha al-hajar yanfa'u wa yadurr` (it benefits and it harms, this stone benefits and harms)." "This is the stone that gives harm and also gives benefit." So it is said that by this statement of Amir al-Mu'minin, a movement occurred in the spiritual world. And the movement was such that... from the *huddatayn* (the two limits/vile places)... from the *huddatayn*, two vile forms attached themselves to... to... 'Umar ibn al-Khattab. So... as soon as he said it, two vile forms attached themselves to him from the *huddatayn*. The *huddatayn* is where the vile thoughts of the opponents of the Awliya' Allah are gathered. Their form is not there. The vile thoughts are gathered. Two of those vile thoughts attached to him, and his animosity increased a lot towards the Ahl al-Bayt. `Allahumma ikfina wa da'ina shurur al-addadi wa ma admaruhu min al-kayd wa al-makruh wa al-'inad` (O Allah, suffice us and our Da'i against the evil of the opponents and what they harbor of plots, of disliked things, and of enmity). And O Allah, protect us, and our *da'i*... from the evils of the opponents and what they harbor of plots and... of disliked things and of enmity. `wa-nsurna 'alayhim nasran 'aziza` (and grant us a mighty victory over them). And... grant us victory over them, a mighty victory. Yes. `wa-j'alna fi hizn...` and... `wa-j'alna fi hiznin min himayatika hariza` (and place us in a protection from Your safeguarded protection). And make us... in a protection... from Your protection, which is... a protection which is very strong. Strong means it is very hard. `wa-khtim lana al-jami'a bi-khayr al-khitam` (and seal for us all a good conclusion). And seal our end, of all of us, with a good conclusion. `wa-waffiqna wa jami' mu'minina li-ta'at hudud al-nizam` (and grant us and all believers success in obeying the established hudud). And grant us success, and all believers, to obey... the *hudud* (ranks). The established *hudud*. `wa hasbuna Allahu wa ni'm al-wakil` (and Allah is sufficient for us, and He is the best of disposers). `wa ila dhalika... `adat al-mas'alatayn` (and to that... returned the two questions). And these... when we came to the conclusion, of this book, so two questions reached us. Of whom? `al-sundu` (the brother)... Ibrahim bin 'Ali. Ibrahim bin 'Ali, who he is, I don't know. Ibrahim bin 'Ali's... and... may Allah expand him... that... their answers... from the author of this treatise... `Shams al-Huda wa Badr al-Dhuja` (Sun of Guidance and Full Moon of the Night). That... the one who wrote this treatise, `Shams al-Huda wa Badr al-Dhuja`. `insan 'ayn abihi` (the pupil of his father's eye). Who... of his father... meaning Sayyidna Hibatullah sahib's... were the pupil of his eye. Meaning, they were the pupil of his eye. `al-Wahhab al-Mannan` (the Bestower, the Benefactor)... their... they are talking about their greatness. `al-Wahhab al-Mannan`. Sayyidna... `Sayyidna wa Mawlana al-Da'i al-Awwab` (our master and our Mawla, the ever-returning Da'i). Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sayyidna Hibatullah. Who... and this... the *Da'i* is Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hibatullah, `qaddas Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu` (may Allah sanctify his soul and illuminate his tomb). `Wa huma...` (And they are...) Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. And those two questions are... what is he saying? That... Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim. `Alhamdulillah... bi-ni'matihi tatimmu al-salihat` (Praise be to Allah... by His grace, righteous deeds are completed). That all praise is for Allah, due to whom... his... blessings... are completed. `juriat fihi maulana wa malikina...` (Our Mawla and our Malik was made our ransom...). `shams al-din Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sayyidna Da'i Hibatullah...` `...fihi mughabat min farishtin` (with him are successive companies of angels)... through those angels. `min amrihi min bayni yadayhi wa min khalfihi` (by His command, from before him and from behind him). That... they remain before you and they remain behind you. The `maqluq al-raqib` (the appointed guardian)... he has asked a question, and the `al-qinn al-saghir` (the little slave), and who... is a little slave... `taqat nafsuhu` (his soul longed). That his soul longed for this matter, and... he was ready for the discussion/search. About these matters. And he was ready for the discussion/search, meaning for knowing. About these two questions... which are very noble questions. `wa qad qala Allahu... fas'alu ahl al-dhikr in kuntum la ta'lamun` (and Allah has said... so ask the people of remembrance if you do not know). And Allah Ta'ala has ordained, "Ask the people of remembrance if you do not know." `wa qala...` One time... it so happened that... the lesson was being given. Because the method was this, that after the passing of Rasulullah, and in the lifetime of Mawla... Mawla-e Kainaat also... after the passing of Rasulullah, Imam Hasan and Imam Husayn... in the *masjid* of the Rasul, after the prayer, would sit and give a lesson. And a circle would gather around them, of all the people. So at that time, this... this verse, you read: `fas'alu ahl al-dhikr in kuntum la ta'lamun` (so ask the people of remembrance if you do not know). Ask the *Ahl al-Dhikr* (people of remembrance) if you do not know. So you said, "Look, this... this verse is a very eloquent verse of the Qur'an al-Majid." That... "so ask the people of remembrance." "You... ask." So here, two things have been said. And you have been commanded by Allah Ta'ala to ask. That "you... ask... the people of remembrance." So the *Ahl al-Dhikr* are separate, and the ones asking are separate. So you... the entire Ummah of Islam's... circle is of asking. It is of the asker. "And we are the *Ahl al-Dhikr*." "So we have been given the command to be asked a question." And... it is said, "`in kuntum la ta'lamun` (if you do not know)." "While you do not know." "So you are in the circle of `la ta'lamun`." "That you do not know." "And you will know when you ask a question of the *Ahl al-Dhikr*." Yes. So the *Ahl al-Dhikr* are us. "So we have been given the command to be asked a question." And it has also been said that "we... if we wish, we can give you the answer, or if we wish, we may not give the answer." We have this choice. We have not been told that "we should give you the answer." Rather, you have been... mentioned to ask a question. Yes. One time it so happened that an Arab came into that assembly. And he asked permission to ask some questions. So you said, "Yes, ask what you want to ask." So he asked 100 questions. Very strange and unusual questions he asked. So those questions... those questions were very strange and unusual, that... छोटी-छोटी बातों का सवाल किया उसने [he asked questions about small things]. (The session ends here).

<!-- lecture 14 -->

*As-salamu alaykum wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh*. *Wa alaykum as-salam*. *As-salamu alaykum wa rahmatullah*. *Wa alaykum as-salam*. *As-salamu alaykum*. *Wa alaykum as-salam*.

*A'udhu billahi minash shaitanir rajim*. (I seek refuge in Allah from the accursed Satan). In the name of *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. (In the name of Allah, the Most Gracious, the Most Merciful). *Wa bihi nasta'in, sallallahu ala rasulih, Sayyidina Muhammadin wa ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim*. (And in Him we seek help. May Allah bless His Messenger, our master Muhammad, and the family of Muhammad, and grant them blessings and peace).

The first question is about *Mawlana Mehdi*, *sallallahu alayhi*. The first question is about *Mawlana Mehdi*, *sallallahu alayhi*, and *Mawlana Mehdi*—there are two *Mehdi*s. There are two *Mehdi*s. One is our 11th Imam, who is *Mehdi*, and this one is our... uh... 50th Imam. He is also a *Mehdi*. Yes.

And this 50th one is... uh... of the same kind as *Rasulullah*. He does not come within the circle of *Imamat*. So, the last... *Mehdi*... *Mehdi Akhir al-Zaman* is also not within the circle of *Imamat*. He is not an Imam. He is not the son of an Imam. Yes, he is not the son of an Imam, but he has great status (*shaan*). He is also called *Mim-e-Akhirah*. *Mim-e-Ula* is *Rasulullah*, *'ala dhikrihis salaam*. And *Mim-e-Akhirah* is *Mawlana Mehdi*, who is the 50th Imam. This is in the sense that *Rasulullah* broke the chain (*silk*) of *Imamat*. The 29th person breaks the chain of *Imamat*. So... uh... uh... similarly, this *Mawlana Mehdi* also broke the chain of *Imamat* because... uh... after the 49th Imam, the *Qa'im* was supposed to come, but he came in between.

Today's Imam is *Abul Qa'im*. So after *Abul Qa'im*, the *Qa'im* should have come, but he came in between. So... his... his... the great precedents (*sawabiqat*) and his great status (*shaan*) are the reason for this.

But the forefathers (*jadd*) of *Janab-e-Mehdi* were also Imams, right? The forefathers were Imams. Yes. Just like... just like *Rasulullah*'s were. Yes, yes, that's what I'm saying. Just like *Rasulullah*'s were. Yes. The father was not. No, the father was not.

So today's... Imam's father... brother... son of a son... nephew. Brother's... today's Imam's father's... uh... grandson, right? Just like *Muttalib... 'Abdul Muttalib*'s brother... No, uncle. Uncle's son. So, today's... the Imam who is... uncle's... nephew.

So, a question was asked about him. *Wa qala Ta'ala... ta'ruj al-mala'ikatu wa al-ruh ilayhi fi yawmin kana miqdaruhu khamsina alfa sanah*.
