(And Allah Ta'ala says, "The angels and the Spirit ascend to Him in a day, the measure of which is fifty thousand years"). As Allah Ta'ala says, that with Him, meaning with Allah, one day... its measure is what? Fifty thousand years. *Fa ma'na al-yawm 'alayhi sallallahu alayhi wa al-dawr al-'adhim, dawr al-kashf.* (The meaning of 'the day' for him, peace be upon him, is the great cycle, the cycle of unveiling). The cycle of *kashf* began with him. From you... from you, the cycle of *kashf* began. Your cycle is called the cycle of *kashf*. From you, the cycle of *kashf* began. I mean, from *Janab-e-Mehdi*. From *Mehdi*. Yes. Once, I was studying with *Sayyidna Husayn bin Isma'il*, and we have this concept that there is *Mehdi*, and then there is *Qa'im*. So he said, "*al-Mehdi huwal Qa'im, wal Qa'im huwal Mehdi*." (The Mehdi is the Qa'im, and the Qa'im is the Mehdi). Yes. So why do you people think of them as separate? That *Mehdi* is separate and *Qa'im* is separate. So your concept should be that *al-Mehdi huwal Qa'im, wal Qa'im huwal Mehdi*.

Yes, yes.

*Fa al-ma'na 'alayhi sallallahu alayhi wa al-dawr al-'adhim, dawr al-kashf, dawr... wa lahu mutimmoona min waladihi... wa huwa ya'khudh zakaa*. (The meaning for him, peace be upon him, is the great cycle, the cycle of unveiling... and for him are completers [mutimmin] from his children... and he will take the khilafah of zakaa). And in your cycle, there are completers (*mutimmin*), from your children... from your progeny. And he will take the *khilafah* of *Zakaa*. And they will take the *khilafah* of *Zakaa*. *Zakaa* means... the sun.

You see, in that too, the word *zakaa* comes. In that... wasn't there a *munajat*? That... which we recite in *Sadaqallah*. In *Sadaqallah*, the *munajat* that was there... *Mansur... Khassa 'Amir bin Sa'b*. Yes. No, of our *Mansub... Khassa 'Amir bin Sa'b*. In his *munajat*, the word *zakaa* is also there. So, *Antum al-zakaa, wa al-nasu...* (You are the sun, and the people are...). You are in the position of the sun, i.e., *Janab-e-Ali*. Yes, and the others are in the position of stars. That's what he said.

*Wa ya'khudh zakaa*. (And he will take the sun). The questioner has said it. What does *mutimmin* mean? *Mutimmin* means... *Mutimmin* means... completers. So, just as there were *mutimmin* of the *dawr* of 'Isa, those who came at the end of the *dawr* of 'Isa are called *mutimmin*. Or those who come at the beginning of a *dawr* are also called *mutimmin*.

*Wa kam takunu khidmatuhu*. (And what will be his service?). *min waladihi...* (from his children). Whose children are being referred to? *Mawlana Mehdi*'s. In *Mawlana Mehdi*'s progeny, there is a *mutim*. And they will... you, *Mawlana Mehdi*, will take the *khilafah*. This is what the questioner is asking. The *khilafah* of the sun. *Wa kam takunu khidmatuhu? Wa ya'khudh khilafat al-'ashir*. (And what will be his service? And he will take the khilafah of the 'Ashir'). And what will your service be? And you will take the *khilafah* of the *'Ashir*. *Tafaddal 'alayna ya mawda' al-sirri wa al-sara'ir*. (Bestow upon us, O repository of the secret and secrets). So please bestow your grace upon us, for you are the place of secrets and hidden matters. *Jazakallahu afdal al-jaza'*. (May Allah reward you with the best of rewards). May Allah give you the best of rewards. *Bi-haqqi Sayyidina Muhammadin wa aalihi al-tahirin*. (By the right of our master Muhammad and his pure family).

*Al-jawab: Bi-'awnillahi subhanahu wa mannihi wa yusrihi wa ta'yidi mawlana al-Malik, mahbat ash'at al-mala'ik*. (The answer: With the help of Allah, Glorified is He, and His grace and ease, and the support of our master, the King, the landing place of the rays of the angels). With the help, meaning with the support of Allah, Glorified is He, and with His grace and ease, and with the support of *Mawlana Malik*, meaning *Sayyidna Hibatullah*, who is the place where the rays of the *mala'ikah* descend. Who? *Mawlana Hibatullah saheb*. *Anna mawlana al-Mehdi al-'adhim, alladhi huwa qa'im dawr al-sitr*. (Know that our master, the great Mehdi, is the one who is the Qa'im of the cycle of concealment [dawr al-sitr]). Yes. And know this matter, that the one who is *Mawlana Mehdi*, who has great status, he is, in fact, the *Qa'im* of... yes... he is the *Qa'im* of the *dawr al-sitr*. He is the *Qa'im* of *dawr al-sitr faqih*.

*Wa lahu sha'nun 'adhim*. (And for him is a great status). And his status is very great. *Wa fadlun jasim*. (And a great virtue). The author has not said "*Jami'*" (confluence), but rather "*Qa'im*" of the *dawr al-sitr*. He is the *Qa'im* of the *dawr al-sitr*. *Wa huwa majma' thaman suwar... suwar 'idham samiyah*. (And he is the confluence of eight surahs... great, lofty surahs). And you are the confluence of eight surahs that are such surahs, very lofty surahs. Because of the saying of Allah Ta'ala, *Wa yahmilu 'arsha rabbika fawqahum yawma'idhin thamaniyah*. (And on that day, eight will carry the Throne of your Lord above them). That on that day... on the day of resurrection... those who will be carrying the throne of their Lord will be eight angels. *Wa sab'u suwarin fi dawr al-kashf al-madi li-hadha al-shakhs*. (And seven surahs are in the past cycle of unveiling for this person). And seven surahs are those which are in the past, meaning the past *dawr al-kashf* which has passed. Meaning a *surah* that came every 7,000 years. That. *Alladhi qala fihi Sayyiduna 'Ali ibn Hanzala, 'alla Allahu qudsahu...* (*Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hanzala* said about him, may Allah sanctify his soul...) In regard to which *Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hanzala* has said... may Allah the Most High keep you in the highest station, in the chambers of paradise... that... what is the word? *Wa hiya yas'adu*. *Wa hiya yas'adu... fi asna'ihi min suwar...*. (And it ascends... in its midst from the surahs...). Before that too there is something, no? No. *Wa hiya yas'adu fi asna'ihi min suwar...*. (And it ascends in its midst from the surahs...). You ascend from the surahs... I don't know what it is. *Sab'un min al-qism alladhi...* (Seven from the portion which...). That *surah... 'azimah fi thalathat alaf fi dawr al-fatrah*. (great... in three thousand [years] in the period of interruption [dawr al-fatrah]). And the three surahs, meaning the one that came every 1,000 years, one *surah*. So, three surahs. *Wa hadha dawr al-sitr al-mutaqallibina fihi ahl al-sharra'*. (And this is the cycle of concealment, in which the people of evil have prevailed). And our cycle is the *dawr al-sitr*, where evil people have dominance. *Wa yawmuhu huwa dawr al-asna'*. (And his day is the lofty cycle). And your day is your cycle, which is lofty. *Wa al-imamah fihi mustamirrah*. (And the Imamat in it is continuous). And in it, the *Imamat* will continue. Just as it did in the *dawr al-sitr*. *Min dhurriyyatihi, alladhina hum ma'ani al-asma' al-husna*. (From his progeny, who are the meanings of the Most Beautiful Names). From your progeny. Those who are the meanings of the *Asma' al-Husna*. *Wa dhakarta annahu ya'khudh zakaa, fa-laysa dhalika kadhalik. Bal alladhi ya'khudhuha ba'da intiha' al-rub'atati huwa al-rukn al-asham*. (And you mentioned that he will take the sun [zakaa], but it is not so. Rather, the one who will take it after the end of the four [rub'atat] is the lofty pillar [rukn al-asham]). And you have mentioned that he will take the *khilafah* of the sun, so it is not so. Rather, the one who will take it after the end of the *rub'atat*... after the end of the four... he is the *rukn al-asham*. Meaning, *rukn al-asad*. He will take the *khilafah*. Of what? Of the sun. Meaning, those who ascend, how do they ascend? He is explaining that. That he will take the *khilafah* of the sun. He... yes. *Fa-'rif dhalika wa ta'aqqal ma hunalika. Wa 'alayka bi-al-tasawwur al-mustaqim*. (So know that and comprehend what is there. And upon you is the straight understanding). Now you should know this matter and keep your understanding straight. *Li-takuna min sukkan jannat al-na'im*. (So that you may be among the dwellers of the Garden of Bliss). So that you become from among the dwellers of *Jannat al-Na'im*. *Wa amma al-Qa'im, 'alayhi salamu, wa tahiyyatuhu wa ikramuhu, fa-innahu 'inda firaqi min hadhihi al-dar, yakhluf al-'ashir*. (And as for the Qa'im, peace be upon him, and his greetings and honor, then indeed, upon my departure from this abode, he will succeed the 'Ashir). And as far as the *Qa'im* is concerned—may our salutations be upon him... and may greetings and honor be upon him... then indeed, upon my departure from this abode... meaning at the time of passing away... when you pass away from this abode, then you will take the *khilafah* of the *'Ashir*. Yes.

And for how... for how long will you remain, *Qa'im*... in the world? Three days. Three days. Just for three days will the *qiyam* of the *Qa'im* last. Yes.

So they are two different personalities then? Yes. So they are two different personalities then? *Qa'im* and *Mehdi Akhir al-Zaman* are two different personalities? They are two different personalities. Yes. Yes.

But the *Qa'im* of the *dawr al-sitr* has been called *Mehdi*.

And here it is said that *Mawlana Qa'im*'s... the status will certainly be greater than *Mehdi*'s. And that *Qa'im*, peace be upon his mention, will take the *khilafah* of the *'Ashir 'Aziz al-Jabbar*. The *'Ashir* who are... meaning the *'Ashir* here means... the second *juz'i da'i* of this *kawr*.

And he who is... when he progresses after one *kawr*, then he will become the *juz'i da'i* of the next *kawr*. That will be the *Qa'im*. And that too in a *juz'i* (partial) capacity. Not in a *kulli* (total) capacity. Because there are many people standing in the line. There are many people standing in the line. As it is in the Holy Qur'an, what is it? That... uh... In the beginning, right? *Yasin, Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim, Yasin, wal-Qur'an al-hakim, innaka la-mina al-mursalin, 'ala siratin mustaqim*. (Yasin. By the wise Qur'an, indeed, you are from among the messengers, on a straight path). That you are from among the messengers. Right? And you are on the straight path. So, one doesn't understand that Allah gave you such greatness that He said you are from among the messengers, and then to say that you are on the straight path? It seems like a strange thing. For instance, if someone is introduced and it is said that he has a PhD and is a professor at such-and-such place, and he has degrees from eight or ten universities, and he knows English, and he has also passed his matriculation, then people will say, "Hey, what kind of thing are you saying?" So, this same thing has happened here too. That you are from among the messengers, you are one of the messengers, and you are on the straight path. You are in the line. So what does that mean? That *'ala siratin mustaqim* means that line, the line of *juz's*. In that line of *juz's*, the people who are standing... among them... you are also there. Yes. And... uh... *'ala siratin mustaqim*... in that, who is before you? The *Thalathat Kubara'* (The Three Great Ones) are there. The *Thalathat Kubara'* are before you. And after them, it is you. Yes.

So, the *Qa'im* of the *dawr*... so before this, the four hundred *dawrs* that have passed, this *kawr*... Yes, yes. In that, everyone will be in the line. Yes, absolutely.

And then this too: *Innaka 'ala siratin mustaqim* (Indeed, you are on a straight path). This was said for you, and then what did Allah Ta'ala say? Uh... *Ana 'ala siratin mustaqim*. (I am on a straight path). There is a verse in the Qur'an. "I am on the straight path." This is also a strange thing. And the Prophet, peace be upon him, said, *Inna rabbi 'ala siratin mustaqim*. (Indeed, my Lord is on a straight path). This is a hadith of the Prophet. That "My Lord is on the straight path." Meaning... Abu Talib is being talked about. That my Lord... the one who raised me... he is on the straight path. So from this, *sirat mustaqim* means this path of *juz's*. It is the straight path. But the *A'immah Tahirin* (Pure Imams)... or the *Rasulullah* of the *da'is*, their *sirat mustaqim* is different. And when we say, *Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim* (Guide us to the straight path), O Allah, keep us firm on the straight path and make us walk on it. Meaning, don't just make us stand. Don't just make us stand on the straight path, but also make us walk on the straight path. Yes. As *Haddrat al-Muqaddas* once explained, that *Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim* does not mean to just go and make us stand on the straight path. Rather, make us walk on that straight path. Yes.

So, when we say *Ihdina al-sirat al-mustaqim*, this is a very magnificent prayer. And... that He should make us walk on the straight path, meaning... the chain that is going... from a *mu'min* to the Imam... in that... in that chain, there are the lower ranks (*hudud daniyah*) and also the higher ranks (*hudud 'aliyah*). Make us walk on that path, so that we may quickly reach the *Bab al-Abwab*. This is our *sirat mustaqim*. For a *mu'min*... the *surah* of a *mu'min*... reaching the *Bab al-Abwab* is its destination. That when... it reaches that station of *Bab al-Abwab*, and in that assembly... the assembly of *Bab al-Abwab*, which is a magnificent assembly... it is *fasl al-khitab* (the decisive speech). So... that... that is... and when the Imam obtains that assembly... and gives it to the *Farzand-e-Imam* (son of the Imam), then it becomes like a fixed deposit. Now, it will progress... wherever the *Farzand-e-Imam* goes, it will go with them. Yes. So it is said that there are two types of spiritual progress (*taraqqiyat*). One is that a person strives and progresses on their own, and the second is that one progresses by sitting in someone's lap. One progresses by sitting in someone's lap. So that is also a form of progress. Yes.

The duration is not fixed? How long...? To reach the *Bab al-Abwab*. No, that depends on each person's capacity. As the person is, so will be their progress. So, his... As the *taqwa* (piety) and *taharat* (purity) are... so will be his... but this is so that the *sadaqat* (charities) and good deeds that are done after the passing of a *mu'min*, and his children do... recite for him... do good deeds, then their benefits also reach him. And he progresses faster. At whatever stage he is. Yes.

A human's... a human's *surah*... it is divided among the Imams. No, all the units gather in one place. Yes, they gather in one place. Because the one who... the previous Imam... Yes. And he doesn't come into the world, right? A *surah sharif* that is progressing, it progresses. Whatever comes into the world, as a person passes away, after a thousand years, he comes back in the same way, but it is minus the vegetative soul (*namiyah*), minus the sensory soul (*hissiyah*), minus the rational soul (*natiqah*). And sometimes, it even rises above his *moththalan ikhtira'i* (archetypal form). But... its progress is very slow. It keeps progressing. And in one *kawr*, its... its... its manifestations (*zuhurat*) happen 360 times. Yes. 360 times. 360 times. Yes. Of the *walad*. Of the *walad*, the integration. Yes. Therefore, *Sayyidna Ali bin Sulayman* said that in the human body, there are 360 *nufus* (souls/parts). So this means that one of his *nafs* progresses in one *kawr*. In one... in one *dawr*. When his integration through grace happens, then one of his... one part, out of 360, progresses.

*Bhaijaan*, so... Yes? *Mawlana Ja'far al-Sadiq*'s *du'as* (prayers), which are to be recited daily... Yes. In them too, there is such a verse... in them too, there is such a phrase: *Innaka 'ala siratin mustaqim*. (Indeed, you are on a straight path). In the 27th *tariqah*. Its meaning is addressed to Allah, right? That, "O Allah, you are on the straight path." Yes, yes. That's the same answer. Yes, yes. "Allah" there does not mean *Mubdi' Ta'ala* (the Originator, the Exalted), but the *juz'i da'i* (partial missionary) of that *kawr*. The indication is towards him, that you too are on the *sirat mustaqim*, meaning you are in the line to take the *khilafah* of the *'Ashir*. Yes, yes. That's what it means.

Because everyone has to progress. Yes. *Allati ma yuhitu biha illa man safat suratuhu min al-rayb*. (Which cannot be encompassed except by one whose form has been purified from doubt). And these things can only be understood by one... and one can encompass them... whose *surah* has been completely purified from doubts and suspicions. Yes. *Wa tawalla amra 'alam al-tabi'ah*. (And he took charge of the affair of the world of nature). And they become masters of the affairs of the natural world. *Wa yadharu fi afwaniha surur al-badi'ah... Fa-hafiz hadha al-jawab al-mukhtasar wa sunhu fa-innahu min al-sirr al-akbar*. (And in its horizons appear wondrous joys... So preserve this concise answer and guard it, for it is from the greatest of secrets). So you should know this concise answer and guard it, for it is from the lofty secrets.

Let's stop here for today, as the need has been met.

*Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Qul huwallahu ahad...* *Fatihah*. A gift, a connection, a praise, a pure and growing one... to the soul of *Sayyidna Ali bin Hibatullah*... and for his... for us... from his blessings, his support, and his aid... *Sayyid...* May Allah accept it from us... and recite it in the best way possible... for the sake of... *salam... Fatihah*. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Al-hamdu lillahi rabbil 'alamin...*

And also for *Sayyidna Mawlana...* the departed, *Janab-e... Sayyidna... al-Hajj... Shaykh... Janab... Mawlana Ghulam Husayn... Haddrat al-Muqaddas*... and *Mawlana...* and for all... so that Allah may elevate their ranks and forgive their sins... and gather us in their company... and in the company of *Ahl al-Bayt...* and our departed... and for the fulfillment of our needs... and for our children and their progress... and for the *khilafah*... and for the Imam of the time... and for our health... and for the fulfillment of our needs... and for *Sayyidna Mawlana...* and for all... *Fatihah*. *Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim*. *Al-hamdu lillahi rabbil 'alamin...*

<!-- lecture 15 -->

...wa salli Allahu 'ala rasulihi sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'ala aali Muhammadin wa barik wa sallim.

This... before showing the camera... this is *al-su'al al-thani* (the second question). Yes. `Al-su'al al-thani 'an qawlihi ta'ala: Yawnma natwi al-sama'a katayyi al-sijilli lil-kitabi. Wa hal dhalika lahu haddun ma'roof am huwa ma yakunu dhalika illa min ba'di an tafattatat al-jibalu wal-hijara...` (The second question about His, the Exalted's, saying: "The Day when We will roll up the heaven like a scroll rolled up for books." Is there a known limit for that, or will it not happen until after the mountains and stones have crumbled...) and... yes. And the second question... that is, they had asked two questions, right? It was... who had asked them? Ibrahim had.

Ibrahim bin... yes, this book was, so to speak, finished, but then, for this reason, two... one person had asked two questions.

So...

His name was Ibrahim.

Ibrahim bin 'Ali. Ibrahim bin 'Ali asked two questions, so he wrote those questions at the end of the book.

So, we read one question yesterday. This is the second, final question: that their saying... that his saying... that on that day, `natwi al-sama'` (We will roll up the heaven). Allah Ta'ala says, it is an ayat of the Qur'an Majeed, that "We will roll up the heaven as a scroll is rolled up, for the book..."
...of the book.
Yes. The book is... and the pages of the book are... in that way We will roll up the heaven.
`Wa hal dhalika haddun ma'roof...`
It is `lil-kutub` (for the books). There is a mistake in writing.
It is `lil-kutub`.
It should be `lil-kitab`.
I have checked. Let's see. It is `kutub`. `Kutub`.
The ayat is `kutub`.
It is an ayat of the Qur'an.
Yes. `lil-kutub`. `lil-kutub`. The books... just as they are rolled up. `Wa hal dhalika lahu haddun ma'roof?` (Is there a known limit for that?) Is there a known limit for it, meaning a period, until when will this matter happen? `Am huwa ma yakunu dhalika illa min ba'di an tafattatat al-jibalu wal-hijara` (Or will it not happen until after the mountains and stones have crumbled?). And this... when the mountains and stones break apart... `wa ya'udu ila al-ma'adin` (and it returns to the minerals/mines), and this... these heavens will enter into the `ma'dan` (mine/source). That is, for it to ascend... will it have to become a `ma'dan` and ascend? Then, `ila al-nabat` (to plant life), then it will enter into plant life and have to move forward. `Wa hal am jara hatta ta'nusu min al-asri` (And has it been that... until it is freed from captivity?), that is, the matter will continue until the captivity it is in, it is freed from that.

The questioner asked a question. This should be considered the question, and our answer should be the answer. `Wa taraqqi al-ruwahat lil-aflak` (and the ascension of spirits to the celestial spheres). And the bonds of the spheres, when they break, then `hal... ya Maulai, ju'iltu fidak...` (so... O my master, may I be your ransom...), so my master, make me your ransom. `...ya'qabu dawr al-kashf al-'azim dawr al-sitr` (will the great era of unveiling follow the era of concealment?). That is, will the era of *kashf* (unveiling) come after the era of *sitr* (concealment)? `mithl hadha al-dawr` (like this era), as happened in this era in which we are, `am ya ta'aqub al-adwar abad al-abad` (or will the succession of eras continue forever and ever?), or the eras that are, will they always follow one after the other? `Wa huwa alladhi ya'lamu kha'inata al-a'yun` (And He is the One who knows the treachery of the eyes)... and He who knows the signs of the eyes, meaning Allah Ta'ala, `wa ma tukhfi al-sudur` (and what the breasts conceal), and what is hidden in your hearts, `wa ila Allahi tasir al-umur` (and to Allah return all affairs), and to Allah all matters return. `Wa salla Allahu 'ala sayyidina Muhammadin wa 'alih wa sallam ajma'in.`
This is... `ya'lamu kha'inata al-a'yun` is a Qur'anic ayat.
Yes.
It knows the secrets of the hearts. Yes.
...the end.

`Al-jawab`. With the help of Allah Subhanahu wa mannatih, wa lillah ta'idih, Maulai al-Malik, mahbat ash'at al-mala'ik.
`An qawlihi ta'ala...`
That is, with the help and grace of Allah Ta'ala, and with His support, Maulai al-Malik, who is the landing place for the rays of the angels, meaning Sayyidna Hibatullah Sahab. `An qawlihi ta'ala` (Regarding His, the Exalted's, saying): `yawnma natwi al-sama'a katayyi al-sijilli` (the Day when We will roll up the heaven like a scroll). That your... Allah Ta'ala's saying that on that day when we will roll up the heaven in this way, just as books are rolled up, `fa dhalika yakunu 'inda intiha'i al-ribatat` (so that will be at the end of the bonds/connections). So when will that happen? When the bonds break.

In other words, at the end of the *kawr* (minor cycle). `Bila shakk wa la rayb wa la irtiyab` (Without doubt, skepticism, or uncertainty). Without any doubt and without any skepticism, it will happen like this. `Yawma tubaddalu al-ardu ghayra al-ardi` (The Day the earth is changed to other than the earth). And the earth... one earth will be changed to another earth. `Wa yukhluqu al-tul 'ala al-'ard` (And length will be created upon width). And the length that will be, it will be changed into the width. `Wa yakunu al-shimal junub` (And the north will become south). And the north will become south. `Wa dhalika ma taqtadihi 'inayat 'allam al-ghuyub` (And that is what the providence of the All-Knower of the Unseen necessitates). And as the All-Knower of the Unseen decides, that decision will be made. `Yawma tamuru al-ardu mawra` (The Day the earth will sway violently), when there will be a *mawr* (great cycle). `Wa tasiru al-jibalu sayra` (And the mountains will move away). And when the mountains will begin to travel, meaning the mountains will move from their places and flee. `Kama qala hadrat al-kha'irin kama bada'na awwala khalqin nu'iduhu` (As the presence of the best of people said, "Just as We began the first creation, We will repeat it."). Just as Hadrat al-Kha'irin, meaning Allah Ta'ala, says, just as We began, the first creation, as the first creation happened, in that same way the creation will happen again, after that. `Wa'dan 'alayna` (A promise upon Us). This is our promise, we will surely do it. Meaning, after one *kawr*, another *kawr* will come. When the creations that happen from the wombs, the bodies that are formed, they are formed only at the beginning of the *kawr*. So after one *kawr*, another *kawr* will surely come. `Wa qawluka hal ya'qabu dawr al-kashf dawr al-sitr` (And your saying, "Will the era of unveiling follow the era of concealment?"). That your saying is that after the *dawr al-kashf*, the *dawr al-sitr* will come? `Mithl hadha alladhi nahnu fihi` (Like this one in which we are), just as... just as we are in this *kawr*, in this *sitr*, after that *kashf* will come, will it be like that? `Fa na'am` (So yes). So he says, "Yes, it is like that." `Yata'aqubu dawr al-sitr wal-kashf` (The era of concealment and unveiling will succeed each other). The *dawr al-sitr* will come, and after that, the *dawr al-kashf* will come. `Ka ta'aqub al-layli wa al-nahar` (Like the succession of night and day), just as night is followed by day. `Bila tahwil wa la tabdil` (Without change or alteration). Without any change or alteration. `Ya jari...` it will continue. `Ila an yatimmu al-mawr al-a'zam` (Until the Great Cycle is completed). Until... the cycle will continue... until the *Mawr al-A'zam* (Greatest Cycle) is completed. That is, inside one *mawr*, there are 400,000 *kawr*. 400,000 *kawr* times 400,000 *kawr*. What a great period it is.

And one *mawr* has passed upon the world. And nine *mawr* remain.

And the *hukm* (rule)...
It was asked that this is a very long period, nine *mawr*. How will... so the Dai who was giving the answer said, "Just as one *mawr* passed over you and you didn't even realize it, in the same way, nine more *mawr* will pass." And how did it happen? After one *mawr* passes, the *'ashir* (Tenth Intellect) who is there, the *taflu* (offspring) of his *'ashir*, he will give a place to the ninth *'aql* (intellect). And then in this way, the place of the intellect will keep changing. Meaning, the *tadbirat* (dispensations/plans) will keep changing. But all those climbing forms, the forms of the *mawr*, and the *'ashir* who will change his place, so he will change and where will they go? To the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal* (First Emitted Being). Because the *'Aql al-Awwal*'s (First Intellect's) domain is the domain of *wahdat* (unity). Meaning, there is no partner in it. It is one. It has no companion. And whatever matter happens, just as they gather at the *Bab al-Abwab* (Gate of Gates), in the same way, all their gatherings, of the climbing forms, which will climb as intellects... the climbing forms will climb as intellects, and all the forms, because with one form, countless other forms will also be climbing... with the intellect. All of them will gather in the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. And only one domain will be... in the end, the domain of the *'Aql al-Awwal* will be there, and the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal* will remain.
So, all will be absorbed into the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*?
They will be absorbed.
And no other being will remain after that?
No.
Meaning, will this happen in the final, tenth *mawr*, or...
...at the very end.
After the ninth *mawr*.
Yes. That is, this process is ongoing. But when the final *mawr* happens, then in the final *mawr*...
You cannot... [unclear]
[Phone ringing]
...it will be cut off.
Actually, he is not...
What is changing?
...what to do?
No, he is not getting the audio, that's why he is saying that.
He told me to cut and then...
No, no, don't cut.
No, then... how will they cut it?
He is not hearing the audio. I am trying to say, let's cut him and then rejoin him.
Is the audio reaching you all?
The audio is reaching?
Yes, it is reaching, it is reaching.
Yes, it is reaching.
Yes.

Yes, it is reaching.

What is the difference between *Jannat al-Ma'wa* and *Sidrat al-Muntaha*?
*Sidrat al-Muntaha* is higher than *Jannat al-Ma'wa*. Yes. And in one sense, *Jannat al-Ma'wa* is at the level of the *bab al-zahir* (the manifest gate), and *Sidrat al-Muntaha* is at the level of the *bab al-batin* (the esoteric gate).
This formula you mentioned, 400,000 times 400,000... So, meaning, 400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr*... So, if 400,000 *kawr* happen, that's one *mawr*?
Yes.
That's what I... meaning...
There is no multiplication, meaning 400,000 *kawr* make one *mawr*.
No, the way I read it was 400,000 *kawr* times 400,000 *kawr* is one *mawr*.
Yes.
That's 16 lakh (1.6 million) *kawr*.
Yes, yes.
The total is... less than that. 50,000 years is one *dawr*.
The *dawr* will also all change.
Yes. See, this is a matter of multiplication. One *dawr*... one *dawr*... one *kawr* is 400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr*. It is like that.
One *kawr*...
It is not that 400,000 *dawr* make one *kawr*.
Yes.
400,000 *dawr* times 400,000 *dawr*.
This is one *kawr*.
Just as it starts with 400,000 *dawr* for one *dawr*, then 400,000 into 400,000 is one *kawr*, then into 400,000 *kawr* is one *mawr*. Yes, yes.
It's very large.
It's a long duration.

By nature, God did not give humans the knowledge of their past lives. He didn't give it. I mean, that is a distant matter, we... we have come from the *dawr al-kashf*, but we have no knowledge of the *dawr al-kashf*... for those in the *dawr al-sitr*. So Allah Ta'ala removes everything from our minds. He makes things forgotten. And how many things... the progress that has happened, in the world, all that progress started from a completely new beginning.
The unveiling is done by the A'immah. Yes, yes.

And the *hukm* (rule/judgment) on that day will be for Allah. And He will pass judgment and decree. And `wa qad dharaba dhalika Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu` (And Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad, may Allah sanctify his soul and illuminate his shrine, gave this example). And Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad... has said this, `qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu`, that `anna dawr al-kashf wa al-sitr yatakarraru fi hadha al-kawr al-a'zam` (that the era of unveiling and concealment repeats in this great minor cycle), `al-mashhur` (the well-known), that the *dawr al-kashf* and the *dawr al-sitr* are repeated in this great *kawr*, meaning the great *kawr* in which we exist, `'inda...`
... `wahid wa 'ishruna lakh marra` (twenty-one lakh times, i.e., 2.1 million times). `Lakh marra` (lakh times). So 2,100,000 times, along with its fractions, 2,100,000 times... one... 2,100,000 *kawr*... 2,100,000... 2,100,000 times this... in one great *kawr*, this has been repeated, the *dawr al-sitr* and the *dawr al-kashf*.
`Lakan`...
Arabic word.
No, not `lakan`, `lakh`. `Lakh`.
Is `lakh` an Arabic word?
*Lakh*.
Yes, for `lakh`.
Yes.
Oh.
I thought it was Hindustani.
I also thought it was `lakan`.
No, he has written it in transliteration. `Lakh`.
`Fa anzala`... `fa atruku`... so he is saying that this... what has happened so far... 2.1 million times. Yes. 2,100,000 *kawr*.
2,100,000. In these... 2,100,000... `dawr al-kashf` and `dawr al-sitr` have happened. In this *kawr*.
In one single *kawr*, so many have happened.
Yes, yes.
This means, so far.
So far.
The one *mawr* that has passed, this is the calculation for one *mawr* تقريبا (approximately).
No, the one *mawr* has passed, but in the second *mawr*'s *kawr* in which we are sitting... its... in the second *mawr*'s *kawr* in which we are, in that *kawr*, 2,100,000 times the *dawr al-kashf* and the *dawr al-sitr* have passed. This is what it means.
`Fa atruku al-ghaflata wal-nisyana` (So leave heedlessness and forgetfulness). `Wa Allahu yuwaffiquna wa iyyakum` (And may Allah grant us and you success). So may Allah give us and you the *tawfiq* (divine success) `lima yurid al-rahim al-rahman` (for what the Merciful, the Compassionate desires), that which would please His Merciful and Compassionate, make Him content. `Bihaqqi safwat Mudar wa 'Adnan` (By the right of the elite of Mudar and 'Adnan). By the right of those who are the essences of Mudar and 'Adnan. Yes, meaning Rasulullah. The essences that are there, your ancestors Mudar who were Imams, and 'Adnan, who were Imams, by their essences, `alayhim al-salam`. Their essences, you are... that from their right... we will do such deeds that our Lord, the Merciful and Compassionate, becomes pleased. `Alayhim al-salawatu wal-salam` (Upon them be blessings and peace). And upon them be Allah's blessings and peace. `Ma ta'aqaba al-malawan` (As long as the two succeed each other). What does this mean? `Al-malawan` means...
The night and day.
Yes. `Al-malawan` is what they call the night and day.
`Al-layl wa al-nahar`.
They call it `al-malawan`.
`Malawan`.

This *'ashir* who is... will ascend one level, and then after one *mawr*... so in ten *mawr*, the *'ashir* will ascend ten steps, so... he will reach the first rank.
Yes, if we count ten...
He will reach the first station.
The matter is that he is not going to the second station. Into the domain of the *'Aql al-Awwal*. Rather, he is being absorbed into the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. Yes. All those who ascend will come into the domain of the *Mumba'ith al-Awwal*. Just as in our time, all the believers' forms that are ascending, whether they are of a lower or higher rank, where are they going? They are going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. They are going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. We spend our whole lives doing *nazar-maqam*, taking the Imam of the Time's name, every moment, right? "O Imam, help us," etc. That is firm in its place, the belief is firm. But our noble form (`surat-i sharif`), where is it going? It is going to the *Bab al-Abwab*. And going through the *bab al-zahir* (the manifest gate), it reaches the *bab al-batin* (the esoteric gate). And this is why it has been said, `la hawla wa la quwwata illa billah al-'ali al-'azim` (There is no power and no strength except in Allah, the Most High, the Most Great). Neither the *bab al-zahir* has that much power, nor the *bab al-batin* has that much power, that they could form the *haykal* (spiritual body). They cannot form the *haykal*. But the one who forms it is Allah, meaning the Imam of the Time, who can form the *haykal*. He will give his *fard* (a high-ranking dignitary) that collection of *suwarat* (forms), the great and magnificent collection. He will take it from the *Bab al-Abwab* and give it to his *fard*. And he, the *Bab al-Abwab*, will pass away. As soon as the *haykal* is "dropped" (`suqut kar di jayegi`), he passes away after three days, the *Bab al-Abwab*. And simultaneously... and the reward that the *Bab al-Abwab* is given for his services is that he becomes the *qalb* (heart) of the *fard*. The *qalb* of the *fard*. And in the same way, the service that Maulai al-Ka'inat (Sayyidna 'Ali) performed, from ancient times, the *suwarat al-anbiyya'* (forms of the prophets) that were coming, the *hayakil* (spiritual bodies) from Maulana Hunaid onwards, from Maulana Hunaid to Maulana Hashim, were coming. Because the amalgamation of the *thalath suwar* (three forms) did not happen with him. And from there, from Janab Hasan to Abul-Qasim, to today's Imam, the one who gathered all their *hayakil* is Sayyidna 'Ali ibn Abi Talib, alayhissalam.
He gathered them for the Qa'im. Yes, and he gathered them for the Qa'im. Right? So, the collection that gathered with him, he will entrust to the Qa'im. And and he... so to speak, he became the *Bab al-Abwab* for the Qa'im. He became the *Bab al-Abwab* for the Qa'im, and he will become the *qalb*. Yes. He will become the *qalb* of the Qa'im.

...by the hand of the *faqir* (the poor/humble one). This is the writing of the *faqir*. The *faqir* to Allah, who is *faqir* before Allah.
...'Ali bin Qutbuddin.
Yes. ...and to his *wali* (guardian), and to his *da'i* (summoner), `al-awwab` (the repentant), who... the *da'i* who is a great, venerable *da'i*, to them this... he is a *faqir*, meaning before Allah, before his *wali*, and before the *da'i*, he is a *faqir*. Who? 'Ali bin Qutbuddin. 'Ali bin Qutbuddin is a great elder from our community. I will tell you.
Buried in Baroda.
He is buried in Baroda. Who... under the neem tree, he is buried. 'Ali bin Qutbuddin. `Waffaqahu Allahu ridahu` (May Allah grant him success in His pleasure). May Allah grant his pleasure... may Allah grant him the *tawfiq* (divine success) that he remains content. `Wa kan al-faraagh` (And the completion was...). This was completed, the book, `fi Hisn Saran` (in the fortress of Saran). He completed it while sitting in Hisn Saran. `Fi wadi Najran` (In the valley of Najran). Yes, what is Hisn Saran? In Wadi Najran, the places that are there, there is a mountain which is called Hisn Saran. So there is a fortress there which is called `An`. So there, in 1166, when you went for the Hijrah, you transcribed this. `Yawm al-khamis` (on Thursday), the day of Thursday. `tis'a wa 'ishrun` (the twenty-ninth) of Rajab... `min man waqafa 'alayha` (from one who came upon it). And whoever comes across this book, `fa huwa mas'ul min katibihi` (so he is asked by its scribe)... that the one who has written the book, meaning... your... 'Ali bin Qutbuddin, who is writing, he is asking, that the reader should forgive him, show mercy, and ask for his forgiveness in this world and the hereafter. And while he is a very great and magnificent elder, 'Ali bin Qutbuddin. And 'Ali bin Qutbuddin has written books too. And he has a book, Sayyidna Isma'il bin... your brother, Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hibatullah's... Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah, you were there in his time. And in that time, you... on Sayyidna Isma'il bin Hibatullah... on his life events... on his *sirat* (biography), you wrote a book in the style of *haqa'iq* (esoteric realities). Which is the first book of the Da'wah, such a *sirat* which is written in *haqa'iq*. A *sirat* is a *sirat*. A *sirat* contains events. But you wrote it in the style of *haqa'iq*. And it is a magnificent book. `Al-Bahirat al-Adillah`. And it... what is it called there? In Najran, people say that this is not a book, it is *halwa* (a sweet dish). Just as there is pleasure in eating *halwa*, similarly, there is pleasure in reading this book. And I remember that Sayyidna Husayn bin Hasan `qaddasa Allahu ruhahu` asked me several times, "Ghulam, where is 'Ali bin Qutbuddin buried in Hind?" And tell me, did he write the book `Al-Bahirat al-Adillah`? So I said, "Yes, I haven't read it, Malik, but I have heard that he wrote this book." He said, "What a magnificent person he was."
Was he a *mansub* (a designated rank holder)? No. He was not a *mansub*.
He was from the time... the first *mansub* in Baroda, Karamullah Sahab, he was from his time.
You used to go there? The...
Yes, yes.
That is the same era.
Yes.
Meaning, what happened was that... the people of Baroda... at that time... in Baroda, what happened was that the Sunnis... the Sunnis who are of the Sadikpuri sect, so they... complained to the king. At that time, there was a Hindu king. So they complained to the king that these people, the Bohra people, and the 'Alawis, their beliefs are not correct. And these are all misguided people. And so the king summoned... he summoned the Bohras. He summoned the Bohras. So the Bohras, what did they do? "We, on our behalf, will give the answer. On our behalf, 'Ali bin Qutbuddin will give the answer." The Sulaymanis. So, and the 'Alawis also said that on our behalf, 'Ali bin Qutbuddin will give the answer. And that... the Sunni people who were there, they asked him several questions. They asked several questions, and you answered them from their own books. And you told them the page number and gave the answer. "In your book, on this page, this is the answer." "On this page, this is the answer." So he was astonished, the king, and he acquitted them with great honor. And... and this... he was a magnificent elder from our community. Then you said, "I will ask you three questions. You give the answer. And I know that you will not be able to answer them." He also said this. And you asked three questions. So I tried very hard to find out where those questions are and where your... what was asked of you, but I couldn't find it. But you had asked three questions.
You are the one who wrote the long *qasidah* of 400 verses, right?
Yes.
He wrote a big *qasidah*, right?
'Ali bin Qutbuddin?
No.
I don't remember.
The *qasidah* that was written, it was... his... son, Shaykh Muhammad Sahab Sindhi... who was the *da'i* of one island, right? Sayyidi Mansub Shaykh Muhammad Sahab Sindhi. So his sons... who are buried in Surat. Buried in the middle of the road. They are great elders. They have written great *qasidah*s. Yes.

This manuscript that is here, it does not have mistakes. If there are mistakes, it's because I cannot read it correctly.
Yes, yes.
It says `tammat haza`. Yes, yes.
It says `tammat haza`. `Tamma`... `tammat haza`. 1417 is written here. Yes, yes.

Our book is finished. So, alhamdulillah. `Bi lillahumma nas'aluka al-Fatiha hadiyyatan wa wasilatan saniyyatan zakiyyatan mardiyya ila ruhi Sayyidna 'Ali bin Hibatullah qaddasa Allahu ruhahu wa nawwara darihahu wa 'afaada 'alayna min barakatihi wa anfasatihi wa ta'idatihi wa taqabbal minna ya Allah al-ifadah wal-istifadah bi ahsan ma yakun bi-jahi Sayyid al-Mursalin wa siyyadi Maulana Amir al-Mu'minin wal-Qur'an al-'azim wa ahl baytihi al-tayyibin al-tahirin salawat Allah wa salamuhu 'alayhim ajma'in, al-Fatiha.`
`Bismillah...`

`Bi niyat Sayyidi Mawlaya Mansur al-kull fi kull laqab al-Zayn bin Sayyidi Mansur al-kull fi kull laqab...` [continues with a long Arabic prayer].

Our second book also... yes. The second book that we will read, it is a short, brief book, in sha Allah we will start tomorrow. Its name is `Al-Risalat al-Mawsumah bil-Mulaliyah`. There is a book by the name of `Al-Mulaliyah`, which is by Sayyidi 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. He is the great-grandson of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad... are the sons of Husayn bin Ahmad, 'Ali bin Husayn. Yes. So, their... their grandsons are buried here in Baroda. That is, this 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad is Zul-Jaddayn. Why is Husayn bin Ahmad called Zul-Jaddayn? Because... two grandfathers. So, who are the two grandfathers? Husayn bin Ahmad's grandfathers... his maternal grandfather was Sayyidna Sulayman bin Hasan, and his paternal grandfather was Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd. So they are called Zul-Jaddayn. Then they are also called Zul-'Ammayn (possessor of two paternal uncles). Who are the two uncles? One is Sayyidna Ibrahim bin Muhammad bin Fahd, he is their uncle. Of Husayn bin Ahmad. And their brother was also there, of Sayyidna Ibrahim Sahab, his name was Isma'il. So, two uncles. And Zul-Khalayn (possessor of two maternal uncles). Who were the two great maternal uncles? They were Sayyidna Ja'far bin Sulayman and Sayyidna 'Ali bin Sulayman. That is, Sayyidna Sulayman Sahab's daughter was Sitt al-Na'm. Sitt al-Na'm was married to Sayyidi Ahmad. Sayyidi Ahmad married her. And from them, a son was born, Sayyidna Husayn. And this Sayyidna Husayn's son 'Ali's written book this is.
'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad.
And Ahmad was the brother of Sayyidna Ibrahim.
The brother of Sayyidna Ibrahim. Yes. And his grandsons, his grandsons are buried in Baroda. His name is 'Ali bin Husayn bin 'Ali. This Husayn bin 'Ali is this one. And Sayyid... 'Ali bin Husayn is buried in Baroda. He has also been called 'Ali bin Husayn Zul-Jaddayn. So generally, it is thought that he is the son of this Zul-Jaddayn. 'Ali bin Husayn Zul-Jaddayn. But he is the great-grandson of Zul-Jaddayn, who is buried in Baroda.
He is in the lineage of Sayyidna Muhammad bin Fahd.
Yes, yes. It is the same lineage.
The great-grandson.
Great-great-grandson. Yes, yes. So this is their book.
'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Muhammad bin Fahd.
So, 'Ali bin Husayn bin 'Ali bin Husayn bin Ahmad bin Muhammad bin Fahd. Yes. And he was a great scholar, the one buried in Baroda. He was a very great scholar. He passed away at the age of 55.

So, in sha Allah, we will read it from tomorrow.
And you should say, send the PDF.
And its... in sha Allah, we will send you the PDF today. So you too study it, and those who can take a printout, please take a printout.

And what was I saying? That there are two systems. One is running on Google Drive, and the other is... the one running on Google Drive, so... just as you all are sitting and listening...
So that is the Google Drive one.
No, we are the Zoom ones.
The Zoom ones.
We are listening on Zoom.
Yes, you are listening on Zoom, so you are the Zoom ones. And the ones on Google Drive... so... the lesson is being sent to them, and they can listen to it later sometime. Yes.

Allah hafiz.
